### [1] [Asymmetric Entanglement-Assisted Quantum Locally Recoverable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23889)
*Vijay Kumar,Ramakrishna Bandi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出具有非对称局域性 {r_X, r_Z} 的纠缠辅助量子可局部恢复码 (EA-qLRC)，推广 CSS 构造并给出 Singleton、Griesmer、Plotkin、球填充及 Cadambe–Mazumdar 类界，刻画达到 Singleton 类界等号的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 qLRC 与 EA-qLRC 构造仅用单一局域参数 r 同时约束 X 型（比特翻转）与 Z 型（相位翻转）错误的恢复，而物理噪声在这两类错误间通常并非对称，因此单参数刻画过于保守、限制了码的参数效率，需要引入非对称局域性以更贴合实际噪声模型并拓展可行参数域。

Method: 1) 定义非对称局域性 {r_X, r_Z}：X 型与 Z 型错误分别由规模至多 r_X+1、r_Z+1 的恢复集纠正；2) 将该性质推广为稳定子充分条件，并特别推广至 CSS 框架，使非对称局域性等价于底层经典码 C_1、C_2 上各自的局域性条件（命题 CSS-EA-qLRC-Asym）；3) 基于该刻画推导 CSS 型 AEA-qLRC 的 Singleton、Griesmer、Plotkin、球填充与 Cadambe–Mazumdar 类界；4) 刻画由一对经典 LRC 构造的纯 CSS 型 AEA-qLRC 取等号（Singleton 类界）的精确条件。

Result: 建立了非对称局域 EA-qLRC 的稳定子与 CSS 充分条件；给出适用于 CSS 型 AEA-qLRC 的五类界（Singleton、Griesmer、Plotkin、球填充、Cadambe–Mazumdar 类）；得到纯 CSS 型 AEA-qLRC 达到 Singleton 类界等号的充要条件。

Conclusion: 非对称局域性为 qLRC/EA-qLRC 提供了更细粒度且更贴合非对称噪声的刻画框架，经典 LRC 对 (C_1, C_2) 与量子码参数之间的对应使参数界与最优性分析可在经典层面完成，为构造高效容错量子存储编码奠定理论基础。

Abstract: Locally recoverable codes (LRCs) permit reconstruction of a lost coordinate from a small subset of the remaining coordinates, a property that is a fundamental coding technique for distributed storage and, more recently, for fault-tolerant quantum memories and distributed storage systems. Existing constructions of quantum locally recoverable codes (qLRCs) and their entanglement-assisted generalizations (EA-qLRCs) impose a single locality parameter $r$ governing recovery from both bit-flip ($X$-type) and phase-flip ($Z$-type) errors, even though physical noise is rarely symmetric between the two. We introduce entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes (EA-qLRC) with \emph{asymmetric locality} $\{r_X,r_Z\}$, in which $X$-type and $Z$-type errors are corrected from recovery sets of size at most $r_X+1$ and $r_Z+1$, respectively. We extend a stabilizer sufficient condition for this property and, in particular, the CSS framework, where asymmetric locality corresponds to separate locality conditions on the two classical codes $\mathcal C_1$ and $\mathcal C_2$ underlying the construction. From this characterization we derive Singleton-, Griesmer-, Plotkin-, sphere-packing-, and Cadambe\textendash Mazumdar-like bounds for CSS-type asymmetric EA-qLRCs (AEA-qLRCs) built from Proposition~\ref{CSS-EA-qLRC-Asym}, and we characterize exactly when a pure CSS-type AEA-qLRC built from a pair of classical LRCs attains the Singleton-like bound with equality.

</details>


### [2] [Designing Robust Quantum Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24039)
*Yuanxing Hao,Marin Bukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文针对"量子门能否鲁棒实现"这一问题，建立了d能级系统中鲁棒门实现的存在性判据：所有门可实现并不充分，鲁棒通用门族存在的充要条件是驱动哈密顿量不共享任何公共对称性。据此可构造对静态微扰、均匀哈密顿量标度、选定频率及任意选定波形微扰均鲁棒的量子门，数值上使平均门保真度误差降低约2–7个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子控制理论只能判定给定控制通道下量子门是否可实现，却无法刻画其对误差的鲁棒性；而已有认识易误以为"所有门可实现"即足够，实际缺少鲁棒可实现性的系统性判据。

Method: 在d能级系统中建立鲁棒门实现的存在性判据，核心为驱动哈密顿量的公共对称性分析；在判据成立时施加鲁棒性条件，覆盖静态微扰、均匀哈密顿量重标度、有限选定频率的微扰及任意选定波形的微扰，并针对平台噪声（如具有主导谱峰的环境噪声，仅需峰频率信息）定制条件；同时数值构造显式鲁棒门协议。

Result: 判据表明：鲁棒通用门族存在当且仅当驱动哈密顿量无公共对称性，仅"全部门可实现"并不充分；所构造协议在哈密顿量微扰与环境诱导噪声下使平均门不保真度降低约2至7个数量级。

Conclusion: 存在性判据与数值构造共同为评估量子平台潜力、设计鲁棒量子门提供了实用指导，并表明鲁棒性条件可在误差信息有限时广泛施加，也可按平台特性定制。

Abstract: For given control channels, quantum control theory determines whether quantum gates can be realized, but not their resilience to errors. We address this problem by establishing existence criteria for robust gate implementation in $d$-level systems. Our criteria reveal that implementability of all gates is not enough: a universal family of robust gates exists if and only if the driven Hamiltonians share no common symmetry. When these criteria hold, we can make gates resilient to any combination of static perturbations, uniform Hamiltonian rescaling, perturbations at a finite set of selected frequencies, and perturbations with arbitrary selected waveforms. We can impose robustness conditions broadly with limited information about errors, e.g., against any static perturbations; we can also tailor these conditions to the platform, e.g., against environment-induced noise with dominant spectral peaks, requiring no spectral details beyond the peak frequencies. We numerically construct explicit protocols for robust gates, reducing average gate infidelity by approximately two to seven orders of magnitude under Hamiltonian perturbations and environment-induced noise. Together, our existence criteria and numerical constructions provide practical guidance for evaluating the potential of quantum platforms and designing robust quantum gates.

</details>


### [3] [Operational Meaning of the Mandelstam-Tamm Time Scale in a Spin-1/2 Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24056)
*Jakub,Rog*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Georgiev关于自由进动自旋中Mandelstam–Tamm量ΔT的批评作出回应，证明ΔT等于投影σ̂x读出下二值结果经典Fisher信息的平方根倒数，因此其在信号极值处的发散反映的是所选读出局部灵敏度的丧失，而非物理时间的无限方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Georgiev指出ΔT=ΔÂ/|d⟨Â⟩/dt|并非时间算符的方差，并进一步断言其在时钟信号极值附近的发散缺乏物理意义、无法刻画系统测量时间流逝的能力。作者认为这一更强结论需要修正。

Method: 在相同的投影σ̂x读出方案下，将ΔT与经典Fisher信息建立定量对应，并考察正则工作点与亚最大相干极值处的行为；同时引入互补的σ̂y测量进行比较分析，并区分已知的tick–tock周期与统计意义上的tick定位精度。

Result: 在正则工作点，ΔT等于单个二值结果经典Fisher信息的平方根倒数；在亚最大相干极值处ΔT发散，说明所选读出丧失局部灵敏性，而非物理时间方差无穷大；互补的σ̂y测量仍保持信息量。

Conclusion: 该解释保留了Georgiev反对时间算符解释的警告，但修正了其"MT尺度无物理内容"的更强论断：ΔT的发散体现的是读出方式的局部灵敏度而非时间的统计性质，并可与tick–tock周期的精确已知性明确区分。

Abstract: Georgiev's analysis of a freely precessing spin correctly emphasizes that the Mandelstam-Tamm quantity $ΔT=Δ\hat A/|d\langle\hat A\rangle/d t|$ is not the variance of a time operator. He concludes, however, that its divergence near the extrema of the clock signal is devoid of physical meaning and that $ΔT$ does not disclose how well the system measures the passage of time. For the same projective $\hatσ_x$ readout, we show that $ΔT$ equals the inverse square root of the classical Fisher information in one binary outcome at regular operating points. Its divergence at submaximal-coherence extrema therefore identifies a loss of local sensitivity in the selected readout, not an infinite variance of physical time; a complementary $\hatσ_y$ measurement remains informative. This interpretation also separates Georgiev's exactly known tick--tock period from the statistical precision with which a tick can be located. It preserves his warning against a time-operator interpretation while qualifying his stronger conclusion that the Mandelstam--Tamm scale lacks physical content.

</details>


### [4] [Quantum error correction well below the color code threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24077)
*Ali Lavasani,Adam P. Reed,Joan M. Dreiling,Annie J. Park,Michael Mills,Matteo Puviani,Gabriel Matos,M. S. Allman,Noah Berthusen,Natalie C. Brown,Victor E. Colussi,Brian Estey,Cameron Foltz,Daniel Gresh,Reza Haghshenas,Akhil Isanaka,Nikhil Kotibhaskar,Anthony Ransford,Ciarán Ryan-Anderson,Brian Neyenhuis,David Hayes,Tzvetan S. Metodi,Andrew C. Potter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在Quantinuum Helios离子阱处理器上演示d=3,5,7三角色码的低于阈值运行，逻辑错误率低至5×10⁻⁶，相邻码距抑制因子Λ≈12；并以色码编码的Clifford双子随机基准验证低于阈值标度（Λ≈9）；同时比较最优算法解码器与CNN/Transformer神经网络解码器，后者实时解码速度比syndrome提取速率快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错是容错的基石，但需在物理错误率低于阈值时才能实现随码距指数抑制逻辑错误。此前多数模态的结果离应用所需逻辑错误率仍有差距，因此需要在高质量离子阱平台上验证色码的低于阈值行为，并评估实用解码方案。

Method: 在Helios离子阱处理器上制备距离d=3,5,7的三角色码；采用电路层与硬件层双重泄漏（leakage）抑制方案；比较近最优算法解码器与卷积神经网络、Transformer神经网络解码器，评估其解码延迟与实时性。

Result: 逻辑错误率低至5₋₂⁺⁴×10⁻⁶，每增加一级码距实现Λ=12₋₂⁺³（超过一个数量级）的抑制；在Clifford双子随机基准电路上d=3→5呈低于阈值标度，d=5每逻辑双Clifford错误率7₋₃⁺³×10⁻⁵，Λ=9₋₃⁺⁸；神经网络解码器实时解码速率比syndrome提取速率快约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果接近megaquop量级量子计算所需的逻辑错误率，并表明可扩展的离子阱处理器有望以比其它模态已报道结果小得多的码块规模达到应用相关性能。

Abstract: Quantum error correction enables scalable fault-tolerant quantum computation by suppressing logical errors exponentially with code distance once physical error rates fall below a critical threshold. Here we demonstrate below-threshold operation of triangular color codes with distances $d=3,5,$ and $7$ on Quantinuum's Helios trapped-ion quantum processor. Leveraging both circuit- and hardware- level leakage reduction schemes, we achieve logical error rates as low as $5_{-2}^{+4}\times 10^{-6}$, and demonstrate error suppression by over an order of magnitude, $Λ=12_{-2}^{+3}$, with each successive increase in code distance. Moreover, we demonstrate below threshold scaling of error rates for Clifford two-qubit randomized benchmarking circuits when encoded in $d=3,5$ color codes, achieving logical error rate of $7^{+3}_{-3}\times 10^{-5}$ per logical two-qubit Clifford at distance $5$ with a suppression factor of $Λ=9_{-3}^{+8}$. We compare logical performance for existing near-optimal algorithmic decoders, and both convolutional neural network and transformer-based neural network decoders capable of real-time decoding an order of magnitude faster than the syndrome extraction rate. These results approach the logical error rates required for megaquop-scale quantum computation and indicate that scaled trapped-ion processors could reach application-relevant performance with substantially smaller code blocks than indicated by previous results in other qubit modalities.

</details>


### [5] [Performance of a Hot Rb Vapour-Based Portable Quantum Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24098)
*Kenneth Gregory,Konrad Socha,Khaled Mnaymneh,Connor Kupchak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文表征了一款基于铷（Rb）原子热蒸气的商用光量子存储器（QMem），在可控温度范围内测量其相干时间、绝对效率、信噪比与误码率，并按照现有光通信协议的统一口径评估其性能，以推进量子中继器中可扩展、便携、低开销存储单元的工程化集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子中继器是未来量子互联网的关键，但光纤损耗使脆弱量子态难以长距离传输；高效中继节点至少需两个量子存储单元，因此由众多节点构成的网络需要可扩展、便携、鲁棒且开销低的QMem。当前缺少对商用QMem在光通信标准口径下的系统性能评估。

Method: 以商用铷原子热蒸气QMem为对象，在一系列易控的蒸气温度条件下实验表征其相干时间、绝对效率、信噪比和误码率，并将这些指标归一化到现有光通信协议所采用的同一约定框架中。

Result: 获得了该器件在温度扫描范围内的相干时间、绝对效率、SNR和BER数据，并给出了与光通信协议一致口径下的性能表现（摘要未提供具体数值）。

Conclusion: 满足这些基准的QMem系统是将量子存储无缝集成到现有通信架构中的关键一步；以光通信标准口径评估QMem有助于其工程化应用。

Abstract: Quantum repeaters will be paramount to the success of the future quantum internet. Presently however, the losses incurred in fibre communications are unsustainable when fragile quantum states are transmitted over long distances. The most effective repeater nodes will require incorporation of a minimum of two quantum memory units (QMems) for successful operation. Therefore a network composed of numerous nodes will ideally require scalable repeaters that contain QMems that are portable, robust and do not require a high amount of overhead. Here we investigate the performance of a commercial QMem based on a warm vapour of Rb atoms. Specifically, we characterize the coherence time, absolute efficiency, signal to noise ratio and bit error ratio of this device over a range of vapour temperatures that are readily controllable. For our system, we place these performance metrics into the same conventions as those established for current optical communication protocols. Development of QMem systems meeting these benchmarks will be a vital step for seamless integration of QMems systems into present-day communication architectures.

</details>


### [6] [Translation-Invariant Pure States with Exotic Half-Chain Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24154)
*Ruizhi Liu,Jinmin Yi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造一维格点上的平移不变纯准自由费米态，使其半链冯诺依曼代数为II_∞或III_λ型因子；经JWA变换得到同型因子的纯自旋链态，并证明一类规范不变二次哈密顿量满足I型/III_1型的二分法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认知倾向于认为纯态半链代数因子局限在I型或III型，需要构造反例检验该直觉，并厘清纠缠熵标度与因子类型之间的关系。

Method: 利用平移不变纯准自由费米态与Jordan-Wigner-Araki变换构造态与无隙、超多项式衰减相互作用的唯一基态母哈密顿量；对有限味道数、指数衰减跃迁的规范不变二次哈密顿量作解析分类。

Result: 半链因子可取II_∞及任意III_λ（0<λ≤1）；同一因子类型下区间纠缠熵增长率可从任意慢发散到近幂律增长；规范不变二次哈密顿量基态呈二分法：面积律+I型，或对数增长+III_1型。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵标度不能决定半链因子的类型；纯态半链因子不必为I型或III型，但受限制的二次哈密顿量类仍呈严格二分。该反例由AI辅助发现。

Abstract: We construct translation-invariant pure quasifree fermionic states on the one-dimensional lattice whose half-chain von Neumann algebras are hyperfinite factors of type $\mathrm{II}_\infty$ or $\mathrm{III}_λ$ for any $0<λ\leq 1$. Via the Jordan-Wigner-Araki transformation, these yield translation-invariant pure spin-chain states with the same half-chain factors, demonstrating that such factors need not be of type I or III. Each state is the unique ground state of a gapless, translation-invariant parent Hamiltonian with superpolynomially decaying interactions. For any fixed factor type, the interval entanglement entropy can exhibit widely varying growth rates, from arbitrarily slow divergence to nearly power-law growth, showing that entropy scaling does not determine the factor type. In contrast, we prove that gauge-invariant quadratic Hamiltonians with finitely many flavours and exponentially decaying hopping exhibit a dichotomy: their ground states either satisfy an area law with a type I half-chain factor, or have logarithmic entropy growth with a type $\mathrm{III}_1$ factor. The counterexample was discovered with AI assistance.

</details>


### [7] [Trajectree - A quantum trajectory-based quantum circuit simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24160)
*Ansh Singal,Allen Zang,Jordan Thomas,Alexander Kolar,Anirudh Ramesh,Joaquin Chung,Rajkumar Kettimuthu,Prem Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Trajectree 是一种基于量子轨迹的量子线路模拟器，通过对线路进行“状态保存式的部分执行”来高效模拟宽而深的含噪量子线路，可复用部分已执行的轨迹生成大量蒙特卡洛样本，在标准含噪基准线路上比 Google QSim 快 5 倍以上，并可支持光量子系统（qudit）模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含噪开放量子系统的模拟通常依赖蒙特卡洛轨迹（quantum trajectory）方法，需要大量样本才能获得可靠统计结果；而宽且深的含噪线路模拟开销巨大，现有模拟器（如 QSim）在效率上存在瓶颈，缺乏对轨迹样本的高效复用机制。

Method: 提出 Trajectree 架构：采用新颖的“状态保存式部分执行”（state-saving partial execution）策略，将部分执行后的轨迹状态保存下来，从而在生成大量蒙特卡洛样本时复用这些中间轨迹而非从头重跑；同时对 Trajectree 方法进行了系统的性能分析，论证轨迹保存方案用于开放量子系统模拟的有效性；并构建光学后端以支持通信与计算相关的光量子系统模拟。

Result: 在标准含噪基准线路上，Trajectree 的模拟速度比 Google QSim（Cirq 软件包的一部分）快 5 倍以上；光学后端成功模拟了三项光学实验：CHSH Bell 测试、纠缠交换（entanglement swapping）以及光学 CNOT 门。

Conclusion: 轨迹保存式部分执行方案对开放量子系统模拟具有显著效用，Trajectree 具备高效模拟含噪 qudit 与光量子系统的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Trajectree, a quantum trajectory-based quantum circuit simulator that uses a novel, state-saving partial execution of the quantum circuits to simulate broad and deep noisy quantum circuits efficiently. Trajectree can efficiently generate a large number of Monte Carlo samples by saving partially executed trajectories. We conduct extensive performance analysis of the Trajectree approach, proving the utility of the trajectory saving scheme for simulations of open quantum systems. Using this architecture, we show that Trajectree can simulate standard benchmark noisy circuits over five times faster than Google's QSim, a part of the Cirq package. We also analyze Trajectree's performance for simulating quantum optical systems relevant to communication and computing applications. We demonstrate applications of the optical backend to simulate three optical experiments: CHSH Bell test, entanglement swapping, and optical CNOT gate. This shows Trajectree's potential for simulating noisy qudit and optical systems efficiently.

</details>


### [8] [Probing wetting and solidification dynamics of neon thin films with a high-$T_c$ superconducting circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24167)
*Brennan Dizdar,Yueheng Shi,David I. Schuster,Christopher S. Lang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用高Tc YBCO多模谐振器在氖三相点附近原位监测氖薄膜厚度，揭示液相丰富的非平衡动力学、静电调控与冻结厚度的大幅离散，并发现加氖使谐振器Q值提升两倍；结论是密封封装利于研究流体动力学与微波损耗，但不适合精确控制固氖膜厚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体氖上的电子被认为是相干性极佳的电荷量子比特，但图案化器件上氖膜的生长缺乏可控性，且固氖膜厚度对超导电路微波损耗的影响尚不明确，需要一种能原位、实时监测氖膜厚度的传感手段。

Method: 采用高临界温度YBCO超导多模谐振器作为厚度传感器：YBCO在液相存在的温度下仍保持超导，其几何结构将厚度感测窗口设在约1 μm。在氖三相点附近对流动态进行监测，并通过与感测体积的电连接（galvanic connection）实现液膜厚度的原位静电调控；同时统计不同液面高度下冻结所得固膜厚度，并测量加氖前后谐振器品质因数与介电损耗。

Method: 将中性原子物理与逻辑错误建模、应用级性能相连接：在物理层识别171Yb里德堡门中的有利噪声偏置，并通过误差选择性脉冲优化增强该偏置；在逻辑层识别内在错误抑制机制并进行表面码电路级仿真；以周期性边界Fermi-Hubbard模型为应用目标设计专用模拟电路，比较部分与全容错门合成的资源权衡。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant (FT) quantum computation offers a pathway to quantum advantage, but its realization is hindered by its significant overhead. The partially FT "STAR" architecture reduces the cost of universal computation at the price of relaxing FT requirements for non-Clifford operations, increasing exposure to hardware noise. Therefore, maximizing its performance and applicability demands careful co-design with the underlying hardware. In this work we investigate the co-design of the STAR architecture with neutral-atom platforms, connecting the underlying atomic physics to logical error modeling and application-level performance. At the physical level, we identify a favorable noise bias in $^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$ Rydberg gates, and show how it can be enhanced through error-selective pulse optimization. At the logical level, we identify an intrinsic error suppression mechanism, yielding vanishing logical coherent noise in leading order. Detailed circuit-level simulations of surface code implementations show more than an order of magnitude reduction in logical error relative to previous estimates. As a concrete target application, we focus on the Fermi-Hubbard model with periodic boundaries, for which we design a simulation circuit particularly suited to early implementations. We quantify the resource tradeoff of partially and fully FT approaches to gate synthesis, with the former showing approximately an order of magnitude reduction in state-injection depth, and an even larger reduction in Clifford resources. Comparing with classical and fully FT approaches, we identify regimes where partial fault tolerance provides both lower overheads and lower error rates. In our estimates, this is accessible with moderate error rates $p\sim 10^{-3}$ and a few thousand qubits, underlining that for these types of problems, partial FT approaches provide a compelling pathway to early quantum advantage.

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子-经典迭代精化方法求解不可压缩Navier-Stokes投影法中的压力泊松方程，将量子求解误差转化为残差的收缩因子而非误差下限，从而在噪声量子硬件上实现高精度压力求解。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次求解方案要求量子态不保真度低于10^-7，这在当前噪声量子硬件上难以达到，形成精度瓶颈。需要避免量子误差直接作为误差下限的方案。

Result: ibm_cleveland 4量子比特：20次预注册实验中全部在8-10次修正内将相对残差降至10^-7以下，而可比shots数的单次求解仅停在0.33-0.59；6量子比特试点3次均在13次修正内达标；IonQ Forte人造解基准9次修正达5.6×10^-8，压力误差4.9×10^-8；硬件上完成变分训练，7次修正残差降至5.6×10^-4（低于10^-3容差），符合噪声模型预测，耗24分钟QPU时间。

Abstract: We present a hybrid quantum-classical iterative refinement for the pressure-Poisson equation of the incompressible Navier-Stokes projection method on noisy quantum hardware. Rather than solving the pressure equation once per time step, we solve it repeatedly for the normalized residual and scale each quantum direction by a least-squares fit to the physical residual. As a result, the error of the quantum solve enters as a contraction factor $\sinθ_k$ of the residual rather than as an error floor. This removes the requirement of a state infidelity below $10^{-7}$ that was estimated for single-solve schemes. With a boundary-consistent staggered (MAC) discretization, the pressure residual equals the velocity divergence left after projection up to the factor $Δt/ρ$. Finally, we carried out the variational training itself on ibm_cleveland, so that every sampled value of the cost function was obtained from hardware measurements. The residual fell to $5.6\times10^{-4}$ in seven corrections, below the pre-specified tolerance of $10^{-3}$, at the rate predicted by a noise model and with 24 min of QPU time.

Abstract: Quantum correlation measurements have emerged as a powerful tool for localizing multiple emitters with subwavelength separation.   <summary>Details</summary>

This paper settles this problem by proving that the exact quantum query complexity of \(f\) is \(4\). For the lower bound, we express every three-query algorithm through layer-by-layer Gram matrices of low-degree Fourier coefficients. We then construct a fixed rational dual certificate whose slack matrices are all positive definite. This certificate separates the necessary Gram-matrix region from the exact-output condition and therefore rules out every exact algorithm using at most three queries in the stated model. The positivity verification reduces to five small rational matrices and Sylvester's criterion. For the upper bound, we invoke the previously known four-query algorithm for the seven-bit function \(\text{EXACT}^7_{3,4}\) and apply the standard padding reduction. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ergotropy（循环幺正操作可提取的最大功）的产生与激活是量子电池研究的核心。正温热态是被动的，而非平衡环境可维持具有非零ergotropy的非被动稳态。然而Lindblad描述中常用的完全世俗近似会抑制稳态相干性，从而掩盖其对ergotropy的贡献，因此需要超越该近似来正确刻画稳态功资源。

Result: 同频两比特的NESS ergotropy完全为相干成分，且近平衡处随温度偏置呈二次增长；失谐比特则可呈现非相干ergotropy。激活的精确解析判据表明，激活要求较热热库耦合到裸频率较高的比特。有无完全世俗化的NESS在ergotropy上表现出定量与定性差异，反映相干与非相干贡献的变化。

Abstract: We analytically characterize how the location and the local or correlated structure of stochastic Pauli errors affect a three-qubit coherent quantum-teleportation circuit. These results clarify the scope of applicability of the automata-theoretic toolbox for quantum circuit verification.

The overlap of two random qubit states attains its mode at its mean for every rotation-invariant ensemble, but for Hilbert--Schmidt qutrits the density at the mean has a strictly negative derivative, so mode and mean separate; for large environments, the centred overlap converges to a variance-gamma law rather than a Gaussian. For two noncommuting qubit observables, the joint law of the two standard deviations is derived for all $K$, and the probability of coming within a small distance of the optimal sum of variances scales as a power of that distance, with a different exponent when the Bloch axes are orthogonal. For three Haar-random qubit states, the exact density of the Bargmann invariant is given in terms of a complete elliptic integral, together with its local behaviour and elementary marginals. In every dimension, the probability that its real part is negative---certifying basis-independent coherence---is computed, along with all mixed moments. For cycles of arbitrary length, the invariant is shown to be driven by an exact three-parameter Markov chain, and the logarithm of its modulus obeys a law of large numbers and a central limit theorem.

Abstract: Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) supports secure key establishment under ideal protocol assumptions, but practical systems remain exposed to implementation-level attacks against optical channels, detectors, and controller behavior. This study presents Quantum Forensic Log (QF-LOG), a simulation-based forensic framework for analyzing aggregated QKD operational logs rather than raw quantum states. QF-LOG uses four observable features: quantum bit error rate (QBER), photon count, latency in milliseconds, and abort flag. The framework classifies normal operation and three simulated attack-related conditions: partial intercept-resend, detector-blind, and fiber-tap. A physics-inspired Generator-A dataset with 5 million balanced records was constructed using explicit mathematical generation models for all four features. A transparent threshold baseline, logistic regression, random forest, and XGBoost were evaluated on Generator-A. The full-feature learning models achieved macro F1-scores from 0.8211 to 0.8243 and outperformed the transparent two-feature comparator. Feature diagnostics showed that QBER contributed most, followed by latency in milliseconds and photon count, while no single feature reproduced the full feature set performance. Robustness was assessed on a separately generated Generator-B dataset of 5 million records that uses the same governing equations under shifted simulated operating parameters. Without retraining or tuning, their macro F1-scores decreased to values from 0.5645 to 0.5669. These findings show that QF-LOG can support interpretable forensic classification in a controlled synthetic setting, but stable internal evaluation does not establish reliability under changed simulated QKD operating conditions. The framework provides a documented basis for future evaluation across broader simulated settings and hardware-generated QKD logs.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开放量子多体系统中量子疤痕在耗散存在下的稳健性，明确何种耗散条件能够保留疤痕结构、何种会破坏它。

Result: 当跃迁算符尊重Hamiltonian对称性时，Liouvillian谱出现疤痕塔：与Néel态大重叠、虚部间隙等间距且由单一频率设定。长时动力学是否由疤痕主导取决于本征值实部，即自旋翻转向下/向上两弛豫速率之比γ±。非对称耗散（γ+ > γ-）下疤痕本征值实部最小，保真度振荡增强并被交错跃迁算符进一步稳定；而以部分投影算符替代完整投影算符会破坏对称性，并彻底摧毁耗散疤痕。

Conclusion: 耗散疤痕的存在依赖于跃迁算符与Hamiltonian的对称性一致性；弛豫速率的不对称性决定疤痕是否主导长时动力学，而对称性破缺（部分投影）会使疤痕消失。

Abstract: We investigate the fate of quantum many-body scars in the PXP model subject to Markovian dissipation. We show that when local jump operators respect the same symmetries of the Hamiltonian the full Liouvillian spectrum displays a tower of states with properties analogous to standard quantum scars: large overlaps with Néel states and equally spaced gaps in the imaginary part of the eigenvalues, set by a single frequency. Whether the long-time dynamics is dominated by scars is determined by the real parts of the eigenvalues, which are set by the ratio between the two relaxation rates $γ_{\pm}$ describing spin flips down-to-up or up-to-down respectively. For asymmetric dissipation ($γ_+ > γ_-$) the scarred eigenvalues have the smallest real parts: the resulting enhanced fidelity oscillations are further stabilized by staggered jump operators. In contrast, replacing the full projection operator in the dissipators with a partial one breaks the symmetries of the problem and is seen to destroy dissipative scars altogether.

</details>

Abstract: The realization of fault-tolerant quantum computation relies on Quantum Error-Correcting Codes (QECC). Of particular interest are codes that combine a high encoding rate with sufficient distance. Existing algebraic constructions, such as lifted product (LP) codes, build a large quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) code from a classical base matrix. In this work we introduce LiftDiff, a generative approach to the discovery of QECC. A discrete denoising diffusion model is trained to generate base matrices over the group algebra $\mathbb{F}_2[\mathbb{Z}_\ell]$, which are then expanded into LP codes. The reverse diffusion process is guided by an energy function that evaluates quantum code properties, including girth, degree, sparsity and decoding performance, steering the sampler toward matrices that yield LP codes with favorable parameters. Our method discovers codes with parameters such as $[[169,13,11]]$, $[[143,15,9]]$, and $[[260,24,10]]$, including a $[[247,19,14]]$ code that achieves the highest ${kd^2}/{n}$ among the baselines. Moreover, several discovered codes outperform the Gross code across a broad range of the tested physical error rates under both code-capacity and circuit-level noise.

</details>

For dynamics, the finite-basis projection error of the exact wave function does not capture the full basis-discretization error: an additional dynamical projection error enters our bound at a different rate, and we introduce an inner-product argument to control this contribution. Interestingly, the spatial convergence rates for wave-function dynamics and ground-state energies differ quantitatively. For ground-state energies, we establish a sharp $M^{-1}$ convergence rate at fixed cell size, where $M$ is the number of one-particle basis functions. Abstract: Laser amplification kicks off with just a few photons in the cavity, a regime where standard additive Langevin noise cannot capture the real fluctuations. Stationary rate equations reveal that stimulated emission takes over across a tight pump window. In nanolasers, however, spontaneous processes coexist across a much broader range. The destruction of an excited carrier concurrent with the generation of a photon creates a negative photon-carrier cross-correlation. This unavoidably leads to a correlated, multiplicative Gaussian-noise framework. We track the resulting discrete dynamics with an established, event-based Laser Markov Chain (LMC) which stochastically samples pump, relaxation, emission, and cavity-loss events keeping all physical quantities as non-negative integers. Comparing its predictions to those of our multiplicative model proves that the latter reproduces excellently well the statistical behavior of photons and carrier, as long as a continuous diffusion description holds. Once the emission drops into the few-photon regime, however, the discrete model becomes indispensable. On the other hand, at large photon counts relative fluctuations shrink to a level where the state-dependence of the diffusion matrix can be ignored, to leading order, thus returning to the classic Langevin description of the system's stochastic dynamics.

Abstract: We study whether a quantum verifier can find a hidden quantum state by exchanging classical messages with an unbounded prover who knows the state. The verifier has access to a reflection oracle about the target. For a Haar-random $n$-qubit target, we prove that achieving constant average fidelity with the target requires $(q+1)^2(B+1)=Ω(2^n)$, where $q$ is the number of verifier queries and $B$ is the total prover-to-verifier communication. The bound allows arbitrary classical interaction and unrestricted computation between queries. Our proof extends the lower bound of Aaronson and Kuperberg from a single classical proof to an arbitrary classical interaction.

  We further give an oracle construction for a notion known as QMA-QCIP[2] gap problem. This gives average case hardness for a QMA language under classical access to a QCIP[2] decision oracle and is used in establishing negative results on witness encryption and quantum indistinguishability obfuscation.

Abstract: Complex amplitudes can be simulated over real Hilbert spaces by adjoining a two-dimensional real/imaginary degree of freedom. For the canonical three-party GHZ program, the translation lies entirely inside the ordinary black-and-white misty gate fragment: controlled-$J$ replaces the complex phase operation, while the invariant $J_jJ_k|0\rangle_P=-|0\rangle_P$ transfers the required branch sign. This yields a short correctness proof for all four promised GHZ inputs and explains the earlier 64-path cancellation calculation at a single logical phase-code boundary. Abstract: The quantum phase of a multiparticle system has remained a long-standing mystery in quantum physics. The absence of a phase operator that faithfully represents this physical quantity has prevented the formulation of a meaningful uncertainty relation with particle number. Here, we resolve this problem by measuring, for the first time, the quantum phase of multiparticle systems containing up to fifteen particles. We achieve this through a new quantum state tomography scheme that combines homodyne interferometry with photon-number-resolving detection. This approach enables us to reconstruct complementary photon-number and phase representations and establish the corresponding number-phase uncertainty relation. Our measurements further provide access to the mutually unbiased basis of a multiparticle system, a fundamental resource for multiparticle quantum information processing. Furthermore, we report the spatially resolved characterization of the quantum phase. These results provide an operational framework for measuring quantum phase in multiparticle systems, with implications for fundamental quantum physics and scalable multiparticle quantum technologies.

Abstract: Many quantum-information tasks can benefit from Gaussian quantum resources, while their fundamental performance limits are characterized by quantum information quantities. However, a unified perturbation theory for these quantities directly within the Gaussian-state formalism has been lacking. We develop such a theory for the von Neumann entropy, quantum relative entropy, quantum Chernoff quantity, and quantum fidelity, using perturbations of the moments of a reference Gaussian state. Using general Fréchet-derivative identities, we derive compact second-order expressions for reference-state covariance matrices whose symplectic eigenvalues are all greater than $1/2$. We also show that the perturbative quantum Chernoff quantity is maximized at its symmetric point, yielding the quantum Chernoff exponent (QCE). We further show that symplectic eigenvalues equal to 1/2 can lead to slower asymptotic vanishing, potentially improving the corresponding fundamental performance limits. For reference-state covariance matrices containing symplectic eigenvalues both equal to and greater than 1/2, we identify sufficient conditions under which the former determine the leading covariance-matrix contributions, allowing the remaining modes to be traced out without changing the leading-order asymptotic scaling or the coefficient. Applying the framework to quantum illumination and incoherent-light sub-Rayleigh object discrimination, we recover known asymptotic QCEs directly within the Gaussian-state formalism. In the latter problem, the slower asymptotic vanishing explains the known quadratic scaling of the QCE with the source separation.

Abstract: Plasma simulations for magnetic-confinement fusion (MCF) involve high-dimensional kinetic dynamics, strong nonlinear coupling, complex geometries, and multiscale spatiotemporal behavior, imposing severe computational bottlenecks. Current studies remain largely restricted to linearized equations, reduced models, or isolated computational subroutines, leaving a substantial gap from realistic fusion simulations. We summarize key problem characteristics, including high dimensionality, structured dynamics, and measurement requirements, under which plasma applications are most likely to yield a quantum advantage. Guided by these considerations, we highlight potential approaches and present a roadmap toward practical quantum advantage of simulating plasma in MCF.

Abstract: Quantum states are ultimately responsible for numerous counterintuitive phenomena that defy classical intuition. As the development of quantum technologies accelerates, our ability to harness these quantum resources becomes essential. This review addresses a fundamental question. How do we quantify the quantumness of a state, and which specific traits distinguish it from its classical counterparts? We examine quantumness through both theoretical and operational lenses, providing a clear framework for state characterization. Ultimately, we demonstrate that certifying quantumness is not just a theoretical exercise. It is a practical necessity for identifying, and realizing quantum advantage in real-world applications.

Abstract: The use of entanglement in sensing is often motivated by improved sensitivity arising from non-classical correlations among probes. For static signals, however, this quantum advantage in Ramsey spectroscopy with $N$ probes is known to be highly susceptible to dephasing, which severely limits the possible gain from entanglement. In contrast, we consider sensing of signals that vary in time. Considering the simplest example of dephasing by classical white noise, we show that entanglement allows a large increase in bandwidth when estimating the frequency components of a time-dependent signal. Without sacrificing any sensitivity, entanglement allows for an increase in bandwidth by a factor of $N$. Alternatively, when interrogation times are limited by the signal bandwidth rather than by dephasing, the average estimation uncertainty over a fixed bandwidth is reduced by a factor of $\sqrt{N}$ compared with separable states.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Accurately characterizing molecular properties and reactivity often requires electronic-structure calculations across an ensemble of molecular geometries. Quantum algorithms that process such ensembles coherently could address this challenge, but they require Hamiltonian block encoding circuits that combine advanced factorization techniques with sublinear scaling in ensemble size. Here, we introduce SVD-THC, which extends tensor hypercontraction to sets of Hamiltonians defined at different nuclear geometries through a singular value decomposition of the ensemble. We use SVD-THC to construct a single multiplexed block encoding circuit for all geometries in an ensemble. We then evaluate the approach on ensembles generated by varying internal rotational coordinates in 1-bromo-2-chloroethane as well as in glycine and alanine dipeptides, corresponding to established model systems for peptide conformational landscapes, with up to 5,184 geometries. Across several resolutions of the internal-coordinate grids, the Toffoli complexity of the resulting circuits grows only weakly with the number of geometries, changing by at most 12\% over grids that differ in size by up to 576$\times$. In an energy-thresholding circuit, this yields 35--200$\times$ fewer Toffoli gates than slicing together single-geometry block encodings. This approach could help make coherent processing of molecular geometry ensembles a useful primitive for fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作已表明在相互作用主导情形下，将子系统的共轭变量耦合到不同环境模式会使子系统趋于最大混合态，但该现象背后的相空间机制尚不明确，且此前结论多限于特定设定。本文旨在推广到完整的二次动力学并给出机制解释。

Method: 考虑单模子系统耦合双模环境，处理完整的二次哈密顿量动力学；通过精确追踪约化Weyl特征函数与Wigner函数随时间的演化，分析其在固定紧区域上对局部相空间坐标的依赖性；并对自由粒子模型与谐振子模型两种情形分别讨论，在有限体积与动量正则化下与最大混合态对应。

Result: 约化Weyl特征函数与Wigner函数在每个固定紧区域上逐渐丧失对局部相空间坐标的依赖；自由粒子模型中该行为对所有耦合强度成立，谐振子模型中则需超过某一临界耦合值；在有限体积与动量正则化下，该行为对应约化子系统趋于最大混合态。

Conclusion: 子系统趋于最大混合态的根源是相空间中约化分布局域依赖性的消失，且存在模型依赖的耦合阈值（谐振子情形需超临界耦合，自由粒子情形无阈值）；结果为理解环境诱导退相干与不可逆混合提供了精确的相空间刻画。

Abstract: In an earlier work, we showed that, in the interaction-dominant setting, coupling the conjugate variables of a subsystem to distinct environment modes drives the subsystem to a maximally mixed state. Here, we extend the study to the full quadratic dynamics of a single-mode subsystem coupled to a two-mode environment, and identify the phase-space mechanism responsible for the asymptotic behavior. By tracking the exact reduced Weyl characteristic and Wigner functions, we demonstrate that they lose their dependence on local phase-space coordinates on every fixed compact region. In free-particle model, this happens for all values of coupling, whereas in the harmonic oscillator model, it happens above a critical value of the coupling. Under a finite-volume and momentum regularization, this behavior corresponds to the reduced subsystem approaching a maximally mixed state.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星基量子链路可克服光纤指数损耗，但上行架构中纠缠光子在经历大气与几何损耗的同时，单卫星双上行配置还要求两个地面站与卫星同时可视，导致地面站间距受限。

Method: 构建双上行双卫星拓扑，引入星间链路（ISL）解耦地面站间距与大气上行几何；在相同物理假设下与单卫星方案对比，采用包含几何衰减、大气湍流、背景噪声与Bell态测量效率的真实信道模型，计算成功概率与实际保真度随卫星高度和地面站间距的变化。

Conclusion: 双上行双卫星架构是可扩展量子互联网基础设施的关键一步。

Abstract: Satellite-based quantum links are a promising enabler of long-distance entanglement distribution, overcoming the exponential losses of optical fibers. In uplink architectures, entangled photons are generated at ground stations and transmitted to orbiting satellites, reducing space-segment complexity but introducing significant atmospheric and geometric losses. Single-satellite dual-uplink configurations are additionally limited by the requirement of simultaneous visibility between both ground stations and the satellite.
  In this work, we investigate a dual-uplink dual-satellite network architecture and compare its performance with the single-satellite scheme under identical physical assumptions. The proposed topology introduces an inter-satellite link (ISL) that decouples the ground-station separation from the atmospheric uplink geometry. A realistic channel model is employed, incorporating geometric attenuation, atmospheric turbulence, background noise, and Bell-state measurement efficiency. The overall success probability and practical fidelity are evaluated as functions of satellite altitude and ground-station separation.
  Our results show that while the single-satellite configuration is suitable for regional-scale networks, it becomes infeasible for large separations due to geometric constraints. The proposed dual-satellite architecture mitigates this limitation and significantly extends the achievable communication distance, representing a key step toward scalable quantum internet infrastructures.

</details>

Abstract: We present a fully quantum theory of time-resolved transient absorption spectroscopy, providing a general framework for describing the linear response of a nonequilibrium target to quantum probe fields beyond the semiclassical approximation. We elucidate how quantum correlations between probe-field frequency modes couple to coherences in the target and derive a simple expression for computing the resulting spectroscopic signals. We identify conditions under which quantum light can modify transient absorption spectra beyond the limits achievable with classical fields. Our work establishes a theoretical foundation for exploiting quantum light in ultrafast spectroscopy and suggests new routes for resolving dynamics in matter on timescales shorter than the duration of the probe pulse.

Abstract: Light curves reveal stellar variability and support studies of stellar evolution and cosmic distances. Reliable classification is therefore important for large time-domain surveys. We present HyQ-LC for ten-class stellar light-curve classification using 359 handcrafted features and 633,034 training objects. On a shared 20,000-object test set, the hybrid has macro-averaged F1 (Macro-F1) point gains of 0.8276 and 1.2393 percentage points over the pure MLP on two fine-tuning seeds. A separate fixed-fusion configuration achieves slightly higher Macro-F1 but lower accuracy than approximately budget-matched LightGBM. Its positive class-level differences are concentrated in CEP, RRC, and ROT, while the full-budget LightGBM reference scores higher on both metrics. All circuits are simulated classically. The comparisons retain existing validation-selected checkpoints and use the same test objects, with features, predictions, and recomputation code publicly available. The results identify point-estimate improvements over the tested controls while leaving the specific contribution of the quantum branch unresolved.

Abstract: Quantum communication networks promise information-theoretic security but this security collapses if intermediate relay nodes are compromised, a risk that grows sharply when compromised nodes cannot be identified. Second, we provide a comprehensive statistical and graph-theoretic analysis of network and node security under compromise, covering full and partial node compromise, optimal placement of additional edges via an iterative method, and comparative hacking-probability analysis across network topologies. Together, these results identify the topology and multi-path routing strategy that most improves security for a given network. We apply this framework to quantum key distribution across multiple network topologies, demonstrating a scalable route to secure, reliable quantum communication under realistic, unidentified adversarial threats, without relying on quantum memory.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate open-system charging protocols for Rabi and Dicke quantum batteries under direct incoherent pumping. The charging dynamics follows a Lindblad master equation, while effective non-Hermitian generators describe the conditioned no-jump mechanism. At the selected Rabi operating points, pumping also increases ergotropy and the extractable fraction of the stored energy. For finite Dicke batteries, direct incoherent pumping likewise stabilizes the charging dynamics. Across the finite-size scan, the strongest pump considered improves the peak stored energy and ergotropy per cell and near-peak timing robustness relative to the Hermitian reference. The maximum average charging power also increases, although this metric includes pump-assisted jump contributions. Equal-input comparisons show that the relative performance of common-cavity Dicke and independent Rabi batteries varies with the pump-input bound. Taken together, these results show that direct incoherent pumping provides a route to stabilized charging in Rabi and finite Dicke quantum batteries.

Abstract: Classical methods for computing quantum expectation values often propagate observables in the Heisenberg picture. To remain tractable, these methods rely on approximations that discard operator components during evolution. Yet the magnitude of these components alone does not determine the resulting expectation-value error, which also depends on their subsequent evolution and the input state. We develop \emph{generalized Pauli propagation}, a common algebraic framework encompassing Pauli propagation, Lie-algebraic simulation, and Krylov subspace methods. Using weighted commutator graphs, we derive exact identities that relate approximation errors to the signed contributions of omitted and discarded operator components. Our central result establishes \emph{delayed observability}, identifying conditions under which these contributions vanish at low orders in propagation time. We use this structure to develop finite-order error estimates and state-dependent selection rules. We further prove that retaining the symmetry-preserving part of a weak perturbation eliminates first-order expectation-value errors under suitable symmetry conditions. By revealing how approximation errors affect the desired expectation value, our framework provides a basis for more informative error analysis and the development of readout-aware simulation strategies.

Abstract: Sidewall morphology is a potentially important factor influencing superconducting qubit performance, yet its quantitative characterization remains challenging due to the absence of standardized measurement protocols.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清量子储层记忆容量的真正限制资源：通常直觉认为更大的系统或更复杂的动力学能带来更长记忆，但噪声环境下记忆究竟受什么制约尚不明确。

Abstract: Fresh qubits, not size, set how long a noisy quantum system can remember. With $k$ input qubits reinitialized per time step, the longest memory any reservoir can attain under depolarizing noise is $\exp[Θ(k)]$ time steps, whatever its size, dynamics, and input encoding. A universal upper bound and an explicit reservoir that attains the exponential scaling establish this optimal resource law. The bound depends on the dynamics only through the entropy that fresh qubits remove per time step, so it holds for any Markovian noisy dynamics without feedback, including measurement-free error correction.

</details>

Abstract: Mixed quantum states can exhibit unconventional forms of symmetry breaking without pure-state counterparts. A prominent example is strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SW-SSB), which cannot be diagnosed by observables linear in the density matrix. Here, we experimentally resolve finite-size critical signatures of SW-SSB transitions on a superconducting quantum processor. We develop a measurement protocol that combines quantum measurements with classical processing based on the known dephasing channel to reconstruct nonlinear mixed-state correlators. Using only two measurement settings, the protocol reconstructs fidelity, Rényi-2, and linear correlators, as well as string observables. Applying this approach to matrix-product states subjected to power-law-correlated dephasing, we observe finite-size crossings and scaling collapses in nonlinear correlators, accompanied by decaying linear correlations. We further map the estimated SW-SSB transition points across initial-state and channel parameters. Measurements of linear and Rényi-2 string observables further reveal signatures of mixed-state symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases, with a nonlinear string-order crossover aligned with an independently estimated SW-SSB transition point. Our results establish a quantum-classical approach to probing SW-SSB critical behavior and mixed-state SPT order.

</details>

Conclusion: 在测试的计算预算内，残差预测对紧凑模拟QNN有效，能显著改善物理空间MSE并优于紧凑经典残差MLP；但更强的经典参考（Grundner-Q1适配）仍取得更低误差，划定了明确边界。结果不构成计算量子优势的证据，也不代表在线气候模式技巧。

Abstract: Compact quantum neural networks have been explored for cloud-cover parameterization, but their predictive value depends on how physical knowledge is incorporated and which classical models provide the reference. We investigate an offline residual formulation that retains a fitted \XR\ cloud-cover estimate and learns a correction to its logit in transformed target space. Using released ICON/DYAMOND-derived data, we compare ten model variants in six- and eight-input settings, with three matched replicates and 10,000 training samples per replicate. All quantum circuits use local Z readout and noiseless exact expectation values. Under a common 30-epoch protocol, residual prediction reduced physical-space test mean squared error relative to direct QNN prediction by 54.3\% and 38.8\% for six-input XYZ and ZZXY circuits, and by 49.2\% and 38.9\% for their eight-input versions. Residual QNNs also outperformed the selected compact residual multilayer perceptron in every paired replicate. A larger, longer-trained Grundner-Q1 adaptation nevertheless achieved lower error: residual Q1 reached mean MSEs of 0.00778 and 0.00791, compared with 0.01124 and 0.01082 for residual XYZ. The results support residual prediction for compact simulated QNNs within the tested budget and establish a clear boundary set by stronger classical references. They do not establish computational quantum advantage or online climate-model skill.

Abstract: We introduce squeezing-type lower bounds for a hierarchy of variance bounds under specific unbiasedness constraints, which generalizes the quantum Cramér-Rao bound. Our construction directly extends standard squeezing coefficients to the corresponding generalized metrological sensitivities while retaining their experimentally accessible structure. We show how observables maximizing these bounds can be identified analytically within families of accessible observables. If arbitrary observables are accessible, the optimized squeezing-type bound recovers the exact generalized metrological sensitivity. These bounds provide efficient and experimentally viable characterizations of generalized metrological sensitivities that are especially relevant in finite-shot scenarios and in the transition from local to global parameter estimation.

</details>

Abstract: The Parity Architecture was originally introduced in the context of quantum optimization algorithms to overcome hardware connectivity constraints. Here, we show that its benefits extend beyond this role by identifying specific parity transformations that enhance the success probability of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) for optimization problems defined on hardware-native and sparse graphs. Numerical simulations suggest that this enhancement originates from the higher-order operators introduced by the Parity representation, which accelerate the propagation of correlations across the physical device. We investigate this mechanism for the Signed Max-Cut problem on 3-regular graph instances and demonstrate its practical relevance on IBM Heron quantum processors. QAOA parameters are first classically optimized on small training instances containing up to 14 vertices and subsequently transferred to larger instances executed on quantum hardware. We consider Signed Max-Cut instances of up to 40 vertices and find that, together with an error mitigation technique, the parity-based QAOA identifies the optimal solutions for all the considered 40-vertex instances, whereas the conventional QAOA fails under the same conditions.

Motivation: 实用规模量子技术不仅需要性能良好的量子平台，还需要可扩展、高效、满足量子系统设计要求的经典控制硬件。硅CMOS为高度可扩展的成熟技术，可用于设计针对量子系统优化的专用集成电路（ASIC）。

Abstract: Utility-scale quantum technologies will not only require a well-performing quantum platform, but also classical control hardware that is scalable, efficient, and meets the design requirements set by the quantum system of interest. One approach is the application of silicon CMOS technology, a highly scalable and mature technology, to design application-specific integrated circuits (ASIC) that are optimized for the quantum system. In this work, we present two-qubit control of electron and nuclear spins in NV centres in diamond, using a cryogenic CMOS qubit controller integrated with a diamond quantum device in a cryostat. Using Gate Set Tomography (GST) we characterize the performance of the single-qubit gates on both electron- and carbon nuclear spins, and analyze the limiting noise processes. We use dynamical decoupling to engineer a two-qubit gate, and estimate $F_\text{2q,avg} = 95(2)\%$ for the electron-nuclear two-qubit gate fidelity. Our demonstration of cryo-CMOS-driven two-qubit gates is a key step towards highly-integrated quantum devices based on colour centres.

This formulation identifies relativistic kinematics as the physical origin of quantum defects, offering a streamlined basis for future studies on complex many-body correlations.

</details>


### [75] [The Four-Qubit Hyperbolic Quadric Has Contextuality Degree 315](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26465)
*Zhengyu Li,Amandeep Gupta,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明四量子比特双曲二次曲面 Q⁺(7,2) 的 contextuality degree 确为 315，且每个最优赋值违反的上下文构成与 DW(5,2) 同构的集合，共 240 个；结果经 Lean 4 与 DRAT 机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muller 等人通过启发式搜索提出两个猜想：Q⁺(7,2)（135 点、1575 条线为上下文）的 contextuality degree 为 315，且每个最优赋值的违反上下文集构成对偶极空间 DW(5,2)。本文旨在严格证明这两个猜想，并为更一般的量子比特数情形建立下界。

Method: 下界：利用每个 doily（GQ(2,2)）含十个 Peres-Mermin 网格、每格线被覆盖四次，得每赋值至少违反 15 条线中的 3 条，对 doily 平均得 1575/5=315。等号情形局部化：赋值最优当且仅当它在每个 doily 中违反某网格的一个 ruling。以此作为 SAT 编码进行枚举。形式化：degree 定理在 Lean 4 中核验，枚举完备性由 DRAT 证明经已验证检查器 cake_lpr 认证。

Result: 严格证明 degree 为 315，并证明恰好存在 240 个最优违反集合，构成 O⁺(8,2) 的一个轨道，每个都与 DW(5,2) 同构。此外对任意 N≥4 量子比特、含 L 条线的二次曲面，首次给出下界 ⌈L/5⌉。

Conclusion: Muller 等人的两个猜想均被证明；等号情形的刻画提供了可计算的 SAT 判据，枚举完备性由形式化验证保障。方法可推广至一般二次曲面，给出统一下界 ⌈L/5⌉。

Abstract: The contextuality degree of a set of Pauli contexts is the least number of contexts that every noncontextual $\pm1$ assignment violates. For the four-qubit hyperbolic quadric $\mathcal{Q}^{+}(7,2)$, with $135$ points and $1575$ lines as contexts, Muller et al. conjectured from heuristic searches that the degree is $315$ and that the violated contexts of every optimal assignment form a dual polar space $DW(5,2)$. We prove both conjectures. The lower bound has a short proof by hand: every doily ($GQ(2,2)$) of the quadric contains ten Peres-Mermin grids covering each of its lines four times, so every assignment violates at least three of its fifteen lines, and averaging over the doilies gives $1575/5=315$. The equality case is local: an assignment is optimal exactly when it violates one ruling of one grid in every doily. Using this as a SAT encoding, we show that there are exactly $240$ optimal violated sets, one orbit of $\mathrm{O}^+(8,2)$, each isomorphic to $DW(5,2)$. The same argument gives the first lower bounds $\lceil L/5\rceil$ for all quadrics with $L$ lines in $N\ge4$ qubits. The degree theorem is kernel-checked in Lean 4, and the completeness of the enumeration is certified by a DRAT proof checked by the verified checker cake_lpr.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: golden态的存在性与Löwdin结构由重叠矩阵（及自由操作类型）决定：最大自由操作下仅需等重叠性，Kraus型操作下则需Löwdin最大相干且额外受Bargmann相位约束。Gram majorization将正交情形下的majorization判据推广到非正交参考态，等重叠态同时提供度量极值态与叠加见证。

Abstract: In the resource theory of superposition relative to linearly independent, nonorthogonal reference states, golden states are known for two reference states and for special overlap matrices, and all of them are maximally coherent in the Löwdin basis. We clarify what decides their existence and their Löwdin structure. The robustness and the geometric measure of superposition are maximized by the same states, which overlap equally with every reference state; their maximal values are set by the ground-state energy of a classical $XY$ model on the overlaps of the dual basis. For every overlap matrix, these states, and only these, are golden under the maximal superposition-free operations, but they need not be maximally coherent in the Löwdin basis. For the Kraus-type free operations, we prove a necessary and sufficient criterion, Gram majorization, for converting pure states into targets with nonzero coefficients; it reduces to majorization for orthonormal reference states. It shows that golden states of these operations must be maximally coherent in the Löwdin basis, but that this does not ensure their existence: three reference states that are not orthonormal admit a golden state if and only if their overlaps have a common modulus $s$ and their Bargmann phase $Φ$ obeys $s\geq2\cos[(π+|Φ|)/3]$. Equal-overlap states also yield a complete family of superposition witnesses in the Löwdin basis, which show that white noise on the span of the reference states is a resource unless these states are orthonormal.

</details>

Abstract: In hyperon-antihyperon production through electron-positron annihilation, quantum steering provides a stricter test of nonclassical spin correlations than entanglement. We determine when steering survives angular averaging and optimize angular selections to reduce the sample size needed to resolve it. In the specified production-frame spin convention, we show that windows symmetric about 90 degrees with even production-angle acceptance yield phase-independent Bell-diagonal states. Applying the necessary and sufficient criterion for steering under arbitrary dichotomic projective measurements determines the exact boundary for this family and reveals a steering region missed by the three-setting Cavalcanti-Jones-Wiseman-Reid (CJWR) criterion. For positive production parameters and uniform full-range acceptance, the steering threshold is $α\simeq0.36065$. We derive the covariance of weak-decay angular moments, construct fixed linear witnesses, and optimize the angular window. For $J/ψ\toΛ\barΛ$, steering survives full-range averaging. The statistical model predicts that, after separately optimizing the two angular selections at nominal $z=5$, the required full-range pair sample decreases from $8.61\times10^4$ for CJWR to $2.80\times10^4$ for the projective witness, a 67.4% reduction. This advantage persists under the production-acceptance and analyzing-power variations studied.

Abstract: The formulation of quantum measurements compatible with relativistic causality is obstructed by the tension between the locality of observable algebras and the dynamical state transformations induced by measurement processes. We develop a local measurement theory for quantum cellular automata, lattices of finite-dimensional quantum systems with a causal reversible dynamics evolving in discrete time steps. Measurement processes are described by localized completely positive maps and organized into operational measurement protocols, finite non-adaptive sequences of local instruments distributed across the causal cone induced by the automaton local dynamics. Each protocol induces an effective measurement on the initial time slice, whose support is constrained by the causal structure generated by the automaton. Non-local measurement effects therefore arise through causal propagation and composition of local operations, rather than from primitive non-local interventions or instantaneous collapse prescriptions. We establish the corresponding spatial support bounds and characterize the resulting measurement architecture. As an explicit example, we construct a two-step protocol for a one-dimensional nearest-neighbour qubit automaton which, for suitable automaton parameters, realizes a Bell-basis projective measurement on two neighbouring sites after coarse-graining of the local outcomes. Away from this point, the same protocol generates a continuous family of deformed Bell measurements whose entanglement and stabilizer Rényi non-classicality can be quantified explicitly. The framework thus provides a causally consistent operator-algebraic mechanism for synthesizing spatially extended quantum measurements from strictly local operations.

</details>

Abstract: An important aspect of many linear optical quantum computing architectures is probabilistically generating small dual-rail qubit entangled states, or seed states. While many schemes to achieve this have been discovered, attention has been primarily focused on minimizing input resources and maximizing success probability rather than establishing a detailed understanding of linear-optical error performance. In this work, we develop a framework for resolving sensitivities to physically distinct errors in linear optical state generation, allowing us to identify precise ways in which these errors affect the properties of the output state. We use this framework to identify seed state generators in which destructive many-photon interference prevents detector loss and detector noise from causing a falsely successful heralding event. We show that this effect suppresses errors, for example cutting the effective loss on the output of particular Bell state generators in half relative to a standard scheme. We also present a new Bell state generator in which both detector loss and detector noise do not affect the quality of the output Bell state to first order, and a 4GHZ generator with >75% higher success probability than a standard circuit. In addition we provide some useful identities, bounds and invariants for studying linear optical seed state generation.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum annealing generally samples degenerate ground states with nonuniform probabilities. This unfair sampling depends on how the driver Hamiltonian couples these states near the end of annealing. For combinatorial optimization problems with a cardinality constraint, the penalty method and constrained quantum annealing (CQA) differ in these couplings. The transverse-field driver of the penalty method does not directly couple feasible ground states, whereas the fully connected XY driver of CQA couples pairs of them related by a single spin exchange. We investigate how this difference affects ground-state sampling, using closed-system simulations of random graph bipartitioning instances together with degenerate perturbation theory. For the instances studied, the conditional ground-state distribution of penalty-based quantum annealing approaches that of CQA at sufficiently large penalties and long annealing times. This convergence can be explained by the effective Hamiltonian of penalty-based quantum annealing, in which virtual single-spin flips to infeasible states generate a second-order interaction proportional to the CQA driver projected onto the feasible ground-state manifold, which is the leading term of the effective Hamiltonian of CQA. In the numerical simulations, the Jensen--Shannon divergence between the conditional ground-state distributions of penalty-based quantum annealing and CQA decreases approximately as the inverse square of the penalty-to-objective energy ratio, consistent with the perturbative analysis. This correspondence between the effective Hamiltonians extends to equal-weight fixed-magnetization constraints under the same perturbative conditions, providing a common mechanism for sampling bias in the two implementations.

Abstract: Accurate modelling of chemical reaction rates requires precise correlated energy differences. Quantum computing offers a potential route to their determination, but extracting energies from quantum-prepared states can demand prohibitive sampling. We demonstrate measurement-efficient correlated energy estimation using an electronically challenging H$_2$DBB--Ru model derived from a Ru-functionalized hafnium metal-organic framework relevant to radiodynamic and photodynamic therapies. The mixed estimator combines a quantum-prepared target with sparse multiconfiguration references and recovers ground-state corrections using magnitude-only compute-uncompute measurements. Analytical variance estimates predict 1.6 mHa precision with a few hundred shots, about 100 times fewer than the single-determinant formulation and three orders of magnitude fewer than optimized Hamiltonian-term sampling. With approximate targets, it reaches comparable accuracy using simpler state preparation than direct expectation-value estimation. Emulator and trapped-ion experiments on Quantinuum Helios validate the approach up to (10,10), including a 50-qubit circuit combining AI-assisted state preparation with error detection, providing a measurement-efficient route to correlated energy differences for challenging chemical problems.

</details>

Conclusion: 加速对纠缠的影响强烈依赖于耦合方式：非RWA（对称）耦合保留反旋转项从而使稳态纠缠随加速度变化，而RWA耦合下加速效应被抵消，纠缠保持不变。

Abstract: We derive a general Gorini-Kossakowski-Lindblad-Sudarshan master equation for two co-located, uniformly accelerated two-level detectors weakly coupled to scalar fields in the Minkowski vacuum, retaining coherent and dissipative contributions for arbitrary orientations, field masses, and spacetime dimensions. For equal orientations, we determine stationary states and their entanglement for two couplings to massless fields in four dimensions: symmetric coupling yields acceleration-dependent concurrence, whereas rotating-wave coupling gives acceleration-independent concurrence.

Method: 以完全保持正部分转置（PPT）的操作作为辅助资源，构造匹配的上界与可达方案，对热损耗、热放大、加性高斯噪声信道的量子通信与纠缠分发容量进行精确求解，并推广至给定平均输入能量约束。

Abstract: We determine the exact quantum communication and entanglement-distribution capacities of three fundamental bosonic channels, namely thermal loss, thermal amplification and additive Gaussian noise, assisted by operations that completely preserve positive partial transpose (PPT). These operations are more powerful than local operations and classical communication (LOCC), but cannot generate distillable entanglement on their own. For thermal loss, the unconstrained capacity coincides with the Pirandola-Laurenza-Ottaviani-Banchi (PLOB) bound throughout the non-entanglement-breaking regime. The corresponding upper bounds are also attained for thermal amplification and additive noise. We extend these results to energy-constrained communication, establishing exact capacities for all three channels at every prescribed mean input energy. Our results solve a class of bosonic capacity problems under PPT assistance and establish exact benchmarks for determining whether the same communication rates can be achieved via LOCC alone.

</details>

Abstract: We benchmark the Iterative Redecode Scheme (IRS) on fast transversal Clifford circuits, achieving high-accuracy correlated decoding using local memory-style detectors and decoders. On a deep random Clifford benchmark, IRS yields approximately $4\times$ lower logical failure rates than the ghost protocol at $d=11$ with matched buffers, and $15\times$ lower rates than logical observable matching (LOM). In three-block GHZ preparation, IRS achieves $2\times$ lower logical error rates than reliable logical Pauli-product decoding with matched initialisation, improving to $25\times$ at $d=19$ with $\lceil d/2\rceil$ full initialisation rounds. We place IRS, which builds on Wan et al.'s scheme, within a broader propagation-assisted local (PAL) framework, where local decoders exchange messages rather than decoding a global hypergraph. IRS alternates local minimum-weight perfect matching (MWPM) on memory-equivalent detector error models (DEMs) with Pauli-frame propagation and detector updates across transversal CNOT layers, seeking local error estimates consistent with the global detector record. Prior-informed DEMs account for probabilities of propagated errors missing from the memory-equivalent model, improving initial decoding and reducing logical error rates. Zero-weight physical Pauli mechanisms enable inference and propagation of initially indeterminate stabilizer outcomes. Error rates saturate after a few iterations even for deep, adversarial alternating-tCNOT circuits, allowing iteration caps with negligible loss of accuracy. We find syndrome-extraction buffers of order $\lfloor d/4\rfloor$ sufficient for distance-dependent error suppression in the demanding regimes studied. The approach extends to other local decoders and CSS codes; for repeated logical $S$ gates, IRS achieves approximately $4$ to $10\times$ lower logical error rates than LOM, depending on initialisation.

</details>


### [86] [Correlated decoding loses the surface code's effective distance below threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26785)
*César Hernando de la Fuente,Joonas Majaniemi,Evert van Nieuwenburg,Felix Frohnert*

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关解码（correlated decoding）能在表面码中改善匹配/聚类解码器，但标准重加权规则在深层阈值以下可能破坏距离-d码本应纠正的尺寸-(d-1)/2故障配置，导致纠错能力退化。作者希望揭示该失效机制并加以修复。

Method: 以一条解码子图的校正结果为条件，对另一子图的边进行重加权；分析在电路级噪声下第一轮单条边对多条相关边的折扣效应（这些相关边的联合出现需要额外独立故障）；提出对重加权规则进行正则化。作者在尺寸-(d-1)/2故障配置及电路级噪声场景下评估距离缩放。

Result: 标准重加权使相关解码器在尺寸-(d-1)/2故障配置上失效，显著削弱阈值以下的错误抑制；电路级噪声下，单条首轮边会过度折扣多条相关边，使解码器偏向过度折扣的校正。正则化后恢复名义距离缩放，相关解码优势明显。

Conclusion: 简单相关重加权存在过度折扣问题，会侵蚀有效码距；对重加权规则加以正则化即可消除该缺陷，恢复名义距离缩放并保留相关解码的增益。

Abstract: Correlated decoding improves matching and clustering surface-code decoders by reweighting one decoding subgraph conditioned on the other's correction. We show that standard reweighting can cause correlated decoders to fail on size-$(d-1)/2$ fault configurations that a distance-$d$ code should correct, reducing error suppression deep below threshold. Under circuit-level noise, a single first-pass edge can discount several correlated edges, even though their joint occurrence requires additional independent faults, so the decoder favors an over-discounted correction. Regularizing the reweighting rule restores the nominal distance scaling and a clear correlated-decoding advantage.

</details>


### [87] [Fast Hubbard-Thouless Pumping with Local Counterdiabatic Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26786)
*Alexander Chansky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Rice-Mele模型中Thouless泵浦的反绝热(CD)驱动：无相互作用时变分近似有效，有相互作用时因精确多体规范势依赖跃迁所耗相互作用能，标准近邻变分失效，改用按能量分辨的近邻流+基态加权作用得到14参数局域拟设，可在T=1/t下保持量子化泵浦且精度随体系尺寸不衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速Thouless泵浦中非绝热激发会破坏泵浦量子化，而精确CD项在多体与拓扑体系中通常是非局域、多体的，难以实现；作者希望刻画这一障碍，并寻找可行的局域近似，尤其在有相互作用且两种相互作用竞争稳定量子化泵浦的区间。

Method: 以Rice-Mele链（无自旋费米子，后加自旋、在位与近邻排斥）的Thouless泵浦为对象；先计算无相互作用情形的精确CD项并用Sels–Polkovnikov变分框架作近邻近似；再有相互作用情形下检验无限温度变分作用与两参数近邻项，并分析精确多体规范势的结构（其各跃迁振幅依赖于该跃迁所耗的相互作用能），据此按能量分辨近邻流、以基态加权作用确定系数，构造14参数局域拟设，在环长8与12、周期T=50/t、2/t、1/t下比较泵浦保真度。

Result: 无相互作用：精确CD非局域，但变分近邻近似精度良好。有相互作用：无限温度变分作用只惩罚CD振幅，两参数近邻项无法阻止周期从50/t缩至2/t时泵浦崩溃。按能量分辨近邻流并基态加权定系数的14参数局域拟设，使相互作用泵浦在T=2/t与T=1/t下保持量子化，且每格点精度在8与12格点环间不退化。

Conclusion: 多体CD的主要障碍在于精确规范势的相互作用能依赖性，而非振幅大小；能量分辨的近邻流结构是构造局域CD拟设的关键，可使相互作用Thouless泵浦在短周期下保持量子化并具尺寸稳健性。

Abstract: Counterdiabatic (CD) driving suppresses the excitations of fast protocols by adding an auxiliary Hamiltonian, but in many-body and topological systems the exact CD term is generally non-local and multi-body. We study this obstruction for Thouless pumping in the Rice-Mele model, first without and then with interactions. For noninteracting fermions, the exact CD term of the pump is non-local, but the variational framework of Sels and Polkovnikov yields accurate nearest-neighbor approximations. For the spinful Rice-Mele chain with onsite and nearest-neighbor repulsion, in the regime where the competition of the two interactions stabilizes quantized pumping, this approach fails: the infinite-temperature variational action only penalizes the CD amplitudes, and the resulting two-parameter nearest-neighbor term does not prevent the pump from breaking down when the cycle time is reduced from $50/t$ to $2/t$. We trace the failure to the structure of the exact many-body gauge potential, whose amplitude for each hop depends on the interaction energy that the hop costs. Resolving the nearest-neighbor currents by this energy, and fixing the coefficients with a ground-state-weighted action, gives a local ansatz with 14 coefficients that keeps the interacting pump quantized for cycle times $T=2/t$ and $T=1/t$, with an accuracy per site that does not degrade between rings of 8 and 12 sites.

</details>


### [88] [Supervised Learning with Quantum Hamiltonian Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26833)
*Riccardo Molteni,Minjun Jeon,Paolo Braccia,Abhinav Deshpande,Vedran Dunjko,Liang Jiang,Su-un Lee,Swarnadeep Majumder,Javier Robledo Moreno,Parthasarathy Suryanarayanan,Kunal Sharma*

TL;DR: 本文提出"量子哈密顿量嵌入"（quantum Hamiltonian embeddings）监督学习框架：将经典训练数据集编码为单个参数化有效哈密顿量，训练优化其参数，以近似基态定义分类规则，并在理论上证明梯度估计高效性、泛化保证及超越经典的可达性，同时在IBM Heron处理器上完成规模化实验演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算有望为机器学习带来计算优势，但现有通用量子机器学习模型在性能、可训练性和高效推理方面缺乏可证明保证，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 构建数据依赖的参数化哈密顿量：将经典数据集编码进单一有效哈密顿量，训练即优化该哈密顿量参数；用量子算法近似其基态后，推理可基于学习态的经典表示或量子表示进行。作者建立了高效的梯度估计方法，并给出泛化保证与基于标准密码学假设的经典不可达性证明，最后在IBM Heron处理器上用标准基准数据集进行规模化演示。

Result: 提出了高效梯度估计方法；证明了有利的泛化保证；在标准密码学假设下证明该框架可实现高效经典算法无法达到的假设函数类；并在IBM Heron量子处理器上以标准基准数据集完成规模化实验验证。

Conclusion: 哈密顿量嵌入构成量子机器学习的新框架，兼具理论保证与在量子硬件上规模化实验研究的可行路径。

Abstract: Machine learning is widely regarded as a promising application in which quantum computing could offer a computational advantage. However, general quantum machine learning models with provable guarantees on performance, trainability, and efficient inference remain limited. In this work, we introduce quantum Hamiltonian embeddings, a supervised learning framework in which a classical training dataset is encoded into a single effective, parameterized Hamiltonian. Training optimizes the parameters of this data-dependent Hamiltonian, whose approximate ground state defines the classification rule. Once the ground state has been approximated using a quantum algorithm, inference can be performed either classically using a classical representation of the learned state, or with a quantum representation of the state. We establish efficient methods for gradient estimation and prove favorable generalization guarantees. Furthermore, under standard cryptographic assumptions, we show that Hamiltonian embeddings can realize hypothesis functions beyond the reach of efficient classical algorithms. We then demonstrate the framework at scale on IBM Heron processors using a standard benchmark dataset. Together, these results establish Hamiltonian embeddings as a new framework for quantum machine learning, combining theoretical guarantees with a path for experimental investigations at scale on quantum hardware.

</details>


### [89] [Quantum algorithms for optimal control: large-scale finite-horizon linear-quadratic regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26843)
*Xiajie Huang,Cristina Álvarez Yuste,Nikita Guseynov,Nana Liu,Javier González-Conde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域连续时间线性二次调节（LQR）问题，提出基于状态-协状态最优性方程与多重打靶法的量子算法，证明其线性系统的条件数界与时间跨度无关，并利用量子线性求解器在系统规模与控制时长上达到多对数复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典求解LQR需解非线性矩阵Riccati方程，对大系统和长控制时域开销高昂；作者希望借助量子算法突破这一瓶颈，并能直接给出最优代价与最优控制输入的经典估计。

Method: 直接处理状态-协状态最优性方程，将LQR重构为多重打靶线性系统，把初末条件并入单一线性系统；在可稳定性与可检测性假设下证明该系统条件数与时域无关的界；结合稀疏矩阵块编码、初始态制备oracle与量子线性系统子程序求解，而不构造Riccati解算子。

Result: 在给定初态下返回最优代价与选定时刻最优控制输入的经典估计；查询与门复杂度对系统规模和控制时长呈多对数缩放（在稀疏性、相关范数与稳定性参数有界时），误差依赖为反线性至多对数因子；对带两个端点控制的阻尼质量-弹簧链族给出与规模和时长一致的界，数值实验支持分析。

Conclusion: 该工作表明量子线性系统方法可在不构造Riccati解算子的前提下高效求解有限时域LQR，条件数界与时间跨度无关是关键；复杂度优势依赖于稀疏块编码、有界范数与稳定性等假设，并通过阻尼质量-弹簧链的数值实验得到验证。

Abstract: We develop quantum algorithms for optimal control over a prescribed finite time interval (finite horizon), focusing on continuous-time linear-quadratic regulation (LQR). This foundational control problem often requires solving a nonlinear matrix Riccati equation, which can be costly for large systems and long control durations on conventional classical solvers. We instead work directly with the state-costate optimality equations, reformulating the LQR problem as a multiple-shooting linear system. Importantly, we prove a condition-number bound for the system that is independent of the horizon, under stabilizability and detectability assumptions. By incorporating the initial and final conditions into a single linear system, we can solve the subsequent linear system more efficiently with a quantum linear systems subroutine. For a given initial state, our quantum algorithms return classical estimates of the optimal cost and the optimal control inputs at a selected time, without constructing a Riccati solution operator. Given sparse-matrix block-encodings, an initial-state preparation oracle and a fixed number of control inputs, the query and gate complexities scale polylogarithmically with system size and control duration, provided that sparsity, relevant norms, and stability parameters are bounded. The dependence on the target error is inverse-linear up to logarithmic factors. We establish the required bounds uniformly in system size and duration for a family of damped mass-spring chains with two endpoint controls. Numerical experiments support the analysis.

</details>

Abstract: We report the cryogenic characterization of a 14-nm nMOS Forksheet transistor down to 10 mK. We demonstrate that the device retains transistor operation with improved characteristics at cryogenic temperatures, while also forming a gate-defined quantum dot that can be tuned to the single-electron regime. In the quantum-dot regime, we perform low-frequency charge-noise measurements across different charge occupations and observe an average charge-noise amplitude of $\langle S_0\rangle = 10\;μ\mathrm{eV}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ at 1 Hz, with a minimum measured value of $3.3\;μ\mathrm{eV}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$. These results highlight the potential of Forksheet technology for the monolithic cointegration of quantum and classical CMOS functionalities in future quantum processors.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
