Method: 将探针简化为闭合钝锥几何，表面离散为面板且尖端附近加密网格；采用修正Green函数计算面板间相互作用，并构建增广线性系统同时施加导体表面等势边界条件与整体电中性约束，求解感生表面电荷分布。

Result: BEM所得近场衰减与三维准静态COMSOL仿真结果一致，且所需网格单元少数个数量级，显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: BEM为系统研究THz近场局域对AFM探针几何的依赖提供了准确且高效的框架，可推广至更复杂、更接近实验的探针几何，为面向特定空间分辨率需求的探针选择与设计提供途径。

Abstract: Terahertz scanning near-field optical microscopy (THz-SNOM) enables spatially and spectrally resolved investigation of low-energy collective and topological states in quantum materials and nano-structured devices by exploiting the strongly localized near field generated by a polarized atomic force microscopy (AFM) tip. Because the THz near-field response is highly sensitive to tip geometry, understanding how geometric parameters control field confinement and decay is essential for selecting tip designs that provide the spatial resolution required for specific experiments. In this work, we develop a boundary element method (BEM) for modeling the electrostatic near-field of a conducting AFM tip in response to an external background field. The tip is represented by a simplified closed, blunt-cone geometry with its surface discretized into panels using increased mesh density near the apex. A modified Green's function is used to calculate interactions between surface panels, while an augmented linear system simultaneously enforces the equipotential boundary condition on the conducting surface and overall charge neutrality to determine the induced surface-charge distribution. We validate the BEM by comparing the resulting near-field decay with that obtained from a three-dimensional quasi-static COMSOL Multiphysics simulation. The BEM accurately reproduces the COMSOL field-decay behavior while requiring orders of magnitude fewer mesh elements and substantially reducing computational cost. These results demonstrate that the BEM provides an accurate and computationally efficient framework for systematically investigating the dependence of THz near-field confinement on AFM tip geometry. This framework can be extended to more complex and experimentally realistic tip geometries, providing a route toward rational tip selection and design for application-specific spatial-resolution requirements.

</details>

Result: 原始UOTe为无贝里曲率、无自旋劈裂能带的共线反铁磁；手性超晶格在其体内产生大贝里曲率并被非线性霍尔效应探测到。在150 K附近，手性超晶格诱导的贝里曲率与共线反铁磁序耦合，在T_N附近突现约0.14的巨反常霍尔角，在块体磁体中位居前列；自旋Hanle进动测量表明手性超晶格还能从共线反铁磁中产生自旋极化电流。

Conclusion: 手性超晶格为在共线反铁磁中实现自旋劈裂拓扑现象提供了新途径，可作为通过实空间工程调控量子几何的手性超晶格路径；基于离子尺寸与层间-层内能量竞争提出的键失配超晶格设计原则，可推广用于发现同类超晶格体系。

Abstract: The interplay between chirality and magnetism is a profound topic, spanning particle physics, condensed matter, chemistry and origin of life. In this paper, we study this interplay in the new antiferromagnet (AFM) UOTe, and we report a chiral superlattice route to spin-split topological phenomena from collinear antiferromagnetism. Pristine UOTe is a collinear AFM without Berry curvature or spin-split bands. However, we identify the spontaneous formation of a chiral superlattice in UOTe, which can be viewed as frozen chiral phonons at finite wave vector. When electrons move through the chiral superlattice, their orbital Bloch wavefunction and quantum geometry are modulated by the strong chiral superlattice potential, generating large Berry curvature in the chiral superlattice, which we detect by the nonlinear Hall effect. At 150 K, the chiral superlattice induced Berry curvature couples to the collinear AFM order, generating a giant anomalous Hall angle ~0.14 abruptly near $T_N$ that is among the largest in bulk magnets. Moreover, our spin Hanle precession measurement shows that the chiral superlattice also generates spin-polarized current from the collinear AFM, a long-standing goal in spintronics. We shed light on the supermodulation formation mechanism based on chemical ion size and physical interlayer-intralayer energy competitions, using which we propose a design principle to discover similar bond-mismatch superlattices, providing a chiral superlattice pathway through real space engineering of quantum geometry.

</details>

*Marc Thelen,Julia Brandt,Stella Gries,Manuel Brinker,Alexander Petrov,Patrick Huber*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出多孔硅可作为对水具有类生物精密操控能力的工程材料，并通过孔隙层级化、表面改性和限域水三个方向拓展其响应功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物体系能精准控制水以输运物质、传递能量和驱动化学反应，而工程材料难以实现同等水平的水控制；多孔硅兼具可精密加工的孔隙结构、可调表面化学与半导体骨架，且原料丰富，有望填补这一空白。

Method: 以观点（perspective）形式，梳理多孔硅中水的多种效应（纳米孔内连续流、毛细与电吸附应力致骨架应变、结构色移动、透明度切换、电信号调制与产生），并提出三条发展路径：构建跨尺度孔隙层级以兼顾界面面积与传输速度并扩展至三维构件；将表面修饰作为可局域设定、可切换的调控变量；利用仅数分子宽的孔道改变水本身性质。

Result: 孔隙层级化打破了界面面积与传输速度的经典权衡，使功能从薄平面膜扩展至三维部件；表面改性可按需、局域地调节水—孔壁相互作用；极窄孔道中缺乏体相水，水可呈介电各向异性并具高导电性。

Conclusion: 上述进展使多孔硅从响应性材料转变为由水驱动的自适应材料，为水基工程系统提供可设计平台。

Abstract: Living organisms control water to transport matter, transfer energy and drive chemical reactions. In this perspective, we argue that porous silicon can give engineering materials comparable control over water. Its pore architecture, surface chemistry and semiconducting skeleton can be shaped across length scales with the precision of semiconductor processing and, like the water it can host, it relies on one of the most abundant chemical elements on Earth. Liquids in porous silicon, most often water, can flow as a continuum through pores a few nanometers wide, strain the skeleton through capillary and electrosorption stresses, shift structural color, switch transparency, and modulate or generate electrical signals. We describe three directions to combine and extend these effects and overcome their limitations. Pore hierarchy resolves the classical trade-off between interface area and transport speed by combining large and small pores and thereby allows functions to be scaled from thin planar films to three-dimensional components. Surface modification becomes a design variable that tunes the interaction between water and the pore wall. It can be set locally and, with switchable coatings, on demand. Finally, pores only a few molecules wide alter the properties of water itself. In the absence of a bulk-like volume, water can become dielectrically anisotropic and highly conductive. Altogether, these steps turn porous silicon from a responsive material into an adaptive, water-driven material.

</details>

TL;DR: 该文提出在标准拓扑不变量之外,用刻画动量空间哈密顿量映射自身几何的不变量来"细化"拓扑分类;以无序一维手性SSH模型为例,发现平面Bloch环除绕数外还具有切向量缠绕定义的整数"转身数",并存在环仍保持有限且谱有隙时的转身数相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑表征基于非相互作用、干净极限下的标准不变量(如绕数),难以刻画映射本身的几何结构;作者希望超越常规分类,寻找由零频单粒子格林函数导出的动量空间哈密顿量映射几何所提供的新拓扑结构。

Method: 由零频单粒子格林函数构造拓扑哈密顿量,得到平面Bloch环;在常规绕数之外引入描述其切向量缠绕的整数转身数(turning number);对含无序的一维手性Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型进行数值计算,并结合渐近分析预测转身数相变的出现及其无序依赖。

Result: 识别出仅由Bloch环切向量消失所驱动、而拓扑哈密顿量仍保持有限且谱有隙的转身数相变;得到在绕数分类之外还包含额外拓扑结构的扩展相图;渐近分析对相应相变区域的出现及其无序依赖的预测与数值结果一致。

Conclusion: 动量空间哈密顿量映射的几何可作为常规拓扑分类之外的额外拓扑结构来源,转身数等几何不变量为无序量子体系的精细化拓扑刻画提供了新途径。

Abstract: The geometry of momentum-space Hamiltonian maps derived from the zero-frequency single-particle Green's function provides a route to a refined topological characterization of quantum systems beyond the noninteracting clean limit. The central idea is to complement standard topological invariants by invariants that characterize the geometry of the map itself. As a paradigmatic example, we study the one-dimensional chiral Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model with disorder, where the topological Hamiltonian defines a planar Bloch loop. In addition to its conventional winding number, this loop possesses an integer turning number that characterizes the winding of its tangent vector. We identify turning-number transitions that occur while the topological Hamiltonian remains finite and spectrally gapped, driven solely by a vanishing tangent vector of the Bloch loop, and obtain an extended phase diagram with additional topological structure beyond the winding-number classification. Asymptotic analysis predicts the emergence and disorder dependence of the corresponding transition regime, in agreement with the numerical results. Our findings establish the geometry of momentum-space Hamiltonian maps as an additional source of topological structure beyond conventional classifications.

</details>


### [95] [Topological Splitting of Sondheimer Oscillations in Thin-Film Weyl Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24967)
*Toshihito Osada*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将Sondheimer磁阻振荡与拓扑能带效应结合，研究Berry曲率与轨道磁矩对薄膜Weyl半金属Sondheimer振荡的修正，发现振荡劈裂为两支并出现频率随磁场变化的"啁啾"特征，源于轨道-Zeeman效应引起的费米面形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sondheimer振荡源于薄膜厚度与回旋轨道螺距的匹配，但现有讨论未考虑非平庸拓扑（Berry曲率、轨道磁矩）的影响；受轨道-Zeeman效应修正的费米面是否会改变该振荡行为尚不清楚。

Method: 基于Boltzmann输运理论，在薄膜Weyl半金属中引入Berry曲率和轨道磁矩对半经典动力学（包括轨道-Zeeman能量修正所致费米面形变）的作用，计算磁阻随磁场的振荡行为，并推广至Dirac半金属。

Result: Sondheimer振荡劈裂为两支具有不同频率的分支；每一分支的频率随磁场变化，即振荡呈啁啾（chirped）特征。二者均源自轨道-Zeeman效应导致的费米面形变。相同机制亦适用于Dirac半金属。

Conclusion: 拓扑能带结构会显著改质Sondheimer振荡的频谱结构，劈裂与频率啁啾可作为探测Weyl/Dirac半金属轨道磁矩效应与费米面形变的输运手段。

Abstract: We discuss topological effects on Sondheimer oscillations in the magnetoresistance of thin-film Weyl semimetals. The Sondheimer oscillations are periodic in magnetic field and originate from the commensurability between the film thickness and the cyclotron-orbit pitch. Using Boltzmann transport theory, we investigate the effects of Berry curvature and orbital magnetic moment on the oscillations. We find that the Sondheimer oscillations split into two branches with different frequencies and that each branch becomes chirped because its frequency depends on magnetic field. These features originate from the orbital-Zeeman-induced deformation of the Fermi surface. The same mechanism also applies to Dirac semimetals.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bi₂Te₃表面Dirac态具有自旋-动量锁定（切向Rashba型自旋纹理）。将薄板分为两半并修饰层间界面（扭转、平移、改变层间距）有望调控Dirac态性质，尤其扭转自由度值得深入探究。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，沿两条路径研究：其一，考察不同堆垛构型，提取小扭转角Moiré结构的相关参数；其二，直接计算公度扭转超胞的性质。

Result: 仅在部分堆垛下存在非平庸拓扑，从而在Moiré尺度上形成拓扑畴；公度扭转超胞不仅呈现可由外压调节的平带，还出现具有非常规自旋-动量锁定的径向自旋纹理。

Conclusion: 界面扭转与堆垛为调控Bi₂Te₃拓扑Dirac态提供有效自由度，可产生拓扑畴、压力可调平带及径向自旋纹理等新奇物态。

Abstract: The three-dimensional topological insulator Bi$_2$Te$_3$ hosts topological Dirac states on its two-dimensional surfaces. These Dirac states show spin-momentum locking, which usually implies a tangential Rashba-like spin texture. One can alter the properties of the Dirac states by dividing a slab of such a material into two thinner halves, modifying the interface by twisting, shifting, and changing the interlayer distance. Using first-principles calculations, we investigate such modified slabs of Bi$_2$Te$_3$. The twisting degree of freedom deserves special attention. To this end, we follow two approaches. On the one hand, we probe different stacking configurations in order to extract parameters relevant for small twist angle Moiré structures. We find non-trivial topology only for some stackings, leading to topological domains on the Moiré scale. On the other hand, we calculate the properties of commensurate twisted supercells. The latter reveals not only flat bands, which are tunable by external pressure, but also radial spin textures with unconventional spin-momentum locking.

</details>


### [97] [Activating Nonstandard Axion Electrodynamics in Topological Insulator Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25452)
*Miklós Horváth,Long Liang,Panagiotis Kotetes*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文指出，拓扑绝缘体中的磁电响应并非必须源于非平庸能带拓扑；通过同时施加电场和磁场破缺宇称与时间反演对称性，可在Bi、Sb基拓扑绝缘体中激活各类张量型磁电耦合，实现“非标准轴子电动力学”，并以非互易方向二色性作为可探测的磁光特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轴子响应被视为拓扑绝缘体的本征、各向同性赝标量磁电耦合，并被归因于能带拓扑。作者希望厘清：这类（及更一般的）磁电响应是否只能来自非平庸拓扑，还是可由对称性破缺在已有材料中产生，从而拓展轴子响应的物理图景。

Method: 采用成熟的、基于第一性原理的参数化有效模型描述Bi基与Sb基拓扑绝缘体；通过外加电场与磁场的组合破缺宇称与时间反演对称性，系统激活并计算各向异性/张量型磁电耦合系数，据此给出可检验的实验预言。

Result: 在无需非平庸拓扑的前提下，对称性破缺可诱导多种张量型磁电耦合，得到非标准的轴子电动力学；基于有效模型估算了相应磁电系数，并识别出非互易方向二色性（nonreciprocal directional dichroism）作为该类效应的特征磁光信号。

Conclusion: 磁电/轴子响应可由对称性破缺而非仅由拓扑产生，从而超越传统拓扑轴子图像；这为在拓扑绝缘体中工程化并探测对称性诱导的磁电现象提供了可行途径，并给出以非互易方向二色性为核心的实验方案。

Abstract: The conventional axion response of topological insulators (TIs) is characterized by an isotropic pseudoscalar magnetoelectric (ME) coupling rooted in band topology: the condensed matter analog of the axion field. However, as we reveal here, this and other more general ME responses can arise in existing TI materials from symmetry breaking without requiring nontrivial topology. Indeed, we show that breaking parity and time-reversal symmetries using combined electric and magnetic fields unlocks nonstandard axion electrodynamics via the controllable activation of various tensorial ME couplings, thus, going beyond the standard axion picture. Using a well-established first-principles-informed effective model for Bi-and Sb-based TI materials, we estimate the resulting ME coefficients and make concrete predictions for experiments. In particular, we identify nonreciprocal directional dichroism as a distinctive magneto-optical signature of these ME effects, therefore opening a route to engineer and probe symmetry-induced ME phenomena beyond the conventional topological response.

</details>


### [98] [Chiral symmetry and the orbital susceptibility of disordered honeycomb flakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25555)
*B. Murzaliev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 无序蜂窝薄片的轨道磁化率由“能隙近乎闭合”的稀有样本主导，手征对称性使化学势恒居于最小能隙中央，因此当无序能闭合该能隙且磁场仍能耦合两能级时，平均轨道磁化率在低温下按 ln(1/T) 增长；该增长是否发生取决于跃迁几何（最近邻保持能隙打开，第三近邻允许闭合），手征随机矩阵模型中对数系数趋近于 1，化学势失谐会截断该对数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小尺度无序颗粒的轨道磁化率主要由占据态与空态近乎简并的稀有样本贡献。在混沌颗粒中，能级排斥抑制近简并，且近简并还须与化学势对齐，故平均磁化率保持有限。作者希望利用蜂窝晶格的中性薄片中手征（子晶格）对称性消除“须与化学势对齐”这一条件，探讨由此可能产生的低温磁化率发散行为及其受跃迁几何约束的物理机制。

Method: 结合手征对称性论证与具体晶格模型分析：研究无序能否闭合最小能隙、同时磁场能否混合两能级；对比紧凑六边形薄片上正最近邻跃迁与第三近邻跃迁对能隙闭合的不同影响；并构建含 n 个能级对的手征随机矩阵模型，推导对数修正系数。

Result: 手征对称性使化学势在每个无序实现中都位于最小能隙中央。当无序可闭合该能隙且磁场混合两能级时，平均轨道磁化率在低温 T 下按 ln(1/T) 增长；跃迁几何决定能否发生：正最近邻跃迁维持能隙打开，第三近邻跃迁则允许能隙闭合。手征随机矩阵模型中，n 个能级对对应的对数系数为 C_n = √(2/n)·Γ[(n+1)/2]/Γ(n/2)，数值接近 1；化学势失谐会截断该对数增长。

Conclusion: 手征（子晶格）对称性可将轨道磁化率的稀有近简并贡献从“有限”转化为低温对数增长，但其出现需同时满足无序闭合能隙与磁场耦合两能级两个条件，因而敏感依赖于跃迁几何；对数的普适系数接近整数并由能级对数决定，化学势失谐则提供截断尺度。

Abstract: The orbital susceptibility of a small disordered grain is dominated by rare samples in which an occupied and an empty level nearly touch. In a chaotic grain such near-degeneracies are suppressed by level repulsion and must also coincide with the chemical potential, so the mean susceptibility stays finite. In a neutral honeycomb flake, chiral (sublattice) symmetry removes the second condition: It places the chemical potential in the middle of the smallest gap, in every disorder realization. Here we show that the mean orbital susceptibility then grows as $\ln(1/T)$ at low temperature $T$ if disorder can close this gap while a magnetic field still mixes the two levels, and that the hopping geometry decides whether it can: Positive nearest-neighbor hopping on a compact hexagonal flake keeps the gap open, third-neighbor hopping allows it to close. In a chiral random-matrix model with $n$ level pairs the coefficient of the logarithm is $C_n=\sqrt{2/n}\,Γ[(n+1)/2]/Γ(n/2)$, close to unity, and a detuned chemical potential cuts the logarithm off.

</details>


### [99] [Engineering topological phenomena via field-driven Weyl motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25609)
*Jeonghoon Shin,Taekoo Oh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 以烧绿石铱氧化物在[111]磁场下为例，通过Weyl节点在动量空间中的移动来展示场驱动的反常霍尔调控，并用多带Kubo公式验证宏观AHC变化本质由Weyl节点运动驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究磁场驱动的动量空间磁单极（Weyl节点）动力学如何决定强关联拓扑材料中的输运性质，特别是反常霍尔电导的场依赖行为及其与实验的对应关系。

Method: 采用成熟微观哈密顿量建模体系，以自旋从all-in-all-out态到铁磁态的旋转刻画磁场效应；追踪自旋旋转过程中Weyl节点的位置，并将完整多带Kubo公式计算结果与Weyl节点几何分离所给出的近似进行对比。

Result: 宏观反常霍尔电导的变化从根本上由Weyl节点运动驱动；仅在临界自旋旋转角附近、多个Weyl节点彼此接近时两者出现偏差。磁场下AHC的演化在数量级和非单调性上与已报道实验一致。

Conclusion: 结果阐明了磁场驱动的动量空间磁单极动力学，为理解强关联拓扑材料中的输运提供了框架。

Abstract: We illustrate the field-driven anomalous Hall engineering via the momentum-space motion of Weyl nodes using pyrochlore iridates under a [111] magnetic field as an example. We model the system with a well-established microscopic Hamiltonian and the field effect with the spin rotation from the all-in-all-out to the ferromagnetic state. Tracking the position of Weyl nodes during the spin rotation, we demonstrate macroscopic anomalous Hall conductivity variations are fundamentally driven by Weyl node motions by comparing the full multi-band Kubo formula with the geometric Weyl node separation. The discrepancy occurs only near a critical spin rotation angle, where several Weyl nodes come into close proximity. We reveal that the evolution of anomalous Hall conductivity under the magnetic field agrees with reported experiments in an order-of-magnitude and non-monotonicity. These results elucidate the field-driven dynamics of momentum-space monopoles and provide a framework for understanding transport in strongly correlated topological materials.

</details>


### [100] [Phase engineering of odd parity state in a quantum dot Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26015)
*Shohei Kobayashi,Tyler Lindemann,Sergei Gronin,Geoffrey Gardner,Peter Stano,Daniel Loss,Michael Manfra,Seigo Tarucha,Sadashige Matsuo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究强耦合量子点约瑟夫森结中由超导相位差 φ 诱导的基态费米子宇称转变，观测到 Andreev 束缚态能量在 φ≈π 附近过零，且与单能级近邻化量子点模型吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点约瑟夫森结中的费米子宇称调控可解锁新奇超导物理，但通常依赖电学栅压调控；作者希望探索以超导相位差作为调控自由度实现宇称转变。

Method: 在强耦合 QDJJ 中扫描量子点两端的超导相位差 φ，测量 Andreev 束缚态（ABS）能谱，以 ABS 能量过零作为宇称跃迁的判据，并与单能级量子点被超导体近邻化的简化模型进行对比。

Result: 发现相位 φ 可控制基态宇称，宇称跃迁表现为 ABS 能量过零，且过零发生在 φ≈π 附近；实验结果与单能级近邻化量子点模型定量吻合良好。

Conclusion: 相位可作为一种有效调控手段实现 QDJJ 宇称切换，相位控制的量子点约瑟夫森结阵列有望成为研究 Majorana 零模等奇异现象的平台。

Abstract: Controlling fermion parity in quantum-dot Josephson junctions (QDJJs) enables access to novel superconducting physics. While parity transitions are usually tuned electrically, we study phase-induced transitions in a strongly coupled QDJJ using the superconducting phase difference across the dot terminals, denoted by $φ$. We find that this phase allows control over the ground state parity, with parity transitions observed as the Andreev bound states (ABSs) energies crossing zero. These crossings occur near $φ= π$, and their behavior is in good correspondence to a simple model considering a single-level quantum dot proximitized by a superconductor. These results place phase-controlled QDJJ arrays as a promising platform for exploring exotic phenomena such as Majorana zero modes.

</details>


### [101] [Bicircular light tunable topological phases and nonlinear Hall responses in odd-parity magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26163)
*Md Afsar Reja,Awadhesh Narayan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用双圆偏振光驱动奇宇称磁体，可实现一系列光致拓扑相变，并伴随量子几何量的突变，从而打开并调控由贝里曲率与量子度规贡献的非线性霍尔响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇波磁体（odd-wave magnets）的动量依赖自旋劈裂呈奇宇称形式，为非常规磁性与拓扑现象提供新平台；但如何对其拓扑相与非线性响应进行主动、可控调控尚不明确，本文旨在探索双圆偏振光作为调控手段的可行性。

Method: 以奇宇称（奇波）磁体为研究对象，施加双圆偏振光（bicircular light）驱动，通过改变两束光的相对振幅比调控体系；计算能带拓扑不变量与量子几何量（量子度规、贝里曲率），并推导非线性霍尔响应的选择定则。

Result: 随双圆偏振光相对振幅比变化，体系出现级联式拓扑相变；相变处量子度规发生剧烈突变；双圆偏振光驱动在辐照奇波磁体中解锁源自贝里曲率与量子度规的非线性霍尔响应，并给出相应的选择定则。

Conclusion: 奇宇称磁体的拓扑性质与非线性响应均可由双圆偏振光驱动直接调控，双圆偏振光为奇波磁体的拓扑与量子几何工程提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Altermagnets are an emerging class of magnetic materials that exhibit characteristic features of both ferromagnets and antiferromagnets. Odd-wave magnets, where the momentum-dependent spin splitting follows an odd-parity form factor, provide a distinct platform for exploring unconventional magnetic and topological phenomena. In this work, we demonstrate light-induced topological phase transitions in odd-parity magnets driven by bicircular light. We show that a cascade of topological phase transitions occur as the relative amplitude ratio of the two beams is varied. These are accompanied by sharp changes of the quantum metric. We demonstrate that bicircular light unlocks nonlinear Hall responses arising from Berry curvature and quantum metric in irradiated odd-wave magnets, and formulate the underlying selection rules. Our results show that both the topology and the nonlinear responses in odd-parity magnets can be directly controlled by a bicircular drive.

</details>


### [102] [Spatiotemporal Imaging of Thermally Driven Metal-Insulator Phase Domain Propagation in VO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26367)
*Ilaria Delbono,Raouf Amara,Matias Feldman,Hankun Yang,Cédric Leau,Carlo Scian,Erwan Dandeu,Sébastien Royer,Debora Pierucci,Emmanuel Lhuillier,Franck Vidal,Tiziana Cesca,Hilton B. de Aguiar,Jose Ordonez-Miranda,Danièle Fournier,Raj Pandya,Boris Kalinic,Giovanni Mattei,James K. Utterback*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文结合频闪光学散射显微与有限元模拟，研究了VO2薄膜中绝缘体-金属相界的热驱动非线性运动，揭示相界传播由局域扩散热输运支配、潜热起热惯量作用，且介观相前速度可超过热扩散预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相界（尤其固-固转变）的性质与动力学难以细致刻画，而这类转变是相变材料的核心，需要介观尺度上对相界时空演化的直接观测与定量建模。

Method: 采用频闪（stroboscopic）光学散射显微术观测VO2薄膜中衍射极限光致金属相畴的横向时空演化（外扩、收缩与消失），并以有限元非线性热输运模拟与之对比；通过激发通量、膜厚与衬底热导率调控相界动力学。

Result: 数据与无自由参数的非线性热输运模拟定量吻合，表明该区域相界输运为局域扩散；量化了潜热作为热惯量抑制输运、延长相共存；发现尽管局域由傅里叶定律热梯度驱动，介观相前传播速度却快于热扩散预期，源于温度场演化与相界等温线运动之间的区别。

Conclusion: 建立了热耗散与介观相前运动之间的联系，为控制VO2光子系统相关的相变动力学提供框架，并预期该方法可推广至一类广泛的一阶与二阶相变。

Abstract: Achieving a detailed picture of the nature and dynamics of phase boundaries remains a major challenge, particularly solid-solid transitions central to phase-change materials. Here, we use stroboscopic optical scattering microscopy and finite-element simulations to study the nonlinear thermally driven motion of the mesoscale insulator-metal phase boundary in VO2 thin films. We visualize the lateral spatiotemporal evolution of a diffraction-limited photoinduced metallic phase domain, resolving its outward propagation, contraction, and ultimate disappearance. The phase boundary dynamics are governed by heat flow, which can be controlled by excitation fluence, film thickness, and substrate thermal conductivity. Parameter-free agreement between the data and nonlinear thermal transport simulations identify the phase boundary transport as locally diffusive under this regime. We quantify how the latent heat acts as a thermal inertia, suppressing transport and prolonging the phase coexistence. Paradoxically, despite being driven by the underlying thermal gradient locally described by Fourier's law, the mesoscale phase front propagates faster than expected from thermal diffusion. This phenomenon arises from the distinction between temperature-field evolution and phase-boundary isotherm motion. These results establish a link between heat dissipation and mesoscale phase-front motion, providing a framework for controlling phase-transition kinetics relevant to photonics applications of VO2. We anticipate that this approach generalizes to a broad class of first- and second-order transitions.

</details>


### [103] [Probing the physical origins of 1/f noise by nonlinear transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26592)
*Nicolas Fontaine,Bertrand Reulet*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文测量了直流/交流偏置隧道结非线性电子输运中的低频噪声，发现不仅电阻、非线性系数也呈1/f型噪声且彼此存在关联，并用势垒高度涨落（两侧近乎完美反相关）解释实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有噪声研究多集中于线性区的电阻涨落，对非线性输运区（非线性系数）是否也具1/f噪声、以及各输运量涨落之间是否存在关联缺乏实验检验，也缺少对涨落微观来源（如势垒高度）的直接判定。

Method: 对直流与交流偏置隧道结进行低频噪声测量，提取电阻与非线性系数的噪声及其互相关；基于隧道结电子输运的常用理论模型，将测量的涨落映射为势垒高度涨落。

Result: 电阻与非线性系数均表现出1/f型噪声，且各量之间存在可测的关联；分析表明结两侧的势垒高度存在强烈涨落，且两侧涨落几乎完全反相关。

Conclusion: 非线性输运系数可作为表征隧道结低频噪声的新探针，交叉相关分析能有效定位涨落的微观来源，并揭示两侧势垒高度反相关的约束机制。

Abstract: We report measurements of low frequency noise in the nonlinear electron transport of tunnel junctions that are dc or ac biased. We show that not only the resistance but also the nonlinear coefficients exhibit $1/f$-like noise, and that correlations among them exist. Using the common theoretical model for electron transport in tunnel junctions, we relate our measurements to fluctuations of barrier heights. We show in particular that the barrier heights on either side of the junction have strong fluctuations that are almost perfectly anti-correlated.

</details>


### [104] [Strain-induced disorder from Schottky gates on high mobility 2DEGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26607)
*Sambit Mohapatra,Lukas Berg,Jean-Baptiste Cuvellier,Yan Sun,Abdelhanin Aassime,Alan Durnez,Antonella Cavanna,Dominique Mailly,Ulf Gennser*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 金属肖特基栅通过应变效应降低GaAs/AlGaAs二维电子气迁移率并抑制分数量子霍尔态；无序颗粒状金属膜等效于超晶格，产生穿透至2DEG的应变场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 栅控2DEG是研究关联现象（尤其分数量子霍尔效应）的关键手段，但常用金属肖特基栅会降低电子迁移率、抑制分数量子霍尔态，其机制此前不明确。

Method: 在特殊设计的纳米结构超晶格栅下测量共度量振荡（commensurability oscillations），以分辨应变效应，并与COMSOL Multiphysics仿真对比；另构建简化模型分析无序颗粒金属膜的作用。

Result: 实验结果表明迁移率下降源于应变效应，与COMSOL仿真吻合良好；规则无图案栅中金属膜的无序颗粒特性可等效为超晶格，产生向下渗透至2DEG的应变场。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了栅致应变无序的物理来源，有助于理解、改善并探索抑制高迁移率2DEG中应变诱导无序的新方法。

Abstract: Electrostatic gates on two-dimensional electron gases (2DEGs) in GaAs/AlGaAs heterostructures are essential for numerous studies of correlation driven phenomena, many performed within the fractional quantum Hall effect. However, the most commonly used type of gates, the metallic Schottky gate, has the propensity to adversely impact the electron mobility and suppress the fractional quantum Hall states. Here we demonstrate through commensurability oscillations under specially designed nanostructured superlattice gates, that strain effects are responsible for the mobility reduction. The experimental results agree well with COMSOL Multiphysics simulations. In a regular, unpatterned gate the disordered, granular nature of the metal film fills the same role as the superlattice in creating a strain field penetrating down to the 2DEG, as we show with a simplified model. These results will help in understanding, improving and finding novel methods to reduce strain-induced disorder in gated high-mobility 2DEGs.

</details>


### [105] [Proximity Transient Absorption in van der Waals Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26715)
*Neema Rafizadeh,Ting Zheng,Hui Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并演示了一种"邻近瞬态吸收光谱"(PTA)：探测的光学跃迁与被监测的光激发载流子分别位于不同材料中，利用石墨烯/hBN/WS₂异质结构证明石墨烯中的载流子可通过库仑屏蔽在电学隔离的WS₂单层激子共振处产生强瞬态信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统瞬态吸收光谱通常将瞬态光学信号归因于承载被探测光学跃迁的材料内部激发。作者希望突破这一"信号与被探测载流子同源"的隐含假设，探索载流子与光学跃迁在空间上分离时是否仍能产生可用的瞬态信号，从而拓展瞬态吸收光谱的适用范围。

Method: 构建石墨烯/hBN/WS₂范德华异质结构，用原子级薄hBN间隔层使石墨烯与WS₂电学隔离；以WS₂单层激子共振为光学探测通道，监测石墨烯中光激发载流子通过库仑屏蔽引起的瞬态光学响应，并系统改变hBN厚度以验证信号的邻近来源。

Result: 石墨烯中的光激发载流子通过库仑屏蔽在WS₂激子共振处诱导出强瞬态光学响应；该信号随石墨烯载流子动力学演化而非WS₂激子动力学，表明WS₂激子充当即邻材料电子过程的光学换能器。信号随hBN厚度增加而系统性减弱，在间隔接近2 nm时仍保持可观幅度，确认了信号的邻近起源。

Conclusion: PTA提供了一种非接触式光学调控手段——用一种材料中的光激发载流子调制另一种材料，并可用于探测难以直接研究的材料中的载流子动力学。更广泛地，PTA确立了超快光谱的新模态，并揭示范德华异质结构中的瞬态光学信号可能来源于空间分离层中的激发。

Abstract: Transient absorption spectroscopy is a widely used technique for investigating ultrafast electronic dynamics, where transient optical signals are generally interpreted as arising from excitations within the material hosting the probed optical transition. A distinct form of transient absorption spectroscopy, termed proximity transient absorption (PTA), is demonstrated, in which the probed optical transition and the monitored photoexcited carriers reside in different materials. Using graphene/hBN/WS$_2$ heterostructures, photoexcited carriers in graphene induce a strong transient optical response at the excitonic resonance of a WS$_2$ monolayer through Coulomb screening, even when the two layers remain electronically isolated by an atomically thin hBN spacer. The resulting signal follows the carrier dynamics of graphene rather than those of WS$_2$, demonstrating that the WS$_2$ exciton acts as an optical transducer of electronic processes in a neighboring material. The signal decreases systematically with increasing hBN thickness, confirming its proximity origin while remaining substantial for separations approaching 2 nm. PTA provides a noncontact approach for optically modulating one material using photoexcited carriers in another and enables probing of carrier dynamics in materials that are otherwise difficult to investigate directly. More broadly, PTA establishes a new modality of ultrafast spectroscopy and reveals that transient optical signals in van der Waals heterostructures can originate from excitations residing in spatially separated layers.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum-interference correction to conductivity in disordered electronic bands with non-uniform quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22500)
*Yip Chun Wong,Asimpunya Mitra,Yong Baek Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出非均匀量子几何可定性改变直流输运的量子干涉修正：连续调节量子几何可使电导修正变号（局域化↔反局域化），且在特定参数点即使微观时间反演对称性破缺，弱局域化/弱反局域化仍可再生，其机制是布洛赫态轨道结构诱导的涌现反幺正对称性保护无能隙Cooperon模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弱局域化源于时间反演散射路径的相长干涉，通常一旦时间反演对称性（TRS）破缺即被抑制。作者希望考察在多轨道电子体系中，非均匀量子几何是否能成为调控量子干涉与电子局域化的新自由度，并以自旋/谷极化的菱面体N层石墨烯（RNG）为动机。

Method: 采用能带投影（band-projected）框架，以自旋-谷极化RNG为motivation，构建包含非均匀量子几何（布洛赫态的量子度规等几何量）的有效理论，计算直流电导的量子干涉（Cooperon）修正，并分析其随量子几何调控参数的符号演化及对称性。

Result: 连续调节量子几何可改变电导修正的符号，实现局域化与反局域化倾向之间的crossover；在控制量子几何的特殊参数值处，即使微观TRS已破缺，WL或WAL仍可重新出现。原因是这些特殊点上布洛赫态的轨道结构产生涌现反幺正对称性，保护无能隙Cooperon模，从而恢复WL/WAL特征的对数发散量子干涉修正。

Conclusion: 非均匀量子几何可作为多轨道电子体系中控制量子干涉与电子局域化的机制，并为在TRS破缺条件下实现和调控WL/WAL提供新途径。

Abstract: Weak localization in two-dimensional disordered electron systems originates from constructive interference between time-reversed scattering paths, giving rise to a logarithmically divergent quantum-interference correction to the dc conductivity. This behavior is generally suppressed when time-reversal symmetry (TRS) is broken. In this study, using a band-projected framework motivated by spin- and valley-polarized rhombohedral $N$-layer graphene (RNG), we show that nonuniform quantum geometry can qualitatively modify quantum-interference corrections to dc transport. In particular, continuously tuning the quantum geometry can change the sign of the conductivity correction, corresponding to a crossover between localization and antilocalization tendencies. Remarkably, at special values of the parameter controlling the quantum geometry, weak-localization (WL) or weak-antilocalization (WAL) can re-emerge despite broken microscopic TRS. At these special points, the orbital structure of the Bloch states gives rise to an emergent antiunitary symmetry that protects a gapless Cooperon mode and restores the logarithmically divergent quantum-interference correction characteristic of WL or WAL. Our results establish nonuniform quantum geometry as a mechanism for controlling quantum interference and electron localization in multiorbital electronic systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Encoding Hyperuniformity in Geometric Networks: Structural Signatures and Diffusion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24317)
*Yongyi Wang,Eli Newby,Yang Jiao,Salvatore Torquato,Réka Albert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 几何网络比Delaunay三角剖分更能保留底层点构型的长波密度信息，超均匀性表现为多种网络结构观测量（接近中心性、顶点强度、特征向量中心性）的顶点间涨落被抑制，并可用基于扩散的弛豫时间中心性在动力学上加以印证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序超均匀性是一种长波密度涨落被异常抑制的新奇态，但其向网络系统的推广仍是重要开放问题；同时网络超均匀性中"近超均匀构型"与"强局部无序的超均匀系统"难以区分。

Method: 在广泛系统中比较几何网络与Delaunay三角剖分对底层点构型长波密度信息的保留程度；以接近中心性、顶点强度、特征向量中心性等网络结构观测量刻画顶点间涨落；并引入基于扩散的弛豫时间中心性，将结构特征与网络动力学联系起来。

Result: 几何网络显著保留更多长波密度信息；超均匀性表现为上述结构观测量的顶点间涨落被抑制，这些指标既能区分超均匀与非超均匀网络，也能区分近超均匀构型与强局部无序的超均匀系统；超均匀几何网络呈现更均匀的弛豫行为。

Conclusion: 大尺度密度涨落的抑制同时体现在网络结构与扩散动力学中，为网络超均匀性提供了结构与动力学层面的判别signature。

Abstract: Disordered hyperuniformity is a recently discovered exotic state exhibiting anomalously suppressed density fluctuations at long wavelengths, and its generalization to network systems remains an important open question. Here, we show that, across a wide range of systems, geometric networks retain substantially more long-wavelength density information from the underlying point configurations than Delaunay triangulations. Within geometric networks, hyperuniformity manifests as suppressed vertex-to-vertex fluctuations in a range of network structural observables, including closeness centrality, vertex strength, and eigenvector centrality. These measures not only distinguish hyperuniform networks from non-hyperuniform ones, but also separate near-hyperuniform configurations from hyperuniform systems with strong local disorder, solving a central challenge in network hyperuniformity. We connect these structural signatures to network dynamics by introducing a relaxation-time centrality based on diffusion and demonstrate that hyperuniform geometric networks exhibit more homogeneous relaxation behavior than non-hyperuniform geometric networks. These results reveal how the suppression of large-scale density fluctuations manifests in both network structure and diffusion dynamics.

</details>


### [108] [Maximum-Temperature Theorem for a Nanothermometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24340)
*Taras Plakhotnik*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文推导了一个仅依赖总热功率、热导率下界与温度计体积的准稳态温升上界，证明传感器定位与间距不确定性无法解释开尔文级读数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想点热源在传统热传导理论中稳态温度发散，但实际温度计测量的是有限体积内的平均值，因此需要给出对传感器实际可测温升的严格、与形状无关的界限，以判断纳米尺度实验中开尔文级温升是否可由稳态热传导解释。

Method: 在等温参考面所包围区域内任意位置放置体积为 V_s 的传感器，推导其准稳态温升的尖锐上界；该界只使用区域内总生热功率 Q̇、热导率下界 κ_m 与 V_s，与热源分布、域形状及源—传感器距离无关。并以 Q̇=50 pW、κ_m=0.1 W/(m·K)、V_s=10⁻²² m³（约60 nm直径球）给出数值算例。

Result: 在上述参数下，定理允许的最大温升约为高于参考水平 2 mK。

Conclusion: 该结果直接适用于纳米金刚石、上转换纳米颗粒、荧光聚合物颗粒及核壳量子点，表明在稳态热传导下，热源局域化与源—传感器间距的不确定性均无法解释观测到的开尔文级读数。

Abstract: The steady-state temperature predicted for an ideal point heat source diverges in conventional heat-conduction theory, but no physical thermometer measures a point value. We derive a sharp, shape-independent upper bound on the quasi-steady temperature rise of a sensor with thermometric volume $V_\mathrm{s}$, placed anywhere inside a region enclosed by an isothermal reference surface. The bound requires only the total heat power generated in the region $\dot{Q}$, a lower bound on its thermal conductivity $κ_\mathrm{m}$, and the value of $V_\mathrm{s}$. It is otherwise independent of the heat-source distribution, domain shape, and source--sensor distance. For $\dot{Q}=50~\mathrm{pW}$, $κ_\mathrm{m} = 0.1~\mathrm{W\,m^{-1}K^{-1}}$, and $V_\mathrm{s}=10^{-22}\mathrm{\,m^3}$, approximately the volume of a $60~\mathrm{nm}$-diameter sphere, the theorem permits a maximum temperature rise of approximately $2~\mathrm{mK}$ above the reference level. The result applies directly to nanodiamonds, upconversion nanoparticles, fluorescent polymer particles, and core-shell quantum dots. It shows that neither source localization nor uncertain source-sensor separation can account for kelvin-scale readings under steady-state heat conduction.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond low-dissipation regime: general condition for Curzon-Ahlborn efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24434)
*Yuki Izumida*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将低耗散卡诺热机模型推广至逆操作时间的非线性响应，导出了在二次阶（温差二阶）恢复Curzon-Ahlborn效率的一般条件，广义对称耗散条件与Newton冷却定律CA模型均为其特例，并给出了具有一般传热律的內可逆模型呈现CA效率的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CA效率作为最大功率效率的普适性仅在温差的线性阶（逆操作时间线性响应内）成立，二次阶一般失效；标准低耗散模型中所需的对称耗散条件并非必要条件（內可逆的原始CA模型即无需该对称性），因此缺乏统一判据来解释何时在二次阶可恢复CA效率。

Method: 构建低耗散卡诺热机模型的推广版本，在逆操作时间中引入非线性响应项；由此推导使CA效率在温差二次阶被恢复的一般性条件，并检验广义对称耗散条件与基于Newton冷却定律的CA模型是否作为该条件的特例被涵盖。

Result: 获得了一个一般性条件，其在二次阶给出CA效率；广义对称耗散条件与Newton冷却定律CA模型均被证明是该条件的特例；进一步应用该条件，明确了具有一般传热律的內可逆模型显示CA效率所需满足的条件。

Conclusion: CA效率在二次阶的普适性可由一个统一的、基于逆操作时间非线性响应的一般条件刻画，从而把低耗散框架与內可逆框架（含一般传热律）纳入同一判据体系，澄清了对称耗散条件的作用及其非必要性。

Abstract: In recent years, physicists have extensively investigated the universality of Curzon-Ahlborn (CA) efficiency as the efficiency at maximum power of heat engines, which, like Carnot efficiency, is determined only by heat-bath temperatures. While the universality of the CA efficiency to the first order in the temperature difference is established within linear response theory in the inverse operation time, it is generally lost at quadratic order. In the low-dissipation Carnot heat engine model known as the standard framework, the symmetric dissipation condition is necessary. Yet, it is not a necessary condition as the original CA model that adopts endoreversibility does not require such symmetry. In this study, we extend the low-dissipation model to include nonlinear response in the inverse operation time and derive a general condition under which the CA efficiency is recovered in the quadratic order. The generalized symmetric dissipation condition and the CA model with Newton's law of cooling are shown to be special cases of this condition. By applying the obtained condition, we also clarify the condition under which an endoreversible model with a general heat-transfer law exhibits the CA efficiency.

</details>


### [110] [Energy-optimal scheduling for clocked reversible computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24435)
*Michael C. Thumann,David Soloveichik*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出了"半可逆能量—内存—时间权衡"框架：在内存有限、时间有限的计算中，将可逆检查点移动与局部不可逆擦除按价格比 λ 进行取舍，为线性依赖图的顺序计算导出精确分隔递推，并证明渐近最优调度为"严格可逆块+一次局部擦除"的循环结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算耗散既来自信息擦除，也来自有限时间操作；原则上二者在无限空间与时间约束下均可避免（逻辑可逆计算以ancilla清理替代擦除，慢速绝热协议消除有限时间耗散）。但内存与时间受限时，用可逆清理替代擦除本身需要额外耗散操作，从而形成能量—内存—时间之间的本质权衡，论文旨在形式化并求解这一权衡。

Method: 在外部时钟驱动、热耦合计算机的固定工作点上建模：可逆检查点移动与局部不可逆擦除具有有效价格 ε_R 与 ε_I，其比值 λ=ε_I/ε_R 决定在检查点内存约束下能量最优的半可逆调度，通过可逆移动与擦除的交换求解；针对依赖图为一条直线的顺序计算，推导精确的分隔递推（separator recurrence），并证明渐近最优调度结构；进一步耦合随机热力学本构模型，将温度、基本操作时长、内存及器件相关操作代价纳入分析。

Result: 导出了线性依赖图计算的精确分隔递推；证明渐近最优调度由"严格可逆块+单次局部擦除"重复构成；结合本构模型表明温度、基本操作时长、内存规模与器件操作价格共同决定最优调度采用可逆清理、选择性擦除还是每次处置即擦除三种模式。

Conclusion: 可逆性与不可逆性并非二元对立，而是由 λ、内存界限与操作时间共同刻画的连续权衡；所给调度定理与随机热力学模型为在给定硬件参数下选择能量最优的擦除/清理策略提供了可计算的判据。

Abstract: Computational dissipation may arise from both information erasure and finite-time operation, although both contributions can in principle be avoided in the absence of space and time constraints. Logically reversible computation can replace erasure with ancilla cleanup, and slow adiabatic protocols can eliminate any excess finite-time dissipation. Under limited memory and in finite time, however, choosing reversible cleanup over erasure requires additional dissipative operations, creating a semi-reversible energy--memory--time tradeoff. We formulate this tradeoff at a fixed operating point of an externally clocked thermally coupled computer, where reversible checkpoint moves and local irreversible erasures have specified effective prices $ε_R$ and $ε_I$. Their ratio $λ=ε_I/ε_R$ selects an energy-optimal semi-reversible schedule under a checkpoint-memory bound by trading reversible moves against erasures. For a sequential computation whose dependency graph is a line, we derive an exact separator recurrence and prove that an asymptotically optimal schedule repeats a strictly reversible block followed by one local erasure. Coupling this scheduling theorem to a stochastic-thermodynamic constitutive model shows how temperature, primitive duration, memory, and device-dependent operation costs determine whether the optimal schedule uses reversible cleanup, elective erasures, or erasure at every disposal.

</details>


### [111] [Autonomous Thermal Switching Outperforms the Frozen Optimum with Vanishing Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24589)
*Siyi Deng,Longxing Xu,Zhuolin Ye,Xian He,Jianhui Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究在固定降温预算与可见平衡态、且保持联合细致平衡的条件下，自主热开关能否在系统—辅助体关联任意小的同时优于最佳冻结势垒分配。结论是存在"弛豫优势"：最优快开关为将预算集中于单条边的混合分布，三态模型给出严格增益及其有限速率阈值，且更快切换可使关联在弛豫全程趋于消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有势垒设计（冻结/静态分配）在给定资源下已最优化，但尚不清楚"随时间自主切换"这一动态自由度是否能带来超过静态最优的收益，以及该收益是否必须以系统与辅助体的关联为代价（即是否可在近无关联极限下实现）。

Method: 在有限可逆网络上，以固定降温预算和固定可见平衡态为约束，保持联合细致平衡；比较自主快切换与最佳冻结势垒分配；通过最优控制/分布优化求最优切换协议，并用三态模型解析刻画增益与有限速率阈值，进一步推广到集体与连续模型。

Result: 建立了固定预算与可见平衡下的弛豫优势；最优快切换为把预算集中于单条边的混合分布；三态模型给出严格正增益及达到增益所需的有限切换速率阈值；切换更快时增益保持而系统—辅助体关联在弛豫过程中消失；集体与连续模型给出可实验的胶体方案。

Conclusion: 自主热切换确实能超越最佳冻结势垒分配，且该优势无需系统—辅助体关联，可在近无关联的准静态极限下实现，并具备胶体实验可检验性。

Abstract: Can autonomous thermal switching outperform the best frozen barrier allocation while keeping system--auxiliary correlations arbitrarily small? We establish this relaxation advantage at fixed lowering budget and visible equilibrium, preserving joint detailed balance. On finite reversible networks, optimal fast switching mixes profiles concentrating the budget on single edges. A three-state model gives a strict gain and its finite-rate threshold. Faster switching preserves the gain while correlations vanish throughout relaxation. Collective and continuous models suggest a colloidal test.

</details>


### [112] [Spontaneous particle-hole symmetry breaking in a disordered two-site quantum breakdown model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24669)
*Kinya Guan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究无序双格点量子击穿模型中自发粒子-空穴对称破缺，在大模式数极限下解析求得自由能并发现有限温度对称破缺相变，随每次转化产生费米子数增加由连续变为一阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无序量子多体系统中自发粒子-空穴对称破缺是否存在有限温度相变，以及该相变的性质如何依赖于模型参数。

Method: 在每格点费米子模式数很大的极限下构建可解的双格点量子击穿模型；在两格点模式数之比较小时解析推导自由能；结合数值计算验证相变类型。

Result: 解析得到自由能并发现有限温度对称破缺相变；随每次转化产生的费米子数增加，相变从连续转变为一阶；数值结果显示两类相变在该解析区域之外依然存在，连续相变的序参量临界指数接近1/2。

Conclusion: 无序双格点量子击穿模型支持有限温度自发粒子-空穴对称破缺；相变级数由转化过程中产生的费米子数控制，且数值证据表明结论具有更广的适用性。

Abstract: We study spontaneous particle-hole symmetry breaking in a disordered two-site quantum breakdown model. The model is solvable in the limit of a large number of fermionic modes on each site. For a small ratio of the numbers of modes on the two sites, we derive the free energy analytically and find a finite-temperature symmetry-breaking transition. This transition changes from continuous to first order as the number of fermions produced in each conversion increases. Numerical results show that both types of transition persist beyond this regime, with an order-parameter exponent close to $1/2$ for continuous transitions.

</details>


### [113] [Frustration and topology in the phase behavior of solute-frustrated $q$-clock models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24923)
*Hari Rajesh,Richard C. Remsing*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出溶质阻挫q-clock (SFqC) 模型，通过蒙特卡洛模拟与理论分析研究阻挫与拓扑的耦合，揭示q=4、6体系丰富的相图及拓扑缺陷驱动结晶等新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫可增强涨落并产生新奇相行为，而阻挫与拓扑缺陷的结合能通过拓扑转变与阻挫拓扑转变进一步复杂化相图；现有工作对二者如何共同影响相行为缺乏系统理解，因此需要构建可调控的模型体系。

Method: 在q-clock模型（每个格点自旋可取q个取向，可表现自发对称破缺与拓扑转变）中引入溶质格点，构建溶质阻挫q-clock (SFqC) 模型；溶质与自旋的相互作用实质上为体系中正拓扑缺陷（涡旋）浓度设定固定下界，从而引入阻挫。针对q=4与q=6情形，采用蒙特卡洛模拟并结合理论描述对相行为进行分析。

Result: SFqC模型相图依赖参数选择且十分丰富；低温下存在多样相态，包括强阻挫畴的形成以及长程有序的缺失；即便在低温仍存在拓扑效应，例如拓扑缺陷可驱动结晶。

Conclusion: 阻挫与拓扑的耦合显著拓展了clock类模型的相行为，为利用阻挫与拓扑调控相行为与涨落的材料设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Frustration can enhance fluctuations and create new and interesting phase behavior. When combined with the ability to host topological defects, frustration can increase the complexity of a system's phase diagram through topological and frustrated topological transitions. To understand the interplay of frustration and topology on phase behavior, we introduce and study solute-frustrated $q$-clock (SF$q$C) models, in which solute sites are added to the $q$-clock model, a lattice model where each spin can take one of $q$ orientations. The $q$-clock model can exhibit spontaneous symmetry breaking and topological transitions, and the interaction between the spins and a solute frustrates the system, in essence by creating a fixed lower bound on the concentration of positive topological defects or vortices. We perform Monte Carlo simulations and develop theoretical descriptions of SF$q$C models with $q=4$ and $q=6$ to understand their phase behavior. We find that these systems exhibit rich phase diagrams that depend on the choice of parameters. In particular, SF$q$C models have a wide range of interesting low-temperature phases, including the presence of strongly frustrated domains and the absence of long-range order. We also find topological effects even at low temperatures; for instance, we find that topological defects can drive crystallization. We anticipate these results to guide the development of materials that take advantage of frustration and topology to tune phase behavior and fluctuations.

</details>


### [114] [Scaling Dimensions and Two-Point Amplitudes from Operator Covariance Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24943)
*You-Chiuan Chen,Ribhu K. Kaul*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文深入分析利用晶格算符协方差矩阵本征分解提取连续统标度信息的方法，严格证明其本征值衰减由最小Nℓ个标度维数控制，并提出由本征矢构造连续统两点关联函数振幅的新方法，并在合成数据与二维Ising模型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界晶格统计力学模型的两点函数长程行为蕴含底层量子场论的普适信息，但一般晶格算符会与无穷多连续统标度算符以未知系数耦合，导致这些普适信息难以提取。已有工作（Phys. Rev. E 114, 024146 (2026)）提出用有限Nℓ个晶格算符交叉关联构造的协方差矩阵本征分解来分离部分信息，但缺乏严格论证与完整框架。

Method: 对Nℓ个晶格算符的协方差矩阵作本征分解；在标度不变性假设下（并计入无关微扰）给出显式证明：本征值的领头衰减由最小的Nℓ个标度维数控制；进而提出利用协方差矩阵本征矢构造连续统两点关联函数振幅、从而完整确定其结构的新方法。

Result: 给出本征值领头衰减受最小Nℓ个标度维数支配的显式证明（考虑无关扰动后仍成立）；该方法可从晶格协方差矩阵完全确定连续统两点函数的结构与振幅；在按标度假设生成的合成数据及二维Ising模型的数值精确数据上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 协方差矩阵本征分解是提取临界晶格模型连续统两点函数普适结构的可靠工具，本文的理论证明与新振幅重构方法将其由部分信息提取推广为完整确定方案，并在合成与精确数值数据中证实其有效性。

Abstract: The long distance behavior of two-point functions of critical lattice statistical mechanics models depend on detailed information of the underlying quantum field theory. Yet these universal properties can be difficult to extract because generic lattice operators couple to the infinite hierarchy of continuum scaling operators with unknown coefficients. Recently in Physical Review E 114, 024146 (2026), one approach to disentangle some of this information was proposed using the eigen-decomposition of the operator covariance matrix constructed from cross correlations of a finite set of $N_\ell$ lattice operators. In this work we analyze this method in detail. We provide an explicit proof that under the assumption of scale invariance the leading decay of the eigenvalues are controlled by the smallest $N_\ell$ scaling dimensions, even after taking irrelevant perturbations into account. Based on this understanding, we propose a new method by which the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix can be used to construct the amplitudes of the continuum two-point correlation functions. This allows for a complete determination of the structure of the continuum two-point functions from the lattice covariance matrix. We demonstrate our ideas both on synthetic data sets (drawn from the scaling hypothesis) and numerically exact data for the two dimensional Ising model.

</details>


### [115] [Run-and-tumble particles in a harmonic external field: constructing the solution of the Fokker-Planck equation from the equations of motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24961)
*Aoran Sun,Fangfu Ye,Jeff Z. Y. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 引入一种针对 run-and-tumble 粒子 Langevin 方程的积分方法，用于计算位置与活性速度的联合分布，并证明其与相应 Fokker-Planck 方程精确解等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多以 Fokker-Planck 方程求解 run-and-tumble 粒子的分布，而直接从 Langevin 方程出发获取位置-活性速度联合分布（尤其含外场时）的方法尚不完善，作者希望建立一条从随机轨迹层面求解的互补途径。

Method: 对 run-and-tumble 粒子的 Langevin 方程进行积分：在给定末时刻粒子处于特定状态（tumble 态，或指定速度方向的 run 态）的条件下，构造分布的矩与关联函数；分别在无外场与谐波外势两种情形下处理，一维情形下导出 tumble 态及条件 run 态的精确稳态分布。

Result: 得到含谐波外势的一维体系中 tumble 态及指定速度方向下 run 态的条件稳态分布的精确解析结果，并验证其与对应 Fokker-Planck 方程的精确解一致。

Conclusion: 该矩与条件关联函数方法可作为求解活性粒子 Langevin 动力学的有效工具，所得结果与 Fokker-Planck 途径互相印证，为处理含外场的 run-and-tumble 体系提供了一种可推广的轨迹层面框架。

Abstract: A method for integrating the Langevin equations of motion for run-and-tumble particles is introduced to compute the joint distribution of their position and active velocity, with and without a harmonic external force field. The key step is the construction of the moments of the distribution and the correlation functions, conditional on the particles being in a specific state at the final time. For one-dimensional run-and-tumble particles in a harmonic external potential, the exact steady-state distributions for the tumble state, as well as for the run state conditional on a specified velocity direction, are obtained. This enables us to demonstrate their equivalence to the exact solutions of the corresponding Fokker-Planck equations.

</details>


### [116] [Large Deviations in Switching Diffusion: Dynamical Condensation and Diffusivity BBP Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25072)
*Mathis Guéneau,Timo Schorlepp,Satya N. Majumdar,Gregory Schehr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究泊松切换扩散系数下布朗粒子的大偏差行为，揭示速率函数的动力学相变与轨迹凝聚现象，并证明其与BBP型边缘机制等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作已用更新方法得到位移速率函数并观察到奇异性，但缺乏对相应动力学相变与轨迹凝聚的细致刻画。

Method: 在$W(D)\sim C(D_{\max}-D)^\nu$的边缘分布假设下，分析大偏差区间$x(t)\simeq yt$中位移增量在切换区间间的分配，并结合矩生成函数的过渡分析。

Result: 中等$y$时位移由$O(t)$个增量分摊；超过阈值$y_-$出现单个区间贡献$O(t)$增量（动力学凝聚）；$\nu>1$时存在第二阈值$y_+>y_-$，$0<\nu\leq1$时两阈值合并；矩生成函数发生从集体贡献到$D_{\max}$附近态选择的互补过渡，受与BBP过渡相同的边缘机制支配。

Conclusion: 扩散系数选择与轨迹凝聚是同一现象的两个表现；动力学凝聚预测经稀有事件抽样数值结果验证。

Abstract: We consider a Brownian particle with position $x(t)$ whose diffusivity changes at Poissonian switching times, with each new value drawn independently from a bounded distribution $W(D)$. Assuming $W(D)\sim C(D_{\max}-D)^ν$ near its upper edge, we study the large-deviation regime $x(t)\simeq yt$ with $y = O(1)$ at large times, far beyond the typical diffusive scale. In a recent work Guéneau et al., Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 067102 (2025), the rate function of the displacement was obtained through a renewal approach and it was shown to display several singularities. In this paper, we carry out a detailed study of the corresponding dynamical phase transitions and trajectory condensation. For moderate $y$, the imposed displacement is shared among $O(t)$ displacement increments accumulated during successive intervals between switches. Beyond a first threshold $y_-$, one interval produces an increment of order $t$ carrying a finite fraction of the total displacement, signaling dynamical condensation. For $ν>1$, a second threshold $y_+>y_-$ is reached when this macroscopic increment carries asymptotically the entire imposed displacement, whereas for $0<ν\leq1$ the two thresholds merge, $y_-=y_+$. In turn, the moment-generating function of $x(t)$ displays a complementary transition from collective contributions of many diffusivities to selection of states near $D_{\max}$. We show that this transition is governed by the same edge mechanism as the Baik-Ben Arous-Péché (BBP) transition, indicating that diffusivity selection and trajectory condensation are two manifestations of the same phenomenon. We validate our predictions for the dynamical condensation against numerical results from rare event sampling.

</details>


### [117] [The Ericson Transition in Time-Reversal Invariant Systems: Orthogonally Invariant Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25103)
*Simon Köhnes,Thomas Guhr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究时间反演与旋转不变系统（正交Wigner-Dyson类）在Ericson区间内非对角散射矩阵元与截面的分布，证明其渐近展开首阶为高斯与指数行为，并给出次阶项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作仅对酉类与辛类Hamiltonian用渐近展开分析了Ericson跃迁；正交类常是实验分析中最相关的，但其分布尚缺相应渐近处理。

Method: 基于随机矩阵理论，在正交对称类（时间反演与旋转不变）中对Ericson跃迁的非对角散射矩阵元及截面分布作渐近展开，推导首阶与次阶项，并与数值模拟对比。

Result: 正交情形下分布的首阶渐近项呈高斯（矩阵元）与指数（截面）行为；给出次阶修正，数值模拟与解析结果一致；比较三类Wigner-Dyson类的Ericson跃迁异同。

Conclusion: Ericson跃迁的高斯/指数普适行为在三类Wigner-Dyson类中均成立，正交类的首阶项形式与另两类相同但展开系数与次阶修正存在类依赖差异。

Abstract: We investigate the distributions of off-diagonal scattering matrix elements and cross-sections in the Ericson regime for systems that are time-reversal and rotationally invariant. In previous works, we developed techniques to analyze the Ericson transition by means of asymptotic expansions in the cases of unitarily and symplectically invariant Hamiltonians. Here, we prove that Gaussian and exponential behavior is found as the leading-order term of an asymptotic expansion of those distributions also in the orthogonal case, which is often the relevant one for analyzing experiments. Moreover, we derive the subleading-order term and compare with numerical simulations. Finally, we discuss similarities and differences of the Ericson transition for all three Wigner-Dyson classes.

</details>


### [118] [Origins of Faceted Anomalous Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26356)
*Maximilien Bernard,José Luis Iguain,Alejandro Kolton,Alberto Rosso*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文指出"面状反常标度"（α_s>1、α_s≠α、α_loc=1）的统计起源在于斜率场的空间统计：斜率增量可分解为典型涨落与稀有的大跳跃，二者任一占主导即可产生该标度结构，且无需界面真的形成facet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面状反常标度虽以其界面倾向形成长的近线性段（facet）而得名，但其统计起源以及facet是否为必要条件长期不清楚；作者希望从斜率场统计出发给出统一的解释框架。

Method: 将斜率增量分解为典型涨落与稀有较大跳跃两类贡献，分析各自对二阶矩（粗糙度）的支配作用；并在广义非调和Larkin模型与柱状Kardar–Parisi–Zhang（cKPZ）方程中解析地建立该框架，精确导出全局、谱与局域粗糙度指数。

Result: 反常标度可由两种机制产生：(i) 典型斜率增量本身具有真实的系统尺寸依赖的反常标度；(ii) 稀有跳跃主导二阶矩。两者可共存，其竞争导致斜率的双分形（bifractal）标度。Larkin模型可实现两种机制，产生有facet或无facet的反常界面；柱状KPZ属于跳跃主导情形，给出α_s=3/2。

Conclusion: 面状反常标度源于斜率场中典型涨落与稀有跳跃的相对权重，而非必然要求facet存在；该框架统一刻画了反常粗糙界面的指数结构，并为区分不同生长方程的反常标度类别提供判据。

Abstract: Fluctuating interfaces are typically characterized by their global, spectral, and local roughness exponents. Faceted anomalous scaling, characterized by $α_s>1$, $α_s\neqα$, and $α_{\mathrm{loc}}=1$, owes its name to the tendency of such interfaces to develop long, nearly linear segments called facets. Its statistical origin, and whether facets are required, have remained unclear. We show that this exponent structure is determined by the spatial statistics of the slope field. Slope increments split into typical fluctuations and rare, larger jumps. This anomalous scaling arises either when the typical slope increment displays genuine system-size-dependent anomalous scaling or when the rare jumps dominate the second moment. The two contributions can coexist, and their competition leads to bifractal scaling of the slopes. We establish this framework analytically in the generalized anharmonic Larkin model and the columnar Kardar--Parisi--Zhang equation, and we derive exactly the global, spectral, and local roughness exponents in both cases. The Larkin model realizes both mechanisms and produces anomalous interfaces with or without facets, whereas columnar KPZ belongs to the jump-dominated case and yields $α_s=3/2$.

</details>


### [119] [Annealing Dynamics of the Two-Dimensional Fully Frustrated Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25109)
*Kai-Hsin Wu,Anders W. Sandvik,Ying-Jer Kao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文用模拟退火研究二维完全阻挫Ising模型的动力学标度行为，将Kibble-Zurek机制推广到T_c=0、关联长度指数发散的临界基态情形，所得标度形式与数值结果高度吻合，动力学指数z≈2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型具有高度简并的临界基态（T_c=0）及良好已知的平衡性质，但其非平衡动力学行为尚未被系统刻画；常规Kibble-Zurek机制假设临界点处关联长度按幂律发散，无法直接适用于关联长度在T→0时指数发散的情形，因此需要发展适用的退火方案并建立相应标度理论。

Method: 采用模拟退火（simulated annealing）研究二维完全阻挫Ising模型，设计一种便于把Kibble-Zurek机制推广到指数发散关联长度情形的退火协议，推导相应的标度形式，并对磁化率、过剩能量和Edwards-Anderson序参量等量进行标度分析与拟合，提取动力学指数z及其对初始温度的依赖。

Result: 数值结果与推导出的标度形式高度一致，并发现存在常规Kibble-Zurek机制中不具备的初始温度依赖性。各物理量给出的z相互一致：磁化率拟合得z=1.99(2)，过剩能量得z=2.072(8)，Edwards-Anderson序参量得z=2.09(3)，均接近z=2，且与瞬淬后弛豫及平衡时序关联的既有研究（含平衡自关联函数给出的z=2.004(9)）相符。

Conclusion: 二维完全阻挫Ising模型的非平衡标度行为可由推广的Kibble-Zurek框架良好描述，动力学指数z≈2，与平衡及瞬淬研究结果自洽，表明该模型在非平衡情形下同样具有稳健的普适动力学特征。

Abstract: We study the dynamical scaling properties of the two-dimensional fully frustrated Ising model by simulated annealing. The model has a highly degenerate, critical ground state with well known equilibrium properties. To study its out-of-equilibrium behavior, we develop an annealing protocol that is convenient for extending the Kibble-Zurek mechanism to the case of an exponentially divergent correlation length in the limit of the temperature $T \to T_c = 0$. We find excellent agreement with the scaling forms derived, including a dependence on the initial temperature that is not present in the conventional Kibble-Zurek mechanism. The dynamic exponent $z$ is consistent among all quantities studied; fits of its dependence on the initial temperature give $z = 1.99(2)$ (susceptibility), $z = 2.072(8)$ (excess energy), and $z = 2.09(3)$ (Edwards-Anderson order parameter). These values are close to $z=2$, which is consistent with previous studies of relaxation after instantaneous quenches and of time dependent correlations in equilibrium, including the estimate $z = 2.004(9)$ from equilibrium autocorrelation functions.

</details>


### [120] [Tensor network Monte Carlo study of the J1-J2 Ising model on the square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25270)
*Wanying Zheng,Tao Chen,Youjin Deng,Wanzhou Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出张量网络蒙特卡罗(TNMC)算法研究阻挫J1-J2方形格子Ising模型，支持开/周期边界，结合直方图重加权与扫描块更新，通过有限尺寸标定提取临界温度与有效热指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该阻挫模型磁相丰富，但常规蒙特卡罗受临界慢化困扰、张量重整化群受截断误差影响，相变难以准确刻画，亟需高效且近无偏的大尺度模拟方法。

Method: 开发TNMC算法：周期格子上采用扫描式块更新策略，更新自旋块时固定周围自旋；将采样器与直方图重加权耦合以提升效率，并分别单独测试各技术以验证其独立性优化效果；在g=0.55与g=1.0处对Binder累积量数据进行广泛的有限尺寸标定分析。

Result: 在g=0.55和g=1.0处通过有限尺寸标定提取出表观临界温度与有效热指数，并验证了块更新与直方图重加权两项技术的独立有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为周期边界下J1-J2类阻挫磁体的大尺度模拟提供了高效、近乎无偏的途径，并可便捷推广至其他阻挫自旋格子。

Abstract: The frustrated square-lattice J1-J2 Ising model exhibits rich magnetic phases, yet its phase transitions remain difficult to characterize due to critical slowing down in conventional Monte Carlo and truncation errors inherent to tensor renormalization group methods.We develop a tensor-network Monte Carlo (TNMC) algorithm for this frustrated model, which supports both open and periodic boundary conditions. For periodic lattices, we use a sweeping block-update strategy that updates spin blocks while fixing surrounding spins.To boost efficiency, we couple our sampler with histogram reweighting, and test each technique standalone to validate these independent optimizations.We perform extensive finite-size scaling analyses at g=0.55 and g=1.0, extracting apparent critical temperatures and effective thermal exponents from Binder-cumulant data.Our method offers an efficient, nearly bias-free path for large-scale simulations of J1-J2-type frustrated magnets under periodic boundaries and generalizes readily to other frustrated spin lattices.

</details>


### [121] [Generation of $N$-particle kappa-distributed velocities via superstatistical Gibbs sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25487)
*Leonardo Herrera_Fuenzalida,Sergio Davis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 基于超统计结构实现 Gibbs 采样器，其平稳分布为速度与 β 的联合分布，使采样速度服从 N 粒子 kappa 分布，从而构造性地再现粒子间动能相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kappa 分布广泛用于描述空间与实验室等离子体中的重尾非平衡速度分布。在 PIC 模拟中正确使用它不能仅采样单粒子边缘分布：N 粒子 kappa 分布预测粒子动能之间存在非平凡相关性，而单粒子边缘采样无法复现这些相关性。

Method: 利用 kappa 分布的超统计结构，构造一个 Gibbs 采样器，使其平稳分布为速度与 β（逆温度型超参数）的联合分布；通过交替条件采样，使输出的速度样本按构造服从 N 粒子 kappa 分布。

Result: 采样得到的速度样本服从 N 粒子 kappa 分布，并按构造自动再现粒子间动能相关性。

Conclusion: 以超统计为基础的 Gibbs 采样为 PIC 模拟提供了一种正确初始化 kappa 分布粒子的方法，超越单粒子边缘采样，保留 N 粒子联合分布所固有的动能相关性。

Abstract: The \emph{kappa} distribution is widely used to model heavy-tailed, non-equilibrium velocity distributions in space and laboratory plasmas. Its correct use in particle-in-cell (PIC) simulations requires more than sampling the single-particle marginal: the $N$-particle \emph{kappa} distribution predicts non-trivial correlations between the kinetic energies of particles. Building on the superstatistical structure, we implement a Gibbs sampler whose stationary distribution is the joint distribution of the velocities and $β$, so that the sampled velocities follow the $N$-particle \emph{kappa} distribution and reproduce these correlations by construction.

</details>


### [122] [Phase transitions of the antiferromagnetic $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ Ising model on the cubic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25884)
*Kai-Wei Huang,Chien-Ting Chen,Shang-Wei Li,Fu-Jiun Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过远超以往尺寸的蒙特卡洛模拟结合高效神经网络方法，确认三维立方晶格上J1=J2=J3=1.0的反铁磁J1-J2-J3 Ising模型相变为二级相变，从而解决了此前的争议；同时研究了J2/J1=4/7情形的临界现象，并提出资源极省、可扩展至更高维系统的NN训练测试策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于三维J1-J2-J3反铁磁Ising模型（J1=J2=J3=1.0）相变性质的研究结论相互矛盾，亟需以更大系统规模厘清该争议；同时传统神经网络方法计算成本与存储开销大，限制其向高维系统推广。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟与神经网络（NN）相结合的方法研究该模型：使用比以往研究大得多的晶格尺寸生成数据并分析相变性质；针对J2/J1=4/7、J3=J1=1.0情形也进行临界现象分析；在NN部分引入专门的训练与测试策略，降低计算开销与存储需求。

Result: 对J1=J2=J3=1.0，大尺寸晶格数据表明相变明确为二级相变，无任何歧义，从而化解了此前的争论；同时给出了J2/J1=4/7及J3=J1=1.0情形的临界行为结果。所提出的NN训练测试策略较文献中常用标准流程效率显著更高，且在大线性尺寸L下所需存储容量仅为传统NN计算的千分之一量级。

Conclusion: 该模型在J1=J2=J3=1.0处的相变被确定为二级相变，争议得以解决；所发展的资源高效NN方法为将神经网络技术用于研究高维系统临界行为提供了极低资源消耗的途径。

Abstract: Motivated by the conflicting findings in prior studies regarding the nature of the phase transition for the three-dimensional antiferromagnetic $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ Ising model on the cubic lattice with $J_1 = J_2 = J_3 = 1.0$, in this study we conduct a detailed investigation of this model using both Monte Carlo simulations and neural networks (NN). Here $J_1$ and $J_2$ are the intralayer nearest-neighbor and next-nearest-neighbor couplings, respectively, and $J_3$ is the nearest-neighbor interlayer interaction. By investigating the data obtained from much larger lattices than those used in previous studies, we find that the phase transition for $J_1 = J_2 = J_3 = 1.0$ is second order without any ambiguity. Hence we bring a resolution to this debate. Apart from $J_1 = J_2 = J_3 = 1.0$, we study the critical phenomenon associated with $J_2/J_1 = 4/7$ and $J_3 = J_1 = 1.0$ as well. In addition, by employing a dedicated training and testing strategy, our NN computation not only is much more efficient than those utilizing the standard procedures commonly used in the literature, but also requires only a per-mille fraction of the storage capacity needed by a conventional NN calculation when a large linear system size $L$ is considered. The method employed here provides an extremely resource-efficient route for applying NN techniques to study the critical behaviors of higher-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [123] [Gini coefficient of scientists and universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26053)
*Klaus M. Frahm,Dima L. Shepelyansky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 应用洛伦兹曲线与帕累托曲线及基尼系数分析顶尖科学家与大学的引用分布，发现引用高度集中，并用Rayleigh-Jeans热化/凝聚类比财富不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引用分布的不平等程度缺乏直观、可跨领域比较的量化刻画；作者借鉴经济学中分析财富不平等的成熟工具（洛伦兹曲线、帕累托曲线、基尼系数），以期获得对顶尖科学家和大学引用集中度的新洞见。

Method: 基于Google Scholar公开的科学家与大学文章引用数据构建引用分布，对分布施加洛伦兹曲线构建算法并计算基尼系数G；对15位诺贝尔奖得主（含A. Einstein、R. Feynman）的文章引用分析，再通过附属科学家引用计算世界顶尖大学（如Princeton、Cambridge）的基尼系数；并用帕累托曲线描述尾部，最后以Rayleigh-Jeans热化与凝聚概念拟合引用分布。

Result: 15位诺奖得主文章平均G=0.81；50%的文章仅占全部引用的2.7%，前10%（1%）文章占71%（30%）的引用。大学层面，约65%的引用仅由10%的科学家产生，50%的科学家仅贡献3%的大学引用。引用分布可由Rayleigh-Jeans热化与凝聚概念较好描述，并与世界财富不平等存在相似性。

Conclusion: 经济学不平等度量工具能有效刻画学术引用分布的高度集中特征；顶尖科学家与顶尖大学的引用不平等程度与财富不平等类似，且可纳入Rayleigh-Jeans热化/凝聚的物理框架理解。

Abstract: Using the public database of Google Scholar article citations of scientists and universities we apply the algorithm for constructing Lorenz curves to the resulting citation distributions. Using Lorenz and Pareto curves, broadly used for economic analyses of wealth inequality across countries, we show that this approach gives a useful insight of citation distributions of world-leading scientists characterized by the Gini coefficient $G$ obtained from the Lorenz curves. For articles of 15 Nobel laureates (including A. Einstein and R. Feynman) we find the average $G=0.81$ with 50% of articles capturing only 2.7% of all citations and the top 10% (1%) of articles capturing 71% (30%) of all citations. Furthmore, we determine for world-leading universities the Gini coefficient via the citations of affiliated scientists. We show that about 65 percent of all citations belonging to a university (e.g. Princeton, Cambridge) are generated only by 10 percent of their university scientists while 50% of scientists produce only 3% of all university citations. The citation distributions are well described by the concept of Rayleigh-Jeans thermalization and condensation with similarities traced to the world wealth inequality.

</details>


### [124] [Generalised Fluctuating Dynamic Density-Functional Theory: Derivation and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26129)
*Toby Kay,Serafim Kalliadasis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从微观哈密顿动力学出发，利用粗粒化技术（含Zwanzig投影算符方法）严格推导出适用于任意可观测量场的广义涨落动态密度泛函理论（FDDFT），其动力学由可逆、耗散和随机三部分构成，并演示了其与DDFT、宏观模型的层级关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DFT刻画平衡态密度分布，DDFT将其推广到动力学，但对远离平衡的介观系统，涨落不可忽略，需建立随机性扩展（FDDFT）；同时现有FDDFT缺乏从第一性原理出发的一般性推导。

Method: 以微观哈密顿动力学为起点，采用粗粒化技术（包括Zwanzig投影算符方法）推导广义FDDFT；动力学中的热力学驱动力由熵泛函给出，可逆项、耗散项、随机项分别通过Liouville算符、Green-Kubo关系与涨落耗散定理与微观动力学相联系；并通过进一步平均粗粒化得到宏观尺度的广义DDFT。

Result: 所得FDDFT适用于任意可观测量场；用于胶体和简单黏性流体两个典型体系时，分别重新推导出近期提出的胶体FDDFT，并严格恢复非等温Landau-Lifshitz涨落Navier-Stokes方程，为其提供了基础性推导并澄清了其基本原理。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从微观动力学到介观涨落动力学及宏观DDFT的统一层级框架，证明了FDDFT作为一般性理论的有效性与实用性，并为经典唯象涨落流体力学模型奠定微观基础。

Abstract: Classical density-functional theory is a powerful theoretical framework for studying the equilibrium density distribution of complex many-body systems. The core of the framework is to bridge microscopic and macroscopic descriptions of fluids through a variational principle that minimises the system's free energy. For dynamical settings, dynamic DFT (DDFT) extends the equilibrium framework to capture the time evolution of an order parameter, typically density. However, DDFT is often insufficient for mesoscopic systems far from equilibrium where fluctuations become significant, necessitating a stochastic extension: fluctuating DDFT. Here we derive a generalised FDDFT from first principles starting from the microscopic Hamiltonian dynamics using coarse-graining techniques, including Zwanzig's projection operator method. The resulting FDDFT applies to arbitrary observable fields, where the dynamics are driven by a thermodynamic force expressed in terms of an entropy functional. The dynamics consist of reversible, dissipative, and stochastic contributions, each directly linked to the underlying microscopic dynamics via the Liouville operator, Green-Kubo relations, and the fluctuation-dissipation theorem. To highlight the difference in the level of description between DDFT and FDDFT, we demonstrate how further coarse-graining via averaging leads to a generalised DDFT, valid at the macroscale. To showcase the effectiveness and utility of the generalised FDDFT, we apply it to two prototypical fluid systems: colloidal and simple viscous fluids. In the former case, we utilise the new formalism to re-derive the recently found colloidal FDDFT. In the latter case, we rigorously recover the non-isothermal extension of the Landau-Lifshitz fluctuating Navier-Stokes equations, providing fundamental derivation of this well-known phenomenological model, enabling to clarify its underlying principles.

</details>


### [125] [Motility-Induced Phase Separation of Run-and-Tumble Particles under Local Stochastic Resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26149)
*Pascal Grange*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究一维run-and-tumble活性气体在原点局部随机重置下的非平衡稳态：超过临界重置率后系统形成以原点为中心的液滴-气体两相共存态，给出Lambert函数形式的精确密度剖面，液滴尺寸随重置率非单调变化并存在极大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多关注全局重置，而全局重置会破坏活性系统的大尺度自组织；局部重置可在原点注入质量通量的同时保留MIPS的空间结构，但其对MIPS稳态与相分离形态的影响尚不清楚，故需系统研究局部随机重置下活性气体的非平衡稳态与液滴尺寸调控机制。

Method: 以经历MIPS的一维RTP活性气体为模型，在原点施加局部随机重置；基于动理学演化方程求解稳态，得到用Lambert W函数表示的精确密度剖面；分析过饱和气相动力学稳定性与液滴弛豫过程，并将框架推广至有限重置速度情形。

Result: 存在临界重置率r_c，超过后系统转变为以原点为中心的高密度液滴与周围气相共存的两相稳态；液滴尺寸对重置率r呈非单调依赖并取极大值；成核液滴以1/r的弛豫时间尺度指数弛豫至稳态尺寸；推广至有限重置速度后，稳态液滴尺寸与重置速度无关。

Conclusion: 局部随机重置可作为调控MIPS相分离形态的可控参数：既维持质量注入又不破坏大尺度自组织，且液滴尺寸可通过重置率非单调调节、与重置速度无关，为活性物质中液滴尺寸的稳态控制提供解析依据。

Abstract: We investigate the non-equilibrium stationary states of a one-dimensional active gas composed of run-and-tumble particles (RTPs) undergoing motility-induced phase separation (MIPS) and subjected to local stochastic resetting at the origin. Unlike global resetting, local resetting maintains a mass-injection flux at the origin while preserving large-scale spatial self-organization. We demonstrate that above a critical resetting rate $r_c$, the active system undergoes a transition toward a stationary two-phase state featuring a dense liquid droplet centered at the origin coexisting with a surrounding gas phase. Using kinetic evolution equations, we derive the exact steady-state density profile expressed in terms of the Lambert function. The size of the droplet has a non-monotonic dependence on the resetting rate $r$, and attains its maximum. Furthermore, we analyze the dynamical stability of the supersaturated gas phase and show that a nucleated droplet relaxes exponentially toward its steady-state size with a relaxation timescale of $1/r$. We extend our framework to model a finite resetting velocity, proving that the stationary droplet size is invariant with respect to the resetting velocity.

</details>


### [126] [Poisson-shot-noise induced escape dynamics of a passive particle and a dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26316)
*Gemeit Goel,Rahul Marathe*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了热浴+活性浴中双稳势里单布朗粒子和二聚体的平均首次通过时间(MFPT)，小踢变近似下给出解析标度，一般情形数值求解，并可通过选取合适中间势垒优化MFPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性噪声(Poisson踢)下被动粒子的MFPT动力学是新方向；同时受"加速逃逸机制"相关研究启发，探索中间势垒对逃逸速率的调控潜力。

Method: 系统：单布朗粒子与弹簧连接的二聚体，均处于热浴+活性浴（Poisson分布踢）并囚于双稳势。仅在小踢方差近似下解析：弱噪声用Arrhenius标度、强噪声用有效噪声强度幂律。一般情形数值求解。二聚体在小键强区用顺序跳跃机制，大键强区用绝热消去法。另考察中间势垒情形。

Result: 给出弱/强噪声两标度区间的解析MFPT；二聚体在两种键强极限下分别获得顺序跳跃与绝热消去的MFPT表达式；数值结果表明中间势垒可增强并进一步优化MFPT。

Conclusion: 小踢方差近似下MFPT的标度行为清晰可辨；选取合适的中间势垒剖面是提升与优化逃逸速率的有效手段。

Abstract: Mean First Passage Time (MFPT) is a crucial quantity that characterizes stochastic processes in various stochastic dynamical systems across different disciplines. The inverse of the MFPT, known as the escape rate, is helpful in the context of Brownian motion and chemical processes. The MFPT dynamics of a passive particle in the presence of active noises presents a fascinating avenue. In this work, we study the dynamics of two different systems: first, a single Brownian particle and second, a dimer (two Brownian particles connected by a spring). In both cases, the systems are kept in contact with a thermal bath and an active bath, and are trapped in a bistable potential. The active bath produces kicks that follow the Poisson distribution. The MFPT of the particle and the dimer is investigated in various scenarios. Analytical results are presented for the small kick variance approximation only. In this approximation, we formulate the MFPT in two scaling regimes: a weak-noise regime characterized by classical Arrhenius scaling for escape rate and a strong-noise regime in which the MFPT scales as a power law in the effective noise strength. For the general case, the problem is solved numerically. Next, under the same approximation, the MFPT of a dimer in the small-bond-strength regime is studied using a sequential-hopping mechanism, and in the large-bond-strength regime, the MFPT is formulated using the adiabatic elimination approach. Motivated by recent studies on speeding up the escape mechanism, the MFPT of the particle and the dimer in the presence of an intermediate barrier is also examined. We deduced from the numerical results that the MFPT can be enhanced and further optimized by selecting a suitable intermediate potential profile.

</details>


### [127] [An infinite sequence of higher order Ehrenfest transitions in a one-dimensional crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26371)
*Sakineh Mizani,Szabolcs Varga,Martin Oettel,Péter Gurin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可精确求解的一维模型，展示任意 n≥2 阶的 Ehrenfest 相变序列；该类相变无对称性破缺，二阶相变亦缺乏常规临界行为，但关联长度对密度的导数发散，从而揭示了一类独特的非临界相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶 Ehrenfest 相变极为罕见且理解不足，其分类适用范围与物理内涵尚不清晰，需要可解析处理的范例加以厘清。

Method: 构造并精确求解一个一维模型，使其相变序列可按 Ehrenfest 分类达到任意 n≥2 阶，并分析其对称性与临界性质。

Result: 模型出现任意阶的 Ehrenfest 相变序列；相变不伴随对称性破缺，二阶相变无常规临界行为，但关联长度对密度的导数发散。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在一类区别于常规临界现象的"非临界相变"，有助于界定 Ehrenfest 分类的适用边界。

Abstract: Higher-order Ehrenfest phase transitions are exceptionally rare and poorly understood. We present an exactly solvable one-dimensional model exhibiting a sequence of Ehrenfest transitions of arbitrary $n\geq2$ order. These transitions occur without symmetry breaking and even the second-order transition lacks conventional critical behavior, although the density derivative of the correlation length diverges. Our results reveal a distinct class of noncritical phase transitions and help clarify the scope of the Ehrenfest classification.

</details>


### [128] [Boundary-Sensitive Quantum Order on Non-Amenable Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26859)
*Xingzhi Wang,Zohar Nussinov,Gerardo Ortiz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文证明非amenable晶格（边界占系统比例可观的晶格）上的量子系统可呈现无欧几里得对应物的新型体序。作者结合边界保留的iDMRG与精确对称固定对偶，研究配位数3的无限Cayley树上p<5的态clock模型，发现在有序相与无序相之间存在一个涌现的中间相，其体序由任意弱的边界场诱导而非传统自发对称破缺产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Landau自发对称破缺范式假定体序由体内相互作用自发产生。在边界占系统有限比例的晶格（如无限Cayley树）上，边界的影响不可忽略，作者希望探索边界能否作为一种独立的序参量诱导机制，从而检验并突破Landau范式。

Method: 采用边界保留的无限密度矩阵重整化群（boundary-retaining iDMRG）处理配位数3的无限Cayley树上的p<5态clock模型，并引入精确的对称固定对偶（symmetry-fixing duality）以简化分析、定位相变与中间相。

Result: 在有序相与无序相之间发现一个涌现的中间相；该相的体序由任意弱的边界场诱导，而非体内自发对称破缺。该中间相实现体—边对应：未极化的边界选择特定的有序体态。

Conclusion: 边界敏感序（boundary-sensitive order）作为一种独特的量子序机制成立，独立于Landau自发对称破缺范式，为体—边对应提供新范例。

Abstract: We show that quantum systems on non-amenable lattices, whose boundaries constitute an extensive fraction of the system, host novel forms of bulk ordering with no Euclidean counterpart. Combining boundary-retaining iDMRG with an exact symmetry-fixing duality, we investigate $p<5$ state clock models on the coordination-3 infinite Cayley tree. We find that between the ordered and disordered phases lies an emergent intermediate phase with bulk order induced by an arbitrarily weak boundary field rather than by conventional spontaneous symmetry-breaking. This establishes boundary-sensitive order as a distinct quantum ordering mechanism defying Landau's spontaneous symmetry-breaking paradigm. The intermediate phase realizes a bulk-boundary correspondence in which the unpolarized boundary selects a specific ordered bulk state.

</details>


### [129] [Coexistence phase of the two-dimensional Vicsek model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26561)
*Yu Duan,Hugues Chaté,Benoît Mahault,Xia-qing Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: Vicsek 模型的共存相中存在多种带（band）模式，其中若干为首次观测；但多数在无限尺寸极限下消失。局域扰动可破坏这些规则且全局有序的带状构型，表明共存相至少部分亚稳，其渐近态应为由混沌相互作用的行波带构成的无序相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vicsek 模型是活性物质统计物理研究的核心模型，近期发现著名的 Toner-Tu 群集相存在部分亚稳性等意外现象，促使作者重新审视共存相的相结构与其渐近行为。

Method: 在 Vicsek 模型的共存相中系统考察带模式的多样性，并检验有限尺寸效应（外推至无限尺寸极限）；进一步引入局域扰动，测试这些规则有序带状构型的稳定性。

Result: 观测到多种带模式，部分此前未被报道；多数模式在无限尺寸极限下消失，但仍给出与以往预期不同的相图图像。局域扰动可摧毁规则、全局有序的带状构型，显示共存相至少部分亚稳。

Conclusion: 共存相的渐近态应为无序相，由多种混沌相互作用的行波带组成，而非稳定的规则有序带结构。

Abstract: The Vicsek model continues to be central in statistical physics studies of active matter, with major recent surprises such as the partial metastability of the famous Toner-Tu flocking phase. Here we report on the diversity of band patterns observed in its coexistence phase --some of them never observed before--, but we also argue that most disappear in the infinite-size limit, leaving nevertheless a different picture than anticipated previously. We finally show that these rather regular, globally ordered band configurations can be destroyed by a localized perturbation: the coexistence phase is also at least partially metastable, and thus must be, asymptotically, a disordered phase made of a variety of chaotically interacting traveling bands.

</details>


### [130] [Phase Diagram of the Two-Dimensional Vicsek Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26569)
*Yu Duan,Hugues Chaté,Benoît Mahault,Xia-qing Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文对二维Vicsek模型的相图进行了重大更新，基于前所未有的长尺度与长时间数值模拟，表明存在多种相分离机制，共存相被细分为两部分，并刻画了均匀Toner-Tu液相亚稳区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对Vicsek模型的知识存在空白，尤其是此前提出的相图（如仅含简单微观相分离的图像）可能不完整，需要在大尺度、长时间模拟基础上加以检验与修正。

Method: 对二维Vicsek模型进行综合数值模拟，模拟的体系长度与时间尺度远超以往研究，并据此重新绘制相图。

Result: 发现多种相分离情形并存，而非文献[PRL 114, 068101 (2015)]所提出的单一微观相分离；共存相被分为两个子区域；同时确定了均匀Toner-Tu液相的亚稳区域范围。

Conclusion: 二维Vicsek模型相图比以往认识更复杂：多种相分离场景共同作用，共存相内部结构需细分，且均匀Toner-Tu液相存在可界定的亚稳区，为后续理论分析提供新基准。

Abstract: Motivated by gaps in our current knowledge of the Vicsek model, we present a major update of its phase diagram in two space dimensions, summarizing comprehensive numerical simulations at length- and timescales never explored before. Our main conclusions are that multiple phase separation scenarios are at play, not just the simple microphase separation put forward in [Phys. Rev. Lett. 114, 068101 (2015)], and that the coexistence phase is subdivided in two. We also map out the region of metastability of the homogeneous Toner-Tu liquid phase.

</details>


### [131] [Avalanches and Aging Driven by Parametric Amplification in Kerr Oscillator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26573)
*Alejandro S. Gómez,Javier del Pino*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: KPO网络在瞬态动力学中可出现类似玻璃态弛豫雪崩的激活级联，且网络会"老化"：激活间隔几何增长、激活数目随时间仅对数增长；加性噪声为种子设下限，优先缩短最长驻留时间而不破坏激活序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPO网络（超导、纳米机械、光子阵列）依赖稳态景观用于Ising机、玻色纠错码与传感，但系统如何从给定初态经瞬态到达这些稳态在很大程度上尚未被研究。

Method: 研究具非线性耦合的KPO网络瞬态动力学：参数增益先激活失谐量可被克服的模式，其非线性频移再把休眠模式逐个拉入增益窗口；休眠模式等待期间持续衰减，故每次激活的种子更小。以20模式网络做数值刻画，并引入加性噪声检验鲁棒性。

Result: 观察到类玻璃态弛豫的放大雪崩；激活间隔几何增长（20模式网络中每个驻留约为前一个的两倍），激活数目随时间仅对数累积；加性噪声为种子设下限、使网络"脱困"，最先缩短最长驻留时间，而激活序列在高于数个数量的噪声下仍保留。

Conclusion: 瞬态动力学而非仅稳态景观决定KPO网络的收敛路径；老化虽延迟趋稳，但噪声可加速收敛且不破坏激活顺序，为Ising机与纠错码的初始化/收敛时间设计提供依据。

Abstract: Gain, loss, and nonlinearity shape the phase-space landscape of driven-dissipative systems, such as Kerr parametric oscillator (KPO) networks realized in superconducting, nanomechanical, and photonic arrays. Networks of KPOs exploit the stationary states of this landscape for Ising machines, bosonic error-correcting codes, and sensing. Yet how transients reach these states from a given initial condition remains largely unexplored. Here we show that KPO networks with nonlinear couplings can develop avalanches in their transient dynamics: rapid cascades of amplification between modes reminiscent of the relaxation avalanches of glassy systems. The mechanism is that parametric gain first activates the modes whose detuning it can overcome, and their nonlinear frequency shifts then pull dormant modes into the gain window one at a time. Dormant modes keep decaying while they wait for their turn, so every activation starts from a smaller seed and the intervals between activations grow geometrically. The network thus ages: the longer it has evolved, the slower it changes, and activations accumulate only logarithmically in time. In a 20-mode network, each dwell lasts about twice as long as the previous one. While aging delays the approach to the stationary state, additive noise sets a floor on the seeds and unsticks the network, shortening the longest dwells first while the activation sequence survives to noise several orders of magnitude stronger.

</details>


### [132] [From particles to continuum theories in effective models of self-propelled rods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26593)
*Elia Bronzo,Hugues Chaté,Benoît Mahault,Alexandre Solon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种计入棒状几何的Vicsek型自驱动棒模型（速度向列对齐+两种排斥），作为易于粗粒化的起点，并推导出与粒子层面定性一致的流体力学方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞自驱动棒（如滑行细菌、被分子马达驱动的生物丝）的前期研究多限于二维，涉及大量参数、相图复杂；而显式棒模型的粗粒化极为困难，缺乏忠实于这些唯象行为的连续介质理论。

Method: 构建以"有效"方式体现棒几何的粒子系综：对速度施加向列对齐，并引入两类排斥相互作用，形成简化的Vicsek类模型；据此进行系统性粗粒化，推导流体力学方程。

Result: 所导出的流体力学方程具有与粒子模型定性一致的解，验证了粗粒化过程的保真性。

Conclusion: 该简化Vicsek型棒模型可作为系统粗粒化的可行起点，为中观尺度连续理论提供与粒子层面吻合的框架。

Abstract: Models of colliding self-propelled rods are abstractions of many real systems such as gliding bacteria and biofilaments displaced by molecular motors. They have been studied mostly in two space dimensions and shown to display a wealth of different collective regimes with many parameters involved in the ensuing phase diagrams.
