Continuum-level theories faithful to this phenomenology are largely lacking since coarse-graining models of explicit rods is notoriously difficult. Here we study collections of particles in which the rods geometry is taken into account effectively, by implementing the nematic alignment of their velocities and two types of repulsion. These Vicsek-style models are simple enough to be starting points for systematic coarse-graining. We thus derive hydrodynamic equations that we show to have solutions qualitatively faithful to their particle-level origin.

</details>


### [133] [Statistical decision theory in thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26617)
*Yoshihiko Hasegawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文把统计决策理论引入随机热力学系统，用Le Cam两点法给出极小极大风险的下界，并将其与动力学活性、熵产生联系起来，导出涉及动力学活性或熵产生的不确定性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计、假设检验与分类等统计任务以及感知、不确定关系在随机热力学中通常被分别研究，缺乏统一框架；作者希望用统计决策理论统一刻画感知、分类与不确定性关系，并给出在最优估计器下、最坏参数情形中可达最小期望损失（极小极大风险）的普适下界。

Method: 在随机热力学框架下引入统计决策理论，聚焦极小极大风险；借助Le Cam两点法（two-point method）推导极小极大风险的下界，使其适用于更广泛的热力学场景；并以马尔可夫过程中的分类与分布估计问题作为示例进行具体分析，再考察特定动力学情形。

Result: 在马尔可夫过程的分类与分布估计问题中，证明极小极大风险存在由动力学活性（dynamical activity）给出的下界；进一步考察特定动力学时，所得界可简化为涉及动力学活性或熵产生的不确定性关系。

Conclusion: 统计决策理论可作为随机热力学中统一感知、分类与不确定性关系的有效工具；极小极大风险经Le Cam两点法得到的下界具有较广适用性，并可由动力学活性与熵产生等热力学量刻画。

Abstract: We introduce statistical decision theory in stochastic thermodynamic systems. Statistical decision theory integrates several statistical tasks, such as estimation, hypothesis testing, and classification. In this study, we consider statistical decision theory in stochastic thermodynamics, which unifies sensing, classification, and uncertainty relations. In particular, we focus on the minimax risk, the smallest achievable expected loss under the worst-case parameter choice, even with an optimal estimator. We derive lower bounds on the minimax risk using Le Cam's two-point method. The derived relationships can be used across a broader range of thermodynamic scenarios. To illustrate these relations, we study classification and distribution estimation problems in a Markov process. We demonstrate that the minimax risk provides lower bounds given by the dynamical activity in these problems. Furthermore, by examining particular dynamics, we establish that the resulting bounds simplify to uncertainty relations involving dynamical activity or entropy production.

</details>


### [134] [Nucleation-type criticality with infinite number of free-energy minima beyond mean-field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26638)
*Jun Takahashi,Yoshihiko Nishikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了含Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用与Potts型各向异性的三维Heisenberg模型的相图，发现"绕转相—铁磁相"转变呈现物理量连续但临界指数类似一级转变的反常临界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将de Gennes的成核型转变理论与Dzyaloshinskii关于手性磁体的计算统一起来，并在平均场之外的微观模型中检验这种反常临界性是否真实存在。

Method: 对三维Heisenberg模型（含DM相互作用及Potts型各向异性）进行数值模拟相图研究，提取临界指数并统计自由能局域极小数目随参数的变化。

Result: 绕转相与铁磁有序相之间的相变虽保持物理量连续，但临界指数与一级转变一致；在临界点处自由能局域极小数目不断增加，支持"无穷多个不连续转变叠加形成临界性"的图像。

Conclusion: 此类反常临界性可在超越平均场的微观模型中实现，从而把成核型转变理论与手性磁体计算统一在同一框架内。

Abstract: We study the phase diagram of a three-dimensional Heisenberg model with Dzyaloshinski--Moriya interaction under the presence of Potts-like anisotropy. The phase transition between the winding phase and the ferromagnetically ordered phase exhibits a critical phase transition with anomalous features such as critical exponents consistent with that of a first-order transition while maintaining continuity of physical quantities. Furthermore, at the critical point, we see a growing number of free energy local minima, supporting the picture of infinitely many discontinuous transitions forming criticality. Our numerical result unify de Gennes' theory of nucleation-type transitions and Dzyaloshinskii's calculation of chiral magnets by demonstrating that such anomalous criticality can be realized in microscopic models beyond mean-field.

</details>


### [135] [Phase-sensitive quantum-state texture during thermalization of a Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya Heisenberg dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26736)
*Onofre Rojas,Zhirayr Adamyan,Fadwa Benabdallah,Vadim Ohanyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究各向异性spin-1/2 Heisenberg二聚体在纵向磁场与DM相互作用下热弛豫过程中的量子态"纹理"(texture)，构建弱耦合Lindblad方程，揭示DM耦合对相干性与弛豫瞬态的控制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子热化过程中态结构（纹理）的刻画与相干性、纯度等标准量之间的关系尚不清晰，尤其在DM相互作用与磁场存在时缺乏相位敏感的弛豫诊断工具。

Method: 从局域横向与纵向玻色浴耦合出发构建全局弱耦合Lindblad方程，识别跃迁通道并讨论secular近似适用性；在局域自旋基下显式求解Gibbs态；对加热与冷却协议比较rugosity、基于fidelity的texture与trace-distance texture，并与相干性、矢量自旋手性与纯度对照。

Result: DM耦合通过改变能级劈裂并旋转手性相位，同时调控单激发相干性的模与相位；布居热化与能量基相干性的衰减/进动分离，其叠加导致DM依赖的texture过冲，冷却时出现阻尼振荡与相干正交分量符号变化；trace-distance texture对瞬态的权重与基于重叠的量不同；约化耗散下secular模型仍保留texture极大值。

Conclusion: texture提供了对相位敏感的弛豫诊断量，其瞬态行为区别于相干性模与纯度的演化，可作为量子热化过程的结构性表征工具。

Abstract: We investigate quantum-state texture during the thermal relaxation of an anisotropic spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg dimer subject to a longitudinal magnetic field and a Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interaction. Starting from local transverse and longitudinal couplings to a bosonic bath, we construct a global weak-coupling Lindblad equation, identify its transition channels, and discuss the applicability and limitations of the secular approximation. The Gibbs state is obtained explicitly in the local spin basis, revealing that the DM coupling controls both the modulus and phase of the single-excitation coherence by modifying the energy splitting and rotating the chiral phase. For heating and cooling protocols, we compare rugosity, fidelity-based texture, and trace-distance texture with coherence, vector spin chirality, and purity. The relaxation dynamics separates population thermalization from the decay and precession of deliberately prepared energy-basis coherence. Their combined contribution to local coherence produces DM-dependent texture overshoots and, during cooling, damped oscillations and sign changes of the coherence quadratures. These transients differ from the evolution of coherence magnitude and purity, while trace-distance texture weights them differently from the overlap-based quantifiers. Calculations at reduced dissipation preserve the texture maxima within the secular model. The results identify a phase-sensitive diagnostic of relaxation.

</details>


### [136] [A McLennan formula for equiaccessible steady states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26755)
*Jacob Calvert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文把McLennan公式的展开基准从平衡态改为等可达稳态，从而得到一个可适用于任意远离平衡情形的类比公式：零阶稳态占据率仅由局部逃逸速率决定（"低抖动"原理的强形式），一阶修正则刻画流量失衡对状态可达性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典McLennan公式用瞬态不可逆熵流修正玻尔兹曼分布，但其成立前提是系统接近平衡，无法描述远离平衡的稳态行为。作者希望找到一种不依赖近平衡假设的展开框架，以刻画远离平衡时稳态占据率的决定因素。

Method: 以等可达稳态（equiaccessible steady states）为展开中心而非平衡态，对占据概率进行逐阶展开，并逐阶分析各项的物理含义。

Result: 零阶结果给出稳态占据仅由局部逃逸速率决定，构成活性物质"低抖动"原理的强形式；一阶修正量化流量（traffic）失衡对各状态可达性的影响，标志着相对占据的非局域决定因素开始出现。

Conclusion: 通过在等可达稳态附近展开，可获得McLennan公式的远平衡类比，将稳态占据分解为局部逃逸速率主导项与非局域流量失衡修正项，为远离平衡稳态的占据规律提供了系统性的展开框架。

Abstract: Near equilibrium, the Boltzmann distribution is corrected by the transient irreversible entropy flux, a result known as the McLennan formula. By expanding around equiaccessible steady states instead, we derive an analogue that can hold arbitrarily far from equilibrium. At zeroth order, steady-state occupation is determined solely by local escape rates, yielding a strong form of the active-matter principle of "low rattling." The first-order correction measures the impact of traffic imbalances on the accessibility of each state, marking the onset of nonlocal determinants of relative occupation.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Quantifying the Kinetic and Thermodynamic Origins of the Glass-Forming Ability in Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24101)
*Weiwei Jin,Jinpeng Fan,Mark D. Shattuck,Corey S. O'Hern*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文计算模型纯金属与BCC/B2、FCC合金的临界冷却速率R_c与结晶速率K_X=(IU^3)^{1/4}，发现二者在十一个数量级上强正相关，且热力学贡献主导动力学贡献，玻璃形成能力由约化界面能与约化驱动力ΔG_c~γ̃³/Δμ̃²控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃形成能力（GFA）可由临界冷却速率R_c量化，而R_c取决于冷却速率R与结晶速率K_X的竞争。作者希望厘清K_X中各物理因素的相对权重，从而理解何种机制（动力学或热力学、界面能或体驱动力）真正决定金属液体的GFA。

Method: 在BCC/B2与FCC平衡结构的模型纯金属及合金中，基于经典成核理论（CNT）并独立计算晶体生长速率，在T=0.8T_m处评估K_X=(IU^3)^{1/4}（I为成核率、U为生长率）；将K_X分解为动力学贡献（原子团簇附着与扩散）与热力学贡献，并用约化界面自由能γ̃与约化体驱动力Δμ̃分析成核势垒ΔG_c~γ̃³/Δμ̃²；辅以结构序与化学序分析。

Result: K_X与R_c在十一个数量级范围内呈强正相关；在所考察的冷却速率区间内，热力学贡献主导动力学贡献；成核势垒主导热力学项的大小；γ̃与Δμ̃的相对重要性依材料而异——界面能大可在部分体系抑制成核，体驱动力弱则在另一些体系中抑制结晶。更好的玻璃形成体具有更强的结构阻挫、更多类二十面体且更少类晶体的局域环境，以及超冷液体与晶相之间更大的化学序失配。

Conclusion: K_X可作为GFA的可靠量化指标（与R_c跨十一个数量级强相关），且其大小主要由热力学（成核势垒）而非动力学因素决定；GFA的微观起源在于结构阻挫、局域结构特征与化学序失配，二者共同调控界面能与体驱动力。

Abstract: The glass-forming ability (GFA) of a metallic liquid can be quantified by the critical cooling rate $R_c$, below which it crystallizes upon cooling. $R_c$ is determined by the competition between $R$ and the crystallization rate $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$. Here, we calculate $R_c$ and $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ in model pure metals and alloys with BCC/B2 and FCC equilibrium crystal structures. Using classical nucleation theory and independently calculated crystal growth rates, we evaluate $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}=(IU^3)^{1/4}$ at $T=0.8T_m$, where $I$ and $U$ are the nucleation and growth rates and $T_m$ is the melting temperature. We find a strong positive correlation between $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ and $R_c$ over eleven orders of magnitude. We decompose $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ into a product of a kinetic contribution, involving atomic cluster attachment and diffusion, and a thermodynamic contribution. We show that the thermodynamic contribution to $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ dominates the kinetic contribution over the range of cooling rates that we consider. The nucleation barrier $ΔG_c \sim \tildeγ^3/Δ\tildeμ^2$, where $\tildeγ$ is the reduced effective liquid--crystal interfacial free energy and $Δ\tildeμ$ is the reduced bulk driving force, determines the magnitude of the thermodynamic contribution. The relative importance of $\tildeγ$ and $Δ\tildeμ$ depends on the material: a large interfacial free energy can suppress nucleation in some systems, whereas a weak bulk driving force can inhibit crystallization in others. Analyses of the structural and chemical order show that better glass formers have enhanced structural frustration, more icosahedral-like and less crystal-like local environments, and a larger chemical-order mismatch between the supercooled liquid and crystal phases.

</details>


### [138] [Signed FK percolation in Ising spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24629)
*Alberto Gandolfi,Charles Newman,Danile Stein*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 引入符号 Fortuin-Kasteleyn (FK) 渗流来刻画 ±J 随机相互作用 Ising 模型的非零均匀磁化密度与双副本重叠,以"至多一个无穷 FK 簇"为假设,将磁化与重叠的非零性等价于伴生对象的密度可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无穷体积 Edwards-Sokal/FK 表示至多存在一个无穷 FK 簇的 Gibbs 分布类中,需要一个能同时刻画非零均匀磁化密度与两副本重叠的连通性工具,而普通 FK 连通性无法保留耦合符号信息。

Method: 构造符号 FK 渗流,在普通 FK 连通性基础上保留开路径上耦合乘积的宇称;利用无穷体积 ES 表示及其 DLR 方程研究伴生对象对及其上相容自旋赋值。单副本中以正宇称 FK 连接定义伴生对象;双副本中以宇称相干的 FK 连接定义伴生对象。

Result: 对满足无穷簇假设且沿所选 van Hove 序列伴生密度几乎必然存在的极端态:非零极限期望磁化密度等价于无穷 FK 簇伴生对象的密度可区分性;对两独立副本乘积态,伴生对象密度可区分性等价于非零极限期望互重叠,同态自重叠即 Edwards-Anderson 自重叠。

Conclusion: 符号 FK 渗流把磁化与重叠的非零性统一转化为无穷 FK 簇伴生对象的密度可区分性问题,为随机相互作用 Ising 模型的相变刻画提供了渗流几何框架。

Abstract: We introduce signed Fortuin-Kasteleyn (FK) percolation for the +/-J random-interaction Ising model to characterize nonzero uniform magnetization density and two-replica overlap within the class of Gibbs distributions whose infinite-volume Edwards-Sokal (ES)/FK representation has at most one infinite FK cluster almost surely. The construction refines ordinary FK connectivity by retaining the parity of coupling products along open paths. Using the infinite-volume ES representation and its DLR equations, we study pairs of companion objects and the compatible spin assignments on them. In one replica, companions are defined by positive-parity FK connections. For extremal states satisfying the infinite-cluster assumption and almost-sure existence of companion densities along the chosen van Hove sequence, nonzero limiting expected magnetization density is equivalent to density distinguishability of the companions of the infinite FK cluster. For two independent replicas, companions are defined by FK connections coherent in parity. For products of extremal one-replica states satisfying the infinite-cluster assumption and almost-sure existence of the companion densities along the chosen sequence, density distinguishability of the companions is equivalent to nonzero limiting expected mutual overlap. For two independent replicas drawn from the same extremal state, this specializes to the Edwards-Anderson self-overlap.

</details>


### [139] [Synchronous and asynchronous Monte Carlo dynamics of mean-field spin glasses with rotationally invariant couplings: what they share and where they differ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24942)
*Yoshiyuki Kabashima*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 比较同步Monte Carlo（SyncMC，借助高斯辅助场并行更新全部自旋）与异步Metropolis-Hastings动力学在旋转不变耦合Ising自旋玻璃中的行为，利用Guionnet-Maïda公式以耦合谱的R变换推导二者的动力学平均场理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步与异步MCMC动力学是否给出相同的慢时间尺度物理（涨落-耗散比、Parisi方程等）此前缺乏系统的动力学平均场比较。

Method: 基于Guionnet-Maïda公式，用耦合谱的R变换推导两种动力学的动力学平均场理论；给出各自的有效单自旋过程；并对随机正交模型与Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 两种有效单自旋过程均含延迟自相互作用与高斯噪声；SyncMC另有瞬态自耦合，且噪声仅依赖响应函数。这些附加项只作用于最快时间尺度，因此慢时间尺度方程（含涨落-耗散比；对SK模型含Parisi方程）在两种动力学下完全相同。模拟证实两者趋于相同的涨落-耗散关系，仅所需更新次数不同。

Conclusion: SyncMC与异步Metropolis-Hastings在慢时间尺度上动力学等价，同步更新引入的额外项不改变长时物理，仅影响收敛效率。

Abstract: We compare two Markov chain Monte Carlo dynamics for Ising spin glasses with rotationally invariant couplings: the synchronous Monte Carlo method (SyncMC), which updates all spins in parallel with the help of Gaussian auxiliary fields, and asynchronous Metropolis-Hastings dynamics, which updates one spin at a time. Using the Guionnet-Maïda formula, we derive the dynamical mean-field theory of both dynamics in terms of the R-transform of the coupling spectrum. The two effective single-spin processes share a retarded self-interaction and a Gaussian noise; SyncMC has, in addition, an instantaneous self-coupling and a noise that depends only on the response function. These additional terms act only on the fastest time scale, so that the equations for slow time scales, including the fluctuation-dissipation ratio and, for the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model, the Parisi equation, are the same for the two dynamics. Simulations of the random orthogonal and Sherrington-Kirkpatrick models confirm that both dynamics approach the same fluctuation-dissipation relations, differing only in how many updates they need to do so.

</details>


### [140] [Random spin-3/2 antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chains revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24994)
*Yen-Tung Lin,Pochung Chen,Yu-Cheng Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 利用张量网络强无序重整化群(tSDRG)重新研究随机spin-3/2反铁磁Heisenberg链的自旋约化相变，推导P_n普适类的临界指数并据此定位P_4多临界点及RS相的重入行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机spin-3/2反铁磁Heisenberg链中预测存在由有效spin-1/2矩(弱无序)构成与由完整spin-3/2矩(强无序)构成的两种随机单态(RS)相之间的自旋约化相变；在Damle–Huse分类下该相变对应P_2临界线上的涌现置换对称P_4多临界点，需要定量刻画其普适性与相图。

Method: 采用张量网络强无序重整化群(tSDRG)；基于Damle–Huse框架解析推导P_n普适类下无序平均的体关联函数与端到端关联函数的临界指数；并构造由能隙分布均值与方差组成的诊断量用于定位相变点。

Result: 给出了P_n普适类中无序平均体关联与端到端关联的临界指数理论预测；结合能隙分布均值-方差诊断，成功识别出P_4相变点，并观测到RS相的重入行为。

Conclusion: 数值tSDRG结果与Damle–Huse理论预测一致，证实随机spin-3/2链中存在P_4多临界点及RS相重入，能隙分布统计可作为判别RS相与定位多临界点的有效诊断工具。

Abstract: We revisit the spin-reduction transition in the random spin-$3/2$ antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chain using the tensor-network strong-disorder renormalization group (tSDRG). The predicted transition separates two random-singlet (RS) phases: one formed by effective spin-$1/2$ moments at weaker disorder and the other by full spin-$3/2$ moments at stronger disorder. Within the Damle--Huse classification, this transition corresponds to an emergent permutation-symmetric $\mathcal{P}_4$ multicritical point on a $\mathcal{P}_2$ critical line associated with the RS phases. We first derive the critical exponents governing the disorder-averaged bulk and end-to-end correlation functions in the $\mathcal{P}_n$ universality classes within the Damle--Huse framework. Using these theoretical predictions, together with a diagnostic constructed from the mean and variance of the energy-gap distribution, we identify the $\mathcal{P}_4$ transition point and the reentrant behavior of the RS phase.

</details>


### [141] [Higher-order disorder correlations control reflected phonon counting statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25153)
*Ara Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究一维声学叠层中超越功率谱的结构关联（高阶统计量）如何影响声子计数，发现反射功率的归一化二阶矩趋于 (μ₄+5)/4，双声子 Fock 输入下集合平均反聚束关联为 g⁽²⁾=(μ₄+5)/8，并构造出具有相同两点关联但 g⁽²⁾ 分居泊松值两侧的两个集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无序/随机介质理论通常由功率谱（两点关联）决定输运性质，但功率谱不包含全部结构信息。作者希望检验：在相同功率谱（相同均值和完整两点关联函数）条件下，结构中的高阶关联是否能改变声子计数统计，从而揭示超越功率谱的结构相关性对量子/统计输运可观测量的作用。

Method: 采用整数厚度构造（integer-thickness construction）抵消广延性主导背散射项，使有限链反射振幅由端点"创新"（endpoint innovations）决定；对对称独立、单位方差、四阶矩为 μ₄ 的创新分布解析计算归一化反射功率二阶矩；通过条件张量平均（conditional tensor averaging）给出任意阻抗对比下四阶电阻增长系数；并用精确枚举验证固定 N、小失谐极限及 N 增大时的交叉行为；进一步考察带宽、厚度误差、传播损耗与热背景的影响。

Result: 归一化反射功率二阶矩趋于 (μ₄+5)/4；真空环境下双声子 Fock 输入给出集合平均 g⁽²⁾=(μ₄+5)/8。两个构造集合具有相同均值与完整两点关联，却分别得到 g⁽²⁾=3/4 和 9/8，计数方差分布于泊松值两侧。得到含正的高阶散射贡献的四阶电阻增长系数，该贡献无法通过平移最低阶谱恢复。精确枚举确认固定 N 小失谐极限；固定失谐增大 N 时出现由累积高阶体散射支配的交叉，而非收敛到端点公式。

Conclusion: 结构的高阶（超越功率谱）关联确实能显著改变集合平均的声子计数统计，且相同两点关联不足以确定 g⁽²⁾。但该结果仅涉及无序平均的脉冲统计：它既不能从相干输入产生非经典性，也不改变单个可分辨无源样品的条件阶乘关联。

Abstract: We investigate how structural correlations beyond the power spectrum affect phonon counting in a one-dimensional acoustic stack. An integer-thickness construction cancels the extensive leading backscattering term and leaves a finite-chain reflection amplitude determined by endpoint innovations. For symmetric independent innovations with unit variance and fourth moment $μ_4$, the normalized reflected-power second moment tends to $(μ_4+5)/4$. With vacuum environmental inputs, a two-phonon Fock input therefore gives an ensemble-averaged reflected factorial correlation $g^{(2)}=(μ_4+5)/8$. Two explicitly constructed ensembles with identical mean and complete two-point correlation function yield $g^{(2)}=3/4$ and $9/8$, placing their counting variances on opposite sides of the Poisson value. Conditional tensor averaging also gives a general fourth-order resistance-growth coefficient for arbitrary impedance contrast, including a positive higher-scattering contribution not recovered by shifting the lowest-order spectrum. Exact enumeration confirms the fixed-$N$, small-detuning limit and shows that increasing $N$ at fixed detuning produces a crossover governed by accumulated higher-order bulk scattering rather than convergence to the endpoint formula. Further calculations test bandwidth, thickness error, propagation loss and thermal background. The result concerns disorder-averaged pulse statistics; it neither creates nonclassicality from coherent input nor changes the conditional factorial correlation of an individually resolved passive sample.

</details>


### [142] [Signatures of slow particle transport in the interacting Aubry-André chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25557)
*S. Sharifan,M. Kiefer-Emmanouilidis,J. Sirker*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在相互作用 Aubry-André 链中数值研究淬火后纠缠熵 S(t)~μln t、数熵 S_N(t)~νln ln t（μ~ν~D^{-γ}, γ≈3），以及 Hartley 数熵饱和值随 L 以 ln L 增长且至 D≳8 仍成立；准周期与随机无序行为一致，表明慢输运源于不依赖稀有区域雪崩、且不受共振统计改变影响的共同机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强无序多体量子链中的慢离域化可能有两种来源：基于能量统计的稀有共振与雪崩机制，或算符空间中与能量无关的组合路径熵障碍。准周期势可消除被认为是雪崩不稳定性来源的稀有区域并改变共振统计，因而可用于区分这两种机制。

Method: 对相互作用 Aubry-André 链（无序强度 D）进行量子淬火，数值计算纠缠熵 S(t)、数熵 S_N(t) 与 Hartley 数熵 S_H(t) 的动力学，并与先前随机（quenched）无序情形对比，考察增长指数与饱和值随系统尺寸 L 的标度。

Result: S(t)~μln t、S_N(t)~νln ln t，增长率 μ~ν~D^{-γ} 且 γ≈3，与淬火无序情形相同；S_H(t→∞) 随 L 以 ln L 持续增长，且在曾被认为 MBL 稳定的 D≳8 仍持续。

Conclusion: 准周期与淬火无序情形的相似性指向慢粒子输运的共同起源：该机制不依赖稀有区域雪崩，并在共振统计被强烈改变的情况下依然存在，支持算符空间路径熵障碍的解释。

Abstract: Slow delocalization in strongly disordered many-body quantum chains can originate from two distinct mechanisms: rare resonances and avalanches, rooted in the energy statistics, or an energy-independent combinatorial path-entropy obstruction in operator space. Quasiperiodic potentials eliminate rare regions with similar potentials that are thought to be responsible for avalanche instabilities and modify the statistics of resonances, thus allowing us to delineate the two mechanisms. To do so, we numerically study the entanglement entropy $S(t)$, number entropy $S_N(t)$, and Hartley number entropy $S_H(t)$ following a quantum quench in the interacting Aubry-André chain with disorder strength $D$. We find $S(t)\simμ\ln t$, $S_N(t)\simν\ln\ln t$ with growth rates $μ\simν\sim D^{-γ}$ and $γ\approx 3$, the same behavior that was previously found in the case of quenched disorder. Furthermore, the saturation values $S_H(t\to\infty)$ continue to grow with system size $L$ as $\ln L$, a growth that persists for $D\gtrsim 8$ where MBL was argued to be stable. The similarity of the quasi-periodic and quenched disorder cases points to a common origin of the slow particle transport that does not rely on rare-region avalanches and persists despite strongly modified resonance statistics.

</details>


### [143] [Exact cost of the Euclidean Maximum Traveling Salesman Problem in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26536)
*Sergio Caracciolo,Carlo Vanoni*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究一维随机欧氏最大旅行商问题：刻画不同凸性/单调性代价函数下的最优环，并对均匀采样给出任意N的平均最大代价显式表达与幂律代价的渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大TSP在一维且边代价为欧氏长度函数的设定下缺乏系统性刻画：不同代价函数形状（凸增、凹增、凸减）会改变最优环结构，且随机均匀采样下的平均最优值缺少精确与渐近结果。

Method: 将N个城市置于直线（单位区间），边代价取为欧氏距离的函数；按凸增、凹增、凸减三类代价函数分别刻画最大化Hamiltonian环的结构；对独立均匀采样，推导任意N下平均最大代价的显式表达式，并对幂律型代价做渐近展开。

Result: 给出三类代价函数下最大化环的完整刻画；获得任意N的平均最大代价显式公式，以及幂律代价下的渐近展开式。

Conclusion: 一维最大TSP的最优结构由代价函数的凸性与单调性决定，且随机情形下平均最大代价可精确求解并具可解析的渐近行为，为高维最大TSP的启发式与理论分析提供基准。

Abstract: We study the random Euclidean Maximum Traveling Salesman Problem: finding a Hamiltonian cycle of maximal total cost through any set of $N$ cities on a line, where each edge cost is a function of its Euclidean length. We characterize the maximizing cycles for convex increasing, concave increasing, and convex decreasing cost functions. For points sampled independently and uniformly from the unit interval, we derive explicit expressions for the average maximal cost for arbitrary $N$ and asymptotic expansions with power-law edge costs.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

### [144] [Surface corrections and vortex energetics in the Thomas--Fermi limit of a self-bound unitary Fermi superfluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24565)
*Bao-Duy Le*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在扩展Thomas–Fermi密度泛函框架下研究吸引型1/r相互作用的自旋平衡幺正费米超流体，证明基态能量的领头梯度修正与密度幅值的强收敛，并通过表面问题给出涡旋与表面集体激发阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大粒子数极限对应指数3/2的Lane–Emden多方球，但其梯度修正、表面结构及涡旋/表面激发的能量阈值缺乏严格定量刻画，需区分TF体相精度与主导表面和涡旋芯的梯度物理。

Method: 采用扩展Thomas–Fermi密度泛函；证明领头梯度能量修正及密度幅值强收敛；构造重标度表面问题预测次阶能量修正与积分密度误差；将涡旋芯与多方球体相匹配求中心涡旋阈值（含对数后常数）；线性化表面问题给出集体表面激发的局域阈值；辅以径向定态、球对称Bogoliubov–Poisson与轴对称实时计算验证。

Result: 单量子化中心涡旋常数c₁=-0.535（参考梯度系数下）；表面Landau尺度为1.417 ω_K(ℏ_eff²/2R_TF)^{1/6} ∝ N^{-1/9}，随粒子数减少慢于中心涡旋阈值；有限尺寸表面模式极小值追踪至N≈4×10⁶并与连续表面系数吻合；N≈4.6×10⁵的扇区能量与推断涡旋阈值吻合至10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: 结果区分了Thomas–Fermi体相的高精度与控制表面及涡旋芯的梯度物理，并将涡旋能量学与非线性涡旋进入这一开放问题相分离。

Abstract: We study a spin-balanced unitary Fermi superfluid bound by an attractive $1/r$ interaction within an extended Thomas--Fermi density functional. Its large-particle-number limit is the Lane--Emden polytrope of index $3/2$. We prove the leading gradient correction to the ground-state energy and strong convergence of the density amplitude. A rescaled surface problem predicts the next energy correction and the leading integrated density error. Matching a vortex core to the polytropic bulk gives the energetic threshold of a singly quantized central vortex, including the constant beyond the leading logarithm. Linearizing the same surface problem predicts a local energetic threshold for collective surface excitations that, in the natural TF frequency units, decreases more slowly with particle number than the central-vortex threshold. Radial stationary, spherical Bogoliubov--Poisson, and axisymmetric real-time calculations test the predicted energy corrections and vortex matching, and resolve finite-size surface-mode minima whose approach to the continuum surface coefficient is followed to $N\simeq4\times10^6$. For the reference gradient coefficient the vortex constant is $c_1=-0.535$, and the surface Landau scale is $1.417\,ω_K(\hbar_{\rm eff}^2/2R_{\rm TF})^{1/6}\propto N^{-1/9}$; sector energies at $N\simeq4.6\times10^5$, computed after the fit, agree with the extrapolated vortex threshold to $10^{-4}$. These results distinguish the accuracy of the Thomas--Fermi bulk from the gradient physics that controls the surface and vortex core, and separate vortex energetics from the open problem of nonlinear vortex entry.

</details>


### [145] [Self-Trapping and Liouvillian Spectral Transitions in a Dissipative Bose-Hubbard Dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25222)
*Subhendu Saha,Raka Dasgupta*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文用平均场理论和精确对角化研究耗散双势阱中的自束缚转变，发现纯耗散亦可诱导自束缚型局域化，并将该转变与Liouvillian谱的奇异点及谱隙峰值联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自束缚转变由相互作用驱动（振荡→自束缚），作者希望探究纯耗散是否能单独诱导类似局域化，以及相互作用与耗散共存时的竞争机制。

Method: 采用平均场理论结合精确对角化，分析耗散双势阱的动力学相图，并将动力学转变与Liouvillian谱（奇异点、谱隙）对应起来。

Result: 纯耗散可诱导瞬态但持续较长时间的自束缚态；平均场层面相互作用驱动转变为不连续型，耗散驱动转变为连续型；两者共存时出现三种动力学区间（弱相互作用下耗散驱动振荡→自束缚；中等相互作用下自束缚→振荡→再入自束缚；强相互作用下自束缚持续但被削弱）；纯耗散转变对应谱中奇异点与谱隙峰值，标志量子Zeno区起点；有相互作用时Zeno区起点向更强耗散偏移。

Conclusion: 耗散单独即可产生自束缚型局域化，其转变由Liouvillian奇异点刻画；相互作用使量子Zeno区起点与自束缚转变解耦，二者不再重合。

Abstract: We study the self-trapping transition in dissipative double wells using mean-field theory and exact diagonalization. We find that, similar to the conventional interaction-driven transition from oscillatory dynamics to self-trapping, dissipation alone can also induce self-trapping-like localization in one well. The resulting state, although transient and decaying, persists for a substantial time. At the mean-field level, the interaction- and dissipation-driven transitions are discontinuous and continuous, respectively. When interaction and dissipation both are present, their interplay produces three dynamical regimes: (i) at weak interaction, increasing dissipation drives a transition from oscillations to self-trapping; (ii) at moderate interaction, self-trapping at weak dissipation, oscillatory dynamics at intermediate dissipation, and re-entrant self-trapping at strong dissipation; and (iii) at strong interaction, self-trapping persists but is weakened by dissipation. We connect the transition with the Liouvillian spectrum. It is observed that purely dissipation-driven transition coincides with an exceptional point in the spectra, and a peak in the Liouvillian gap, marking the onset of the quantum-Zeno regime. In the presence of interactions, however, the onset of the Zeno regime is shifted to dissipation strengths larger than that required for the self-trapping transition.

</details>


### [146] [Near self-duality and spatial reorganization of anyon vortex lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25412)
*Bao-Duy Le*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出并利用一个正定、规范不变的局域缺陷量（covariant kinetic energy 超出 magnetic bound 的部分），在近玻色 Chern–Simons–Schrödinger 平均场模型中定位任意子气体自对偶性残余偏离的来源：残余缺陷集中于无涡旋的外壳层，表现为统计流与密度衰减之间的平滑径向失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触吸引使囚禁任意子气体的自生涡旋晶格趋向自对偶，但积分能量无法揭示剩余偏离自对偶性的位置。作者旨在回答：在接近稳定线时，残余缺陷究竟分布在何处、以何种形式存在。

Method: 采用正定、规范不变的局域缺陷量，度量超出磁束缚（magnetic bound）的协变动能；在三组统计磁通下数值计算低能态，考察积分缺陷随逼近稳定线的标度行为，并借助精确的膨胀（dilation）与接触响应恒等式，将能量平方与密度形状关联作为一致性检验。

Result: 随稳定线逼近，积分缺陷下降约两个数量级，而密度调制反而增长，说明磁饱和与密度均匀性相分离；残余缺陷集中在携带未屏蔽统计通量的无涡旋外壳，退化为统计流与密度衰减之间的平滑径向失配，而内部晶格无法抵消它；协变动能与振幅梯度能量则未见可比拟的富集。结果对网格与盒子细化稳健，其幅度依赖于磁通与低能分支。排斥作用呈相反趋势：密度更平滑但缺陷更大。

Conclusion: 局域缺陷量揭示自对偶性残余偏离定位于外无涡旋壳层的径向失配，而非晶格内部；磁饱和与密度均匀性可解耦，且相互作用符号定性地反转该趋势，为诊断任意子气体趋近自对偶的机制提供了局域判据。

Abstract: Contact attraction drives the self-generated vortex lattice of a trapped anyon gas towards self-duality, but integrated energies do not reveal where the remaining departure from it is located. We address this question in the almost-bosonic Chern--Simons--Schrödinger average-field model using a positive, gauge-invariant local defect that measures the covariant kinetic energy in excess of the magnetic bound. Numerically computed low-energy states at three statistical fluxes show that, as the stability line is approached, the integrated defect falls by about two orders of magnitude while the density modulation grows, so that magnetic saturation and density uniformity separate. The residual defect concentrates in a vortex-free outer shell that carries unscreened statistical flux. There it reduces to a smooth radial mismatch between the statistical flow and the density decay, which the interior lattice cannot cancel, whereas the covariant kinetic and amplitude-gradient energies show no comparable enrichment. The effect is robust to grid and box refinement, while its magnitude depends on flux and on the low-energy branch. Exact dilation and contact-response identities relate the squared energy to the density shape and provide consistency checks. Repulsion produces the opposite trend: a smoother density with a larger defect.

</details>


### [147] [Two-Dimensional Quantum Droplets in a Mixture of Bose-Einstein Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26449)
*Yue Zhang,Liangchao Chen,Wei Han,Linshan Gong,Yazhou Wang,Zekui Wang,Xinhao Liang,Wenjie Sui,Pengjun Wang,Lianghui Huang,Zengming Meng,Zhuxiong Ye,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作在准二维几何中通过将单组分BEC转化为处于LHY区域的旋量混合态，首次实验实现二维量子液滴，并进一步制备二维Townes孤子，对比表明二维量子液滴在更宽参数范围内自束缚且更稳定、寿命更长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子液滴是超越平均场物理的重要体现且性质强烈依赖维度；一维与三维量子液滴已实验实现，但二维实现一直缺失。同时，二维Townes孤子稳定性受临界原子数严格限制，亟需与二维量子液滴进行对照研究。

Method: 在准二维几何中，将单组分BEC转移为处于Lee-Huang-Yang（LHY）区域的旋量混合态，其中LHY效应明显偏离三维形式；作为对照，将初始BEC转移至具有负散射长度的另一单自旋态以产生二维Townes孤子，并比较两类自束缚态的稳定性与参数范围。

Result: 观察到二维量子液滴形成；该方案下二维量子液滴比此前报道的量子液滴更稳定、寿命更长；二维Townes孤子仅在由散射长度决定的临界原子数附近稳定，而二维量子液滴在更宽参数范围内保持良好定义的自束缚态。

Conclusion: 二维量子液滴与二维Townes孤子的实现加深了对自束缚态的理解，并为量子液体中的非线性动力学研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum droplets represent a striking manifestation of beyond-mean-field physics, with properties that depend sensitively on dimensionality. Although quantum droplets have been experimentally realized in one and three dimensions, their realization in two dimensions has remained elusive. Here, we observe the formation of a two-dimensional (2D) quantum droplet in a quasi-2D geometry by transferring the single-component Bose-Einstein condensates (BECs) into a spin mixture in the Lee-Huang-Yang (LHY) regime, where the LHY effect evidently deviates from its three-dimensional form. We find that, in our scheme, 2D quantum droplets are more stable and exhibit longer lifetimes than previously reported quantum droplets. By instead transferring the initial BECs into another single-spin state with a negative scattering length, we generate 2D Townes solitons. A comparison between the two types of self-bound states shows that, whereas 2D Townes solitons are stable only near a critical atom number determined by the scattering length, 2D quantum droplets remain well-defined and self-bound over a much broader parameter range. These realizations of quantum droplets and Townes solitons in two dimensions provide new insights into self-bound states and open new avenues for exploring nonlinear dynamics in quantum liquids.

</details>


### [148] [Non-local Stabilization and Synchronization of Larmor Precession in Trapped Polariton Condensate Dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26499)
*Stepan Baryshev,Alexey Yulin,Ivan Gnusov,Sergey Alyatkin,Pavlos G. Lagoudakis*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用光镊捕获空间分离的极化激元凝聚体，通过极化激元外流实现拉莫尔进动的相互同步，并证明全光"搅拌"一个节点可将静态邻居的赝自旋退相干时间延长一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非平衡、时间周期驱动系统（如进动耦合振子）的可扩展实验平台，并为高速"自旋光电子"网络寻找可控的相位锁定机制。

Method: 构建光学陷阱中的极化激元凝聚体二聚体（dimer），以交叉凝聚体关联函数 g⁽²⁾₁,₂(τ) 作为耦合敏感度量，研究同步对光谱重叠与前动缠绕（winding）的共振依赖；对单侧节点进行全光搅拌（all-optical stirring）。

Result: 二聚体中拉莫尔进动可经极化激元外流实现同步，且同步共振依赖于两凝聚体的光谱重叠与进动缠绕数；全光驱动一个节点可非局域地延长其静态邻居的赝自旋退相干时间，使二聚体相位锁定时间提升约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该体系为非平衡周期驱动系统的研究提供了可扩展平台，并可支撑高速自旋光电子网络中的非局域相位控制。

Abstract: We demonstrate mutual synchronization of Larmor precessions between spatially separated, optically trapped polariton condensates. We identify the cross-condensate correlation g(2)1,2(τ) as a sensitive metric for condensate coupling. Synchronization of precessions in a condensate dimer is mediated by polariton outflow and resonantly depends on spectral overlap and precession winding. All-optical stirring of one site non-locally increases the pseudo-spin dephasing time of its static neighbor, prolonging phase-locking in a dimer by an order of magnitude. Our work establishes a scalable platform for studying non-equilibrium time-periodically driven systems and for high-speed spinoptronic networks.

</details>


### [149] [Exploring ground state phase diagrams of Rydberg atom arrays with finite projected entangled-pair states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26520)
*Konrad Schröder,Erik Lennart Weerda,Matteo Rizzi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用基于采样的投影纠缠对态(PEPS)优化方法，研究Rydberg原子阵列(RAA)中不同阻挫几何格点上的争议性物理问题，包括Kagomé格点上的自旋玻璃物理与Ruby格点上超越PXP模型的拓扑序，从而确立PEPS作为构建RAA实验"数字孪生"的合适工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAA被视为量子模拟奇异量子态的有前景平台，但二维setup中基态性质的数值预测困难；忠实处理长程相互作用对判别竞争相至关重要却代价高昂，因此需要能高效处理长程项的方法。

Method: 采用基于采样优化的投影纠缠对态(PEPS)，该方法已被证明能以与短程项相同的代价处理长程相互作用项；将其应用于RAA多种阻挫几何格点上争议物理问题的研究。

Result: 在Kagomé格点上研究了自旋玻璃物理的出现，在Ruby格点上研究了超越PXP模型的拓扑序，针对这些争议性问题给出数值结果。

Conclusion: PEPS是构建RAA实验数字孪生的合适工具，可用于忠实模拟二维Rydberg阵列的基态性质与竞争相。

Abstract: Rydberg atom arrays (RAA) have been advocated as a promising avenue for the quantum simulation of different exotic quantum states. Numerical predictions for the ground state properties of this experimental platform can, however, be challenging in two-dimensional setups. In particular, faithfully treating long-range interactions is often crucial for distinguishing the fate of competing phases, but it usually comes at considerable cost. Projected entangled-pair states (PEPS), optimized via a sampling-based approach, have been shown to deal with such terms on the same ground as shorter-range ones. In this work, we employ these tools to investigate debated physical questions in different frustrated geometries of RAA, from the emergence of spin-glass physics on the Kagomé lattice to topological order beyond the PXP model on the Ruby lattice. By that, we contribute to establishing PEPS as well-suited tools for creating digital twins of RAA experiments.

</details>

### [150] [Two quantum spin liquid states coexisting in the kagome antiferromagnet herbertsmithite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24065)
*Arthur C. Campello,Shengtao Jiang,Wei He,Jiajia Wen,Daniel M. Pajerowski,Steven A. Kivelson,Hong-Chen Jiang,Young S. Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨非弹性中子散射与大规模DMRG/动力学DMRG模拟的对比，为Herbertsmithite的Heisenberg哈密顿量提供定量验证，并揭示基态可能是两个近简并、有能隙量子自旋液态的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Herbertsmithite（ZnCu₃(OH)₆Cl₂）是研究最广泛的量子自旋液体候选材料之一，但其基态的精确性质至今未解决。因此需要定量基准其自旋哈密顿量并阐明基态的基本属性。

Method: 对单晶Herbertsmithite（Zn_xCu_{4-x}(OD)₆Cl₂，x≈0.85）进行高分辨非弹性中子散射测量，并与大规模密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）及动力学DMRG模拟进行直接比较。

Result: 测得的自旋关联给出的Heisenberg模型能量密度与模拟高度一致，构成哈密顿量的关键验证。DMRG模拟给出两个近简并的量子自旋液态：二者均保持平移对称性，但其中一个保留点群对称性，另一个出现各向异性自旋关联并破坏点群对称性；两者的自旋结构因子不同，且将两者混合后与中子谱高度吻合。

Conclusion: 中子散射与模拟之间建立了定量对应关系，表明Herbertsmithite处于两个近邻有能隙量子自旋液态共存的物理区间。

Abstract: Herbertsmithite (ZnCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Cl$_2$) is one of the most extensively studied quantum spin liquid (QSL) candidates, yet the precise nature of its ground state remains unresolved. Here, we compare the results of high-resolution inelastic neutron scattering on single crystals of herbertsmithite (Zn$_\mathrm{x}$Cu$_\mathrm{4-x}$(OD)$_6$Cl$_2$, $x\simeq 0.85$) with large-scale density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and dynamical DMRG simulations to benchmark the spin Hamiltonian and uncover fundamental properties of the ground state. The measured spin correlations determine an energy density for the Heisenberg model that closely matches simulations, an essential verification of the Hamiltonian. In parallel, DMRG simulations reveal two nearly degenerate QSL states. Both preserve translational symmetry, but one retains the lattice point-group symmetries whereas the other develops anisotropic spin correlations that break point-group symmetry. These two QSLs have distinct spin structure factors, and, remarkably, close agreement with the neutron spectra is achieved with an admixture of the two states. Our results establish a quantitative correspondence between neutron scattering and simulations, and suggest that herbertsmithite realizes a regime in which two proximate gapped QSL states coexist.

</details>


### [151] [Monolayer van der Waals kagome lattice for high-temperature flat-band physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24071)
*Hyungkun Bong,Choongjae Won,Seongyong Lee,Soomin Oh,Phuong X. Nguyen,Raghav Chaturvedi,Sehyun Oh,Jiyong Han,Paul Malinowski,Chad Mowers,Jimin Kim,Sae Hee Ryu,Hyunjong Lee,Jinhyeok Choi,Sangsu Yer,Chris Jozwiak,Aaron Bostwick,Eli Rotenberg,Craig M. Polley,Matthew D. Watson,Timur K. Kim,Heejun Yang,Jieun Lee,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan,Jae-Hoon Park,Gil Young Cho,Kyle M. Shen,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作首次在vdW材料家族A2T3X4中实现理想二维单层kagome晶格，通过nano-ARPES直接观测到费米能级下方孤立的kagome平带，并借助栅压调控将其移至费米能级，构建出电子密度比莫尔体系高220倍以上的本征二维平带平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome体系蕴含拓扑、关联与阻挫的丰富物理，但既有研究多局限于三维块体晶体；实现真正的二维kagome体系长期是目标，因其可解锁三维体系中无法获得的物理与调控路径。

Method: 利用新型多功能vdW kagome家族A2T3X4（A=K/Rb/Cs，T=Ni/Pd/Pt，X=S/Se），以纳米聚焦角分辨光电子能谱（nano-ARPES）将电子结构表征推进到单层极限，并结合栅压调控与多体计算。

Result: 在单层极限下直接观测到费米能级下方孤立的kagome衍生平带；通过栅压工程可将该平带调至费米能级，从而直接接触部分填充平带物理；相较莫尔人工平带体系，其平带电子密度高出220倍以上，进入全新的密度与能量区间。

Conclusion: 该vdW kagome本征平带体系为二维平带物理提供了高电子密度平台，指明了将分数量子反常霍尔等涌现平带现象推向更高温度的可实现路径，并获得多体计算支持。

Abstract: Kagome lattice materials host a remarkably rich landscape of novel quantum phenomena, all arising from the intrinsic interplay of topology, correlation, and frustration. While previous studies have been mostly limited to bulk three-dimensional (3D) kagome crystals, the realization of a true two-dimensional (2D) kagome system has remained a long-standing goal, to unlock fundamentally new physics and engineering pathways inaccessible in 3D systems. In this work, we establish the first ideal 2D monolayer kagome lattices by leveraging a novel and versatile van der Waals (vdW) kagome family, A2T3X4 (A = K, Rb, Cs; T = Ni, Pd, Pt; X = S, Se). Our nano-focused angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (nano-ARPES) directly characterizes the electronic structure of A2T3X4 down to the monolayer limit, notably revealing an isolated kagome-derived flat band just below the Fermi level. Via gate-engineering of 2D A2T3X4, the kagome flat band can be further tuned to the Fermi level, allowing direct access to the partially filled flat band physics and establishing a qualitatively new 2D flat band platform. Compared to existing moire-based artificial flat band systems, this vdW kagome-based intrinsic flat band system realizes more than 220 times higher flat band electron density, opening an entirely new density and energy regime for exploring 2D flat band physics. Harnessing this high-electron-density flat band in 2D vdW kagome system provides a clear pathway to scale emergent flat band phenomena - such as fractional quantum anomalous Hall states - to far higher temperatures than currently achievable, as long-envisioned in theory and further supported by our many-body calculations.

</details>


### [152] [Kohler's Rule Holds in the Strange Metallic Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24208)
*Rebekah M. Jin,Emily Z. Zhang,Jixun K. Ding,Brian Moritz,Thomas P. Devereaux*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用行列式量子蒙特卡罗计算空穴掺杂的Hubbard-Hofstadter模型磁阻，发现尽管存在T线性电阻率、超越Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限且无能隙准粒子，Kohler定则仍在较宽的相互作用与掺杂范围内意外成立；Kohler标度在下述交叉温度T*≈t以下出现，该温度由双占位率与比热标识，并伴随格点间局域单重态关联的开启。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多强关联"奇异金属"中被报道存在违反Kohler定则的非寻常磁输运，但其违反的确切微观起源尚不清楚；作者希望通过可控的数值模型厘清强关联与奇异金属性本身是否必然导致异常磁阻。

Method: 采用行列式量子蒙特卡罗（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）方法，计算空穴掺杂Hubbard-Hofstadter模型在不同在位相互作用强度与空穴掺杂下的磁阻，并结合双占位率与比热确定交叉温度T*，同时考察格点间局域单重态关联。

Result: 在电阻率呈T线性、超过Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限且名义上不存在良好定义准粒子的情形下，Kohler定则仍在一系列相互作用强度与掺杂范围内成立；Kohler行为在T*≈t以下涌现，该交叉温度与局域单重态关联的开启相吻合。

Conclusion: 该区域的Kohler标度反映了一种更普遍的多体量子相干形式；强关联与奇异金属性本身并不必然产生异常磁阻，因此Kohler定则的违反需要额外机制来解释。

Abstract: Unconventional magnetotransport violating Kohler's rule has been reported in many strongly correlated strange metals, but the exact origin of these violations is yet unknown. To investigate this question, we use determinant quantum Monte Carlo to compute the magnetoresistance of the hole-doped Hubbard-Hofstadter model. Despite $T$-linear resistivity exceeding the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit and the nominal absence of well-defined quasiparticles, we find Kohler's rule unexpectedly holds across a wide range of on-site interaction strengths and hole dopings. In particular, Kohler emerges below a crossover temperature $T^\star\approx t$ identified from the double occupancy and specific heat, coinciding with the onset of local singlet correlations between lattice sites. We thus propose that Kohler scaling in this regime reflects a more general form of many-body quantum coherence. Our results show that strong correlations and strange metallicity do not themselves generically produce anomalous magnetoresistance.

</details>


### [153] [Theory of High-Temperature Fractional Chern Phases in Monolayer van der Waals Kagome Materials A$_2$T$_3$X$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24633)
*Soomin Oh,Jiyong Han,Jinhyeok Choi,Donghae Seo,Mingu Kang,Gil Young Cho*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文理论预言新合成的范德华kagome家族 A₂T₃X₄ 单层是原子尺度分数陈绝缘体平台，在 Cs₂Pd₃S₄ 中给出约26 meV的多体能隙，并可通过双层反平行磁化实现分数量子自旋霍尔绝缘体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分数陈绝缘体仅在moiré材料中实现，超晶格周期长导致电子相互作用被削弱、相只存在于极低温；早期设想的原子尺度拓扑平带可望在更高温度保持分数化，但一直缺乏合适材料。

Method: 理论识别范德华kagome家族 A₂T₃X₄ 单层兼具本征拓扑平带与强库仑相互作用，并具栅极可调载流子密度与层间堆叠自由度；对由密度泛函理论导出的模型做精确对角化（允许至多一个带间电子-空穴激发以计入带混合）。

Result: 在广泛填充区间发现铁磁性，支持多种分数陈绝缘体、反常复合费米液体与电荷有序相；Cs₂Pd₃S₄ 中分数陈绝缘体多体能隙约26 meV；反平行磁化的双层在现实层间距下可实现分数量子自旋霍尔绝缘体。

Conclusion: 该工作确定了实验可及的原子尺度材料家族，化学组分、载流子密度与堆叠提供互补途径，在更高能标下实现分数化并调控拓扑序。

Abstract: Fractional Chern insulators bring the fractional quantum Hall effect, with its anyonic excitations, to zero magnetic field. So far they have been realized in moiré materials, where the long superlattice period weakens electronic interactions and confines these phases to cryogenic temperatures. Early proposals instead envisioned topological flat bands on atomic-scale lattices, where fractionalization could persist to far higher temperatures, but a suitable material has been lacking. Here we theoretically identify monolayers of the recently synthesized van der Waals kagome family $\mathrm{A}_2\mathrm{T}_3\mathrm{X}_4$ as such a platform, combining intrinsic topological flat bands and strong Coulomb interactions with gate-tunable carrier density and layer assembly. Exact diagonalization of models derived from density functional theory reveals ferromagnetism over broad filling ranges, supporting diverse fractional Chern insulators, anomalous composite Fermi liquids, and charge-ordered phases. In particular, in $\mathrm{Cs}_2\mathrm{Pd}_3\mathrm{S}_4$, the fractional Chern insulator exhibits a many-body gap of approximately 26 meV in the largest system accessible with band mixing allowing at most one interband electron-hole excitation. Layer assembly further provides a natural route to fractional quantum spin Hall insulators: bilayers with antiparallel magnetizations support this phase at realistic separations. These results identify an experimentally accessible material family in which chemical composition, carrier density, and stacking offer complementary routes to fractionalization at enhanced energy scales and control over topological order.

</details>


### [154] [Microwave probing of magnetic phases in a dipolar-exchange magnet LiGdF$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24734)
*S. S. Sosin,A. I. Smirnov,T. A. Soldatov,V. S. Edelman,O. A. Morozov,S. L. Korableva,I. V. Romanova*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 对弱各向异性偶极-交换反铁磁体LiGdF₄单晶进行低温磁共振测量，观察到T_N=0.19(1) K以下有序相形成导致的谱变化、沿四重轴磁场诱导的一系列相变，并归因于交换与偶极作用的竞争性阻挫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱各向异性偶极-交换反铁磁体LiGdF₄的磁有序温度极低且相图复杂，其微观机制（交换与偶极相互作用的竞争）尚未明确，需借助磁共振手段探测有序相及场诱导相变。

Method: 对LiGdF₄单晶样品开展低温磁共振测量，外磁场沿晶体四重轴方向施加，追踪温度低于T_N时共振谱随有序相形成的演变，并识别磁场诱导的相变序列。

Result: 在T_N=0.19(1) K以下观测到共振谱的转变；沿四重轴加场时探测到一系列场诱导相变；在某一低场磁相中及饱和场以上均获得分辨良好的磁共振谱。

Conclusion: 交换与偶极相互作用竞争（一种特殊类型的磁阻挫）可解释该体系的磁有序延迟与复杂磁相图。

Abstract: We report the results of low-temperature magnetic resonance measurements performed on a single-crystal sample of a weakly anisotropic dipolar-exchange antiferromagnet LiGdF$_4$. The transformation of a resonance spectrum due to the formation of the ordered phase is observed at temperatures below $T_N=0.19(1)$ K. A sequence of field-induced phase transitions is detected for the external magnetic field applied along the fourfold axis of the crystal. A well-resolved magnetic resonance spectrum is observed in one of the low-field magnetic phases as well as above the saturation field. Competing exchange and dipolar interactions, as a special type of magnetic frustration, are suggested to account for the delayed ordering and complex magnetic phase diagram of the system.

</details>


### [155] [Interaction-driven competing instabilities in two-dimensional checkerboard quadratic band crossing semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25046)
*Jia-Hao Wen,Wen-Hao Bian,Jing Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 系统考虑全部16种边缘短程费米子相互作用，用动量壳RG研究棋盘格QBCP半金属的低能物理，发现多类不动点及NSN、QAH、手征SC、QSH等主导不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维二次能带交叉点(QBCP)半金属是相互作用驱动量子临界的重要平台，但完整费米子-费米子相互作用的作用此前基本未被系统考察，已有工作多采用简化模型。

Method: 对自旋1/2棋盘格QBCP半金属，纳入全部16种边缘短程费米子-费米子相互作用，采用动量壳重整化群推导耦合流方程，分析不动点结构；再通过引入源项并计算磁化率确定主导与次主导不稳定性。

Result: 低能物理由一系列不动点支配：依初始条件可出现单个稳定不动点、若干离散不动点，或由少数变量参数化的三组连续不动点。主导相为向列自旋向列(NSN)、量子反常霍尔(QAH)、手征超导(SC1)和量子自旋霍尔(QSH)序。

Conclusion: 非对角相互作用产生简化模型中缺失的序；连续不动点群可系统调节主导不稳定性；并出现依赖轨迹的不稳定性，即由完整RG流而非仅不动点决定主导态。结果为二维QBCP半金属及相关电子系统的低能量子临界行为与相不稳定性提供了完整理论图像。

Abstract: Two-dimensional quadratic band crossing point (QBCP) semimetals serve as a fertile platform for interaction-driven quantum criticality, nevertheless, the role of the complete fermion-fermion interactions remains largely unexplored. To address this, we systematically investigate the low-energy physics of a spin-1/2 checkerboard-lattice QBCP semimetal by considering all 16 marginally short-range fermion-fermion interactions. Using the momentum-shell renormalization group, we derive the coupled flow equations, which reveal that the low-energy physics is governed by a series of fixed points. Depending on the initial conditions, the system may exhibit a single stable fixed point, several discrete fixed points, or three continuous groups of fixed points parameterized by a few variables. By introducing source terms and evaluating susceptibilities, we identify the dominant and subdominant instabilities. The leading phases include nematic spin-nematic (NSN), quantum anomalous Hall (QAH), chiral superconducting ($\mathrm{SC}_1$), and quantum spin Hall (QSH) orders. Moreover, several features distinguish our findings from previous simplified models. Off-diagonal interactions generate terms that promote orders absent in such models, the continuous groups allow systematic tuning of the leading instability, and trajectory-dependent instabilities emerge where the full RG flow, rather than the fixed point alone, determines the dominant state. These results offer a comprehensive theoretical understanding of low-energy quantum critical behaviors and phase instabilities in two-dimensional QBCP semimetals and related electron systems.

</details>


### [156] [High-order resonances and emergent coherent dynamics in interacting Stark chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26328)
*Jiahe Chang,Hongzheng Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在相互作用Stark链中揭示了一个系统性的高阶相互作用—倾斜共振层级，共振阶数等于最小基本交换步数，表现为时间尺度依赖阶数的长寿命相干振荡，并可用微观有效描述将动力学时间尺度与多体交换过程及初态结构联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性势梯度（倾斜）能强烈抑制量子动力学，而相互作用可激活共振多体过程；但这些高阶共振结构此前基本未被探索，缺乏对共振层级及其动力学时间尺度的系统理解。

Method: 在相互作用Stark链中定义以最小基本交换步数计数的共振阶数，通过隔离单个共振通道并构建微观有效描述（有效哈密顿量/微扰展开），分析各阶共振对应的相干振荡时间尺度及其对初态结构的依赖。

Result: 发现系统性的高阶相互作用—倾斜共振层级，各阶共振对应长寿命相干振荡且时间尺度随阶数变化；动力学时间尺度可与具体多体交换过程对应，并显著依赖初态结构；当相互作用与倾斜之比为公度比时可产生参数化慢的相干多体动力学层级。

Conclusion: 共度相互作用—倾斜比生成参数化慢的相干多体动力学层级，为高阶多体共振提供了关键认识，并给出连接动力学与多体交换过程的微观框架。

Abstract: A linear potential gradient can strongly suppress quantum dynamics, while interactions can activate resonant many-body processes whose higher-order structure remains largely unexplored. Here we uncover a systematic hierarchy of high-order interaction--tilt resonances in an interacting Stark chain, where the resonance order counts the minimal number of elementary exchange steps involved. These resonances manifest as long-lived coherent oscillations with order-dependent timescales. By isolating individual resonant channels and developing a microscopic effective description, we connect these dynamical timescales to the underlying many-body exchange processes and reveal a pronounced dependence on the initial-state structure. Our results provide crucial insights into high-order many-body resonances and show how commensurate interaction-to-tilt ratios generate a hierarchy of parametrically slow coherent many-body dynamics.

</details>


### [157] [Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions and deconfined criticality in SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25081)
*Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 将DM相互作用加入SU(2)规范理论后证明：时间反演与自旋轨道锁定的滑移对称性禁止任意阶的规范单态自旋子质量项，材料仍是解禁闭临界性的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SrCu2(BO3)2是解禁闭临界性的首要候选材料，但已有的临界区理论忽略了Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用，而DM会为kagome Dirac自旋液体打开能隙，因此需考察DM对临界性的影响。

Method: 将DM相互作用引入SU(2)规范理论，分析对称性（时间反演、自旋轨道锁定的滑移）对规范单态自旋子质量项的限制，并分别讨论面内与面外DM分量的作用及SO(5)各向异性的阶数。

Result: 时间反演与滑移对称性在所有阶上禁止规范单态自旋子质量；面内DM仅"扭曲"自旋子，在0.03–0.17 K以下重构节点；面外DM为无关算符；SO(5)各向异性仅在二阶出现。

Conclusion: SrCu2(BO3)2仍可作为解禁闭临界性的平台；NMR可检验该预言——[100]方向磁场诱导Néel矩而[001]方向磁场不诱导。

Abstract: SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ is the leading candidate for deconfined criticality, yet theories of its critical regime omit the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interactions, which gap the kagome Dirac spin liquid. Adding them to the SU(2) gauge theory, we show that time reversal and a spin-orbit-locked glide forbid any gauge-singlet spinon mass to all orders. In-plane DM only twists the spinons, reconstructing the nodes below $0.03$-$0.17$ K, while out-of-plane DM is irrelevant and SO(5) anisotropy arises only at second order. The material thus remains a platform for deconfined criticality, which NMR can test, since a $[100]$ field induces a Néel moment and a $[001]$ field does not.

</details>


### [158] [Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions in the SU(2) gauge theory of the Shastry-Sutherland Dirac spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25082)
*Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在自旋-轨道锁定的空间群下对Shastry-Sutherland Dirac自旋液体的SU(2)规范理论中费米子双线性作分类，并在1/N领头阶和单圈水平处理SrCu₂(BO₃)₂的DM相互作用：线性阶DM既不能使spinon产生能隙也不能在T*以上破坏SO(5)点，对称性禁戒一切局域规范单态费米子质量；T*以下Dirac锥重构为手征保护的d波二次能带接触，单圈流在T**处落在含价键序的射线上或无能隙手征Z₂自旋液体上，DM对两种plaquette模式的偏置至多O(10⁻⁴)且方向在O(1/N)未定，非磁淬火无序仅对plaquette双重态施加相关随机场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SrCu₂(BO₃)₂是Shastry-Sutherland晶格的代表材料，其低能物理是否可由Dirac自旋液体描述、以及Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用与无序对候选自旋液体态的影响尚不清楚。作者希望厘清DM在自旋-轨道锁定空间群下对Dirac自旋液体SU(2)规范理论的微扰效应与对称性约束，从而判定该材料的低能相图与可观测判据。

Method: 在自旋-轨道锁定空间群下对SU(2)规范理论的费米子双线性作系统分类；在1/N领头阶与单圈水平处理DM相互作用；分析规范单态费米子质量的对称性禁戒；计算面内与面外耦合诱导的自旋流算符及其标度维数；在T*以下对重构的d波二次能带接触进行单圈重整化群流分析，并考察规范场被能带接触屏蔽、DM偏置与非磁淬火无序的效应。

Result: 线性阶DM无法使spinon产生能隙，也无法在T*以上使SO(5)点失稳；对称性在任何阶都禁戒局域规范单态费米子质量。单态双线性中面内耦合仅诱导维数恰为2的守恒自旋流，给出T*=4λ≃0.03–0.17 K；面外耦合仅给出在幺正临界点无关的带味自旋-2流。DM诱导的Yukawa耦合低于限制赝临界窗口者之2%。零场下实测样品在1 K以上、即T*处或以上有序。T*以下二维样品在临界压力下Dirac锥重构为手征保护的d波二次能带接触，其唯一规范单态能隙通道为两个价键分量与标量手征性。含能带接触屏蔽的单圈流在T**~(0.03–0.1)λ终止：对该材料的交换及多数随机耦合落在含价键序的射线上，否则落在有能隙的手征Z₂自旋液体上。两种plaquette模式间的DM偏置至多~10⁻⁴，方向在O(1/N)未定；非磁淬火无序对plaquette双重态施加相关随机场，对手征性则无。沿[100]的场诱导线性于场的(π,π)奈尔矩，沿[001]的场则无。

Conclusion: DM与对称性分析表明SrCu₂(BO₃)₂的低能物理可容纳Dirac自旋液体图像，且赝临界窗口与价键/手征Z₂基态由T*、T**标定；场取向的选择定则（[100]诱导(π,π)奈尔矩而[001]不诱导）在二聚体相中同样成立，可作为压力下¹¹B NMR的可检验判据。

Abstract: We classify the fermion bilinears of the SU(2) gauge theory of the Shastry-Sutherland Dirac spin liquids under the spin-orbit-locked space group and treat the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interactions of SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ at leading order in $1/N$ and at one loop. At linear order DM can neither gap the spinons nor destabilize the SO(5) point above a scale $T^*$. Symmetry forbids every local gauge-singlet fermion mass at any order. Among the singlet bilinears, the in-plane couplings induce only a conserved spin current of dimension exactly 2, which sets $T^*=4λ\simeq0.03$-$0.17$ K, and the out-of-plane coupling only a flavored spin-2 current, irrelevant at a unitary critical point. DM-induced Yukawa couplings start below $2\%$ of the one that bounds the pseudocritical window. In zero field, measured samples order above $1$ K, at or above $T^*$. Below $T^*$, in a two-dimensional sample at the critical pressure, the Dirac cones reconstruct into a chirally protected $d$-wave quadratic band touching whose only gauge-singlet gap channels are the two valence-bond components and the scalar chirality. A one-loop flow, with the gauge field screened by the band touching, ends at $T^{**}\sim(0.03$-$0.1)λ$ on a ray containing valence-bond order for the material's exchange and for most random couplings, and otherwise on a gapped chiral $\mathbb{Z}_2$ spin liquid. The DM bias between the two plaquette patterns is at most of order $10^{-4}$, with a direction not fixed at $O(1/N)$, whereas non-magnetic quenched disorder couples a relevant random field to the plaquette doublet and none to the chirality. A field along $[100]$ induces a $(π,π)$ Néel moment linear in the field; a field along $[001]$ does not. This selection rule also holds in the dimer phase and is testable by $^{11}$B NMR under pressure.

</details>


### [159] [Large exchange bias from coupled antiferromagnetic order and glassy magnetic freezing in $Co_{0.36}NbS_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25102)
*Suman Mondal,Himadri Roy Dakua,Raz Luger,Muntaser Naamneh,Eran Maniv*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 单晶Co0.36NbS2在TN≈26 K附近呈现反铁磁序，低温下发生部分磁矩的玻璃态冻结，并在场冷后出现可观的交换偏置（1.8 K、70 kOe场冷下|HEB|≈4.4 kOe）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非化学计量磁性NbS2插层化合物中的交换偏置机制，检验"反铁磁基体—玻璃态未补偿磁矩耦合"这一图像能否成为获得交换偏置的更普适途径。

Method: 对单晶Co0.36NbS2开展磁化测量：变温磁化、场冷后的M(H)回线、训练效应测试，以及场扫速与冷却场协议的依赖实验。

Result: TN≈26 K处出现反铁磁序；低温磁响应具历史依赖性，符合玻璃态冻结。场冷后M(H)回线显示明显交换偏置，1.8 K、70 kOe场冷下|HEB|≈4.4 kOe；偏置在阻塞温度TB≈12 K（远低于TN）附近消失，并伴随训练效应及场扫速、冷却场协议依赖性。

Conclusion: 结果支持"冻结的未补偿磁矩与反铁磁基体交换耦合"的解释，并提示反铁磁—玻璃态磁矩耦合可能是非化学计量磁性NbS2插层物中获得交换偏置的更普适路径。

Abstract: Magnetization measurements on single-crystalline $Co_{0.36}NbS_{2}$ reveal antiferromagnetic order near $T_{N} \simeq 26 K$ and a lower-temperature history-dependent magnetic response consistent with glassy freezing of a subset of magnetic moments. After field cooling, $M(H)$ loops exhibit a sizable exchange-bias shift, reaching $|H_{EB}| \simeq 4.4 kOe$ at $1.8 K$ for a $70 kOe$ field-cooling sweep protocol. The training effect, field-sweep and cooling-field protocol dependence, and disappearance of the bias near a blocking temperature $T_{B} \simeq 12 K$, well below $T_{N}$, support an interpretation in which frozen uncompensated moments are exchange-coupled to an antiferromagnetic matrix. These results suggest that antiferromagnetic-glassy-moment coupling may provide a broader route to exchange bias in off-stoichiometric magnetic $NbS_{2}$ intercalates.

</details>


### [160] [Frustration-Assisted Electrical Switching in $Co_xTaS_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25112)
*Matan Sterenberg,Bar Segura,Muntaser Naamneh,Eran Maniv*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在近化学计量比CoₓTaS₂中，磁阻挫与玻璃态动力学使电流脉冲可在远低于Hall活性相的阈值下写入非易失各向异性电阻态，开关行为分为两个温度依赖的动力学区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反铁磁电学开关被理解为电流诱导力矩作用于刚性序参量，但在阻挫磁体中竞争态可形成历史依赖的能量景观，慢集体动力学可能主动参与电学重构，这一机制尚缺乏实验验证。

Method: 选取处于Hall活性与Hall非活性多Q磁区成分敏感边界附近的近化学计量比CoₓTaS₂（范德华反铁磁体，T_N≈31.5 K，费米能级附近保留van Hove奇点），在微结构器件中施加电流脉冲测量各向异性电阻，并结合磁化老化与场后弛豫测量表征慢磁动力学。

Result: 晶体无可分辨的自发Hall响应；电流脉冲写入了非易失各向异性电阻态，激活电流密度比Hall活性的低插层相低近一个数量级；开关响应随温度分为两个动力学区间而非单一电流阈值；磁化老化与弛豫测量显示缓慢、历史依赖的磁动力学，且弛豫强度与开关幅值近似成反比。

Conclusion: 磁阻挫与玻璃态动力学是CoₓTaS₂电学重构的核心要素，通过"动力学软"的反铁磁能量景观为功能化电学开关提供了新途径。

Abstract: Electrical switching in antiferromagnets is commonly described as the action of current-induced torques on a rigid order parameter. In frustrated magnets, however, competing states can create a history-dependent energy landscape in which slow collective dynamics actively participate in electrical reconfiguration. Here, we demonstrate frustration-assisted switching in near-stoichiometric $Co_xTaS_2$, a van der Waals antiferromagnet located at the composition-sensitive boundary between Hall-active and Hall-inactive multi-Q-related magnetic regimes. Although the crystals exhibit no resolved spontaneous Hall response, they retain van Hove singularities near the Fermi level and order antiferromagnetically below $T_N \simeq 31.5 K$. Current pulses write nonvolatile anisotropic resistance states in microstructured devices, with an activation current density nearly an order of magnitude below that reported for the Hall-active lower-intercalation phase. The switching response separates into two temperature-dependent dynamical regimes rather than following a single current threshold. Independent magnetization-aging and post-field-relaxation measurements reveal slow, history-dependent magnetic dynamics, and the relaxation strength closely follows the inverse switching amplitude. These results identify magnetic frustration and glassy dynamics as central ingredients in the electrical reconfiguration of $Co_xTaS_2$, establishing a route to functional switching through a dynamically soft antiferromagnetic landscape.

</details>


### [161] [Categorical classification of non-Fermi liquids in 2+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25713)
*Shubham Kukreja,Dawson M. Willerton,Sung-Sik Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用配对通道四点函数与尺度依赖的普适配对相互作用，对2+1维非费米液体给出非微扰分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非费米液体的定义性特征（费米面上无准粒子峰）以及两、三点顶点的性质在众多非费米液体中定性相同，导致只能以"否定式"定义来区分，缺乏正面刻画其差异的判据。

Method: 指出配对通道的四点函数蕴含可正面区分不同非费米液体的丰富信息；引入可刻画尺度依赖普适配对相互作用的广义不动点概念，在大费米动量、固定无量纲耦合极限下，结合运动学约束进行非微扰分析。

Result: 与长波临界涨落耦合的金属被划分为七个"超普适类"（superuniversality classes），每一类在温度—裸四费米子耦合参数空间中呈现独特的相图。

Conclusion: 本工作借助运动学约束，将此前基于微扰论的分类推广至强相互作用的非费米液体，实现了对其的非微扰、正面分类。

Abstract: A defining feature of non-Fermi liquids is a lack of the quasiparticle peak on the Fermi surface in the spectral function, which is usually realized by coupling a finite density of fermions with critical collective fluctuations. However, these features of the two- and three-point vertex functions are qualitatively the same across many non-Fermi liquids, which has made it difficult to classify them beyond the definition by negation. We point out that the four-point function in the pairing channel contains rich information that positively differentiates distinct types of non-Fermi liquids. Using the generalized notion of fixed points that captures scale-dependent universal pairing interaction, we provide a non-perturbative classification of 2+1-dimensional non-Fermi liquids. In the large Fermi momentum limit with fixed dimensionless couplings, metals coupled with long-wavelength critical fluctuations are classified into seven superuniversality classes. Each of them exhibits a unique phase diagram in the space of temperature and bare four-fermion coupling. The present work generalizes the earlier perturbative classification to strongly interacting non-Fermi liquids by leveraging kinematic constraints.

</details>


### [162] [Ferroelastic domain wall driven glassy dynamics and photoresponse in a SrTiO$_3$ (111) two-dimensional electron gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25943)
*Jyotirmay Maity,Manav Beniwal,Pranab Hazra,Sumit Tomar,Srimanta Middey*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在AlOx/STO(111)界面二维电子气中，发现低载流子密度样品的输运呈现玻璃态电子动力学，其非单调温度依赖的弛豫行为源自STO极性铁弹畴壁网络，且该耦合可由载流子密度调控、并主导持续光电导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STO(111)基异质结是二维电子气的重要平台，但STO自身极性铁弹畴壁网络对电荷输运的影响此前基本未被研究，作者希望厘清畴壁态是否构成一个独立的电子自由度。

Method: 制备并对比两种载流子密度不同的AlOx/STO(111)界面二维电子气，开展低温输运测量（热滞回、电阻弛豫、降温速率依赖、记忆效应）与光电导测量，并将弛豫特征的温度演化与体材料STO铁弹畴壁态的已知演化进行对照。

Result: 低密度样品表现出热滞回、缓慢电阻弛豫、降温速率依赖和记忆效应，指示玻璃态电子动力学；弛豫随温度强烈非单调——约80 K以下增强、25 K附近达峰、约12 K以下消失，与STO铁弹畴壁态演化一致而非任何电子能标；高载流子密度的金属性界面中上述特征消失；持续光电导在约80 K以下急剧上升，与畴壁极性出现温度吻合。

Conclusion: 极性铁弹畴壁是STO(111)基二维电子气中一个载流子密度可调的电子自由度，可通过拓扑缺陷调控二维电子气性质，为畴壁工程化器件提供途径。

Abstract: The (111) orientation of SrTiO$_3$ (STO)-based heterostructures has emerged as a versatile platform for two-dimensional electron gases (2DEGs), yet the influence of STO's polar ferroelastic domain-wall (DW) network on charge transport remains largely unexplored. Here we study AlO$_x$/STO (111) interfaces hosting 2DEGs of different carrier densities. In the lower-density 2DEG, low-temperature transport exhibits thermal hysteresis, slow resistance relaxation, cooling-rate dependence, and memory effects, signaling glasslike electron dynamics. The relaxation is strongly non-monotonic in temperature, increasing below $\sim$80 K, peaking near 25 K, and vanishing below $\sim$12 K, tracking the established evolution of the ferroelastic DW state of bulk STO rather than any electronic energy scale, which identifies the DW network as its origin. These signatures are absent in a second, more metallic interface with much higher carrier density, demonstrating that the electron-DW coupling can be tuned. We further show that polar DWs govern the photoresponse: the persistent photoconductivity rises sharply below $\sim$80 K, coinciding with the onset of DW polarity. Together, these results establish polar ferroelastic DWs as a carrier-density-tunable electronic degree of freedom in STO (111)-based 2DEGs, opening routes to manipulate 2DEG properties through topological defects.

</details>


### [163] [Methods of density functional theory in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26012)
*Thomas E. Baker,Kasey Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文讨论一维Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论的高效数值计算策略、精确性质（含时间依赖）的确定方法，并发布了一个公开的一维DFT计算库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论仅由存在性证明在形式上得到保证，因此借助计算方法研究该理论成为理解完整理论的有效途径，作者据此发展一维Kohn-Sham数值方法。

Method: 综述一维密度泛函实现所用的表达式；给出一维Kohn-Sham系统数值求解策略；提出梯度型技术的替代性论证，可应用于精确Kohn-Sham势的时间演化；并配套公开库实现。

Result: 获得一维Kohn-Sham计算的高效数值方案与精确性质（含含时情形）确定方法，并有可公开获取的库支撑一维DFT计算。

Conclusion: 数值方法是理解DFT理论的重要途径；一维情形为精确性质与含时Kohn-Sham势的梯度型算法提供了可行且可复现的计算框架。

Abstract: Density functional theory is formally justified by an existence proof. As such, the need for computational methods to study the theory is a useful route to understanding the full theory. In this paper, we discuss the numerical strategies to efficiently evaluate computations for Kohn-Sham systems in one dimension and also methods for determining exact properties, including time-dependence. An accompanying library is also available for computing one-dimensional density functional theory and publicly available. The expressions used to implement the density functional in one-dimension are surveyed. An alternative justification for a gradient-based technique is also shown that can be applied to time-dependent evolution of the exact Kohn-Sham potential.

</details>


### [164] [Evidence of valence bond solid and quantum paramagnetism in the $J_1$--$J_2$--$J_3$ Heisenberg model from pseudofermion functional renormalization group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26135)
*Gün Günal,Erhai Zhao,Ahmet Keleş*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用改进频率分辨率的pf-fRG系统计算方格$J_1$–$J_2$–$J_3$海森堡模型零温相图，并通过二聚体响应的流行为判定量子顺磁窗口分裂为VBS区与QSL区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期张量网络研究报道量子顺磁（QP）区中存在量子自旋液体（QSL），需在更大参数空间内独立验证并厘清QP区内部是否隐含键序。

Method: 采用赝费米子泛函重整化群（pf-fRG），提升频率分辨率并增大格点截断，扫描更广的$J_1$–$J_2$–$J_3$参数空间；通过施加破坏点群对称性的$J_1$键各向异性微扰，测量二聚体响应，分析二聚体磁化率在RG流中的演化。

Result: 二聚体磁化率的RG流呈现两类行为：一类持续增长至最低能标，指示价键固体（VBS）序；另一类在有限能标达峰后被抑制，指示QSL。据此QP窗口分裂为两个区域。

Conclusion: pf-fRG可区分QP区内的VBS序与QSL，支持QP区存在QSL子区的结论，并为张量网络结果提供独立数值佐证。

Abstract: Motivated by recent tensor network studies reporting a quantum spin liquid (QSL) within the quantum paramagnetic (QP) regime, we investigate numerically the zero-temperature phase diagram of the $J_1$--$J_2$--$J_3$ Heisenberg model on the square lattice using the pseudofermion functional renormalization group (pf-fRG). Thanks to improved frequency resolution and larger lattice truncations, we are able to explore a significantly larger parameter space than previous pf-fRG work to map out the phase boundary of an extended QP region. We further resolve possible bond order within the QP regime by measuring the dimer response to small deformations in $J_1$, i.e. bond anisotropy that breaks the point-group symmetry of the Hamiltonian. The functional renormalization group flow of the dimer susceptibility suggests that the QP window splits into two: a region where it grows continuously down to the lowest energy scale to signal valence bond solid (VBS) order, and another region where it reaches a peak at a finite scale and is subsequently suppressed to indicate a QSL.

</details>


### [165] [Variational Block Correlated Coupled Cluster Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26646)
*Hunter Brodie,Alexander Shilcusky,Adrian E. Feiguin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种适用于量子蒙特卡罗模拟的变分耦合簇实现，通过Young表表示波函数系数及其导数以降低变分优化的计算复杂度，并在一维和二维Heisenberg模型上以含双、三重激发的块关联耦合簇（BCCC）拟设验证其竞争性精度与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受量子神经态近期成功的启发，希望将变分耦合簇方法适配到量子蒙特卡罗框架中，但变分优化中波函数系数及其导数的高计算复杂度是主要障碍。

Method: 引入Young表（Young tableaux）表示波函数系数及其导数，显著降低变分优化的计算复杂度；算法具有通用性，具体以块关联耦合簇拟设（BCCC，含双激发与三激发）在一维和二维Heisenberg模型上实现；并采用基于虚构温度的重要性采样改进收敛。

Result: 基于虚构温度的重要性采样显著改善收敛；在一维和二维Heisenberg模型上取得具有竞争力的精度，同时提供可扩展的变分耦合簇计算框架。

Conclusion: Young表表示结合虚构温度重要性采样，使变分耦合簇方法可用于量子蒙特卡罗模拟并获得竞争性精度，为大规模变分耦合簇计算提供了可扩展途径。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent success of quantum neural states, we present a variational implementation of the coupled cluster method suitable for quantum Monte Carlo simulations. We introduce a Young tableaux representation of the wave-function coefficients and their derivatives that substantially reduces the computational complexity of the variational optimization. While the algorithm is generic, we illustrate its implementation with an application to the Heisenberg model in one and two dimensions, using the block correlated coupled cluster ansatz (BCCC) with doubles and triples. Convergence is greatly improved by means of importance sampling based on a fictitious temperature. The resulting approach achieves competitive accuracy while providing a scalable framework for variational coupled-cluster calculations.

</details>


### [166] [Kondo Lattice model for field induced triplet pairing in CeRh$_2$As$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26858)
*Indra Gankhuyag,Piers Coleman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文为CeRh2As2建立了Kondo晶格模型，证明Ce位局域反演对称破缺导致的d-f轨道局域杂化产生交变反对称自旋轨道耦合(ASOC)，从而给出近简并的偶/奇宇称超导态及场致宇称转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋三重态超导体因拓扑性质与高磁场鲁棒性而受关注。CeRh2As2是唯一同时具备偶宇称相与候选奇宇称相、并可经场致相变在两者间切换的重费米子超导体，该宇称切换由交变ASOC驱动；但其超导的重费米子起源及ASOC的微观来源此前未被研究。

Method: 构建面向CeRh2As2的Kondo晶格模型，引入由Ce位局域反演对称破缺所驱动的Ce d轨道与f轨道之间的局域杂化，并分析其对ASOC生成的贡献。

Result: 局域d-f杂化在Ce位产生ASOC；所得Kondo晶格模型给出近简并的偶宇称与奇宇称超导态，并再现偶宇称态与奇宇称赝自旋三重态之间的场致宇称转变。

Conclusion: ASOC源于局域d-f杂化与局域反演对称破缺，该机制可推广至CeSiI等其他重费米子体系，具有更广泛意义。

Abstract: Spin triplet superconductors are of interest because of their topological properties, and their robustness to high magnetic fields. CeRh$_2$As$_2$ is a unique heavy fermion superconductor with both even-parity and candidate odd-parity superconducting phases with a special feature enabling a field induced phase transition between the two. The field induced transition which switches the parity of the Cooper pairs is enabled by a staggered antisymmetric spin orbit coupling (ASOC) interaction. However, the heavy fermion origins of the superconductivity in CeRh$_2$As$_2$ and the origin of the antisymmetric spin-orbit coupling are currently unexplored. Here, we develop a Kondo lattice model for CeRh$_2$As$_2$ and demonstrate how local hybridization between Cerium $d$- and $f$-orbitals, driven by the locally broken inversion symmetry, at the Cerium sites generates the ASOC. The resulting Kondo lattice model leads to nearly degenerate even and odd parity superconducting states that display a field induced parity transition between an even-parity state and an odd-parity pseudospin triplet state. We discuss the wider implications of our results extended to other heavy fermion systems like CeSiI.

</details>


### [167] [On Higher-Form Fracton Hydrodynamics with Momentum Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26862)
*Ben Bobell,Rahul Nandkishore,Andrew Lucas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用MSR有效场论建立平移不变系统流体动力学的表示论框架，给出奇异守恒律（如fracton、高形式对称性）与动量守恒相容的两个表示论一致性条件及广义高斯定律约束，并证明高形式对称性是唯一可行类（在SO(3)及足够大奇数维SO(d)下）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量守恒在流体中普遍存在，但对具有奇异守恒律（如fracton流体动力学）的理论，除非自发破缺对称性，否则可能与其流体动力学不相容；作者希望在不发生自发破缺的前提下系统刻画哪些奇异守恒律可与动量守恒共存。

Method: 采用Martin-Siggia-Rose（MSR）有效场论形式，构建平移不变系统的表示论框架；在关于流体动力学形式的温和假设下，推导表示论一致性条件与广义高斯定律约束，并以高形式对称性作为检验与构造案例。

Result: 必须满足两个表示论一致性条件和一个广义高斯定律约束；高形式对称性满足这些条件，据此导出其理想流体动力学，三维中一形式情形给出理想非相对论磁流体力学；并证明在SO(3)及足够大的奇数维SO(d)中，除此之外无其他理论与动量守恒相容。

Conclusion: 动量守恒对奇异守恒律施加强烈的表示论限制：高形式对称性（含一形式磁流体力学）是主要可行类，fracton等理论若要保持动量守恒则需自发破缺对称性；该框架为判定流体动力学相容性提供了系统判据。

Abstract: Momentum conservation is common in fluids, yet it may be incompatible with the hydrodynamics of theories with exotic conservation laws (such as fracton hydrodynamics) unless symmetries are spontaneously broken. Using the Martin-Siggia-Rose (MSR) effective field theory formalism, we develop a representation-theoretic framework for the hydrodynamics of translationally invariant systems with exotic conservation laws without spontaneous symmetry breaking. Under mild assumptions on the form of hydrodynamics, we show that such theories must satisfy two representation theoretic consistency conditions and a generalized Gauss's law constraint. Higher-form symmetries satisfy these conditions and we derive their ideal hydrodynamics; in three dimensions, the one-form case produces ideal non-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics. Finally, we prove that no other theories are compatible with momentum conservation in SO(3) nor SO($d$), for sufficiently large odd $d$.

</details>
